  function craftedTargetCap(profile){
    // Beim Schutzpaladin existieren für die zusätzlichen Schmiedekunst-Slots sehr gute aktuelle M+-Alternativen.
    // Deshalb bleiben hier bewusst nur die zwei langfristig sinnvollen Craft-Ziele (Handgelenke + Füße) erhalten.

  function enforceCraftedTargetLimit(profile){
    if(!profile?.gear)return 0;
    const cap=craftedTargetCap(profile);
    let crafted=profile.gear.filter(x=>x?.sourceType==='Hergestellt');
    if(crafted.length<=cap)return 0;
    const bundled=BUNDLED_BIS_GEAR.get(profile.key)||[];
    const fallbackBySlot=new Map(bundled.filter(x=>x?.sourceType==='Mythic+').map(x=>[x.slot,x]));
    // Zuerst überschüssige Crafts zurück auf das geprüfte Dungeon-Ziel des integrierten Snapshots setzen.
    const replaceable=crafted.filter(x=>fallbackBySlot.has(x.slot));
    let changed=0;
    for(const item of replaceable){
      if(crafted.length<=cap)break;
      const fallback=fallbackBySlot.get(item.slot);if(!fallback)continue;
      const idx=profile.gear.indexOf(item);if(idx<0)continue;
      profile.gear[idx]=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(fallback));changed++;
      crafted=profile.gear.filter(x=>x?.sourceType==='Hergestellt');
    }
    return changed;
  }

  function isGenericKeystoneSource(v){return /keystoneloot|aktueller saison-2-dungeon|dungeon noch nicht im lokalen quellenkatalog|beruf abhängig vom gegenstand|beruf aus gegenstandsdaten|^herstellung(?:\s*[·:-]|$)/i.test(String(v||''))}
  function knownAcquisition(entry,currentItem=null,craftedHint=false){
    const id=Number(entry?.itemId??currentItem?.itemId)||0;
    const name=entry?.name||currentItem?.name||'';
    const verified=VERIFIED_DUNGEON_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
    if(verified)return {...verified};
    const seasonalDungeon=KEYSTONELOOT_DUNGEON_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
    if(seasonalDungeon)return {...seasonalDungeon};
    const seasonalRaid=KEYSTONELOOT_RAID_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
    if(seasonalRaid)return {...seasonalRaid};
    const verifiedCraft=VERIFIED_CRAFT_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
    if(verifiedCraft)return{sourceType:verifiedCraft.sourceType,source:verifiedCraft.source};
    if(INVALID_RAIDFREE_SOURCE_IDS.has(id))return{sourceType:'Nicht zulässig',source:'Nicht raidfrei · fehlerhafter Import (Raid/andere Nicht-M+-Quelle)',invalidForRaidfree:true};
    const known=ITEM_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id)||ITEM_SOURCE_BY_NAME.get(normal(name));
    if(known&&known.sourceType!=='Matrixkatalysator')return {...known};
    if(currentItem&&!isGenericKeystoneSource(currentItem.source)&&currentItem.source){
      return{sourceType:currentItem.sourceType||(craftedHint?'Hergestellt':'Mythic+'),source:currentItem.source};
    }
    if(craftedHint)return{sourceType:'Hergestellt',source:'Herstellung · Beruf aus Gegenstandsdaten'};
    return{sourceType:'Mythic+',source:'Dungeon noch nicht im lokalen Quellenkatalog'};
  }
  function repairAcquisitionSources(profile){
    for(const item of profile.gear||[]){
      const id=Number(item.itemId)||0;
      const verified=VERIFIED_DUNGEON_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
      if(verified){item.sourceType=verified.sourceType;item.source=verified.source;delete item.invalidForRaidfree;continue;}
      const seasonalDungeon=KEYSTONELOOT_DUNGEON_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
      if(seasonalDungeon){item.sourceType=seasonalDungeon.sourceType;item.source=seasonalDungeon.source;delete item.invalidForRaidfree;continue;}
      const seasonalRaid=KEYSTONELOOT_RAID_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
      if(seasonalRaid){item.sourceType=seasonalRaid.sourceType;item.source=seasonalRaid.source;delete item.invalidForRaidfree;continue;}
      const verifiedCraft=VERIFIED_CRAFT_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id);
      if(verifiedCraft){item.sourceType=verifiedCraft.sourceType;item.source=verifiedCraft.source;delete item.invalidForRaidfree;continue;}
      if(INVALID_RAIDFREE_SOURCE_IDS.has(id)){item.sourceType='Nicht zulässig';item.source='Nicht raidfrei · fehlerhafter Import (Raid/andere Nicht-M+-Quelle)';item.invalidForRaidfree=true;continue;}
      if(!isGenericKeystoneSource(item.source))continue;
      const crafted=item.sourceType==='Hergestellt';
      const known=ITEM_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(id)||ITEM_SOURCE_BY_NAME.get(normal(item.name));
      if(known&&known.sourceType!=='Matrixkatalysator'){item.sourceType=known.sourceType;item.source=known.source;item.invalidForRaidfree=Boolean(known.invalidForRaidfree)}
      else if(crafted){item.source='Herstellung · Beruf aus Gegenstandsdaten'}
      else item.source='Dungeon noch nicht im lokalen Quellenkatalog';
    }
  }
  for(const p of root.profiles||[])repairAcquisitionSources(p);

  function deutsch(v){const m={warrior:'Krieger',arms:'Waffen',fury:'Furor',protection:'Schutz',paladin:'Paladin',holy:'Heilig',retribution:'Vergeltung',hunter:'Jäger','beast mastery':'Tierherrschaft',marksmanship:'Treffsicherheit',survival:'Überleben',rogue:'Schurke',assassination:'Meucheln',outlaw:'Gesetzlosigkeit',subtlety:'Täuschung',priest:'Priester',discipline:'Disziplin',shadow:'Schatten','death knight':'Todesritter',deathknight:'Todesritter',blood:'Blut',unholy:'Unheilig',shaman:'Schamane',elemental:'Elementar',enhancement:'Verstärkung',restoration:'Wiederherstellung',mage:'Magier',arcane:'Arkan',fire:'Feuer',frost:'Frost',warlock:'Hexenmeister',affliction:'Gebrechen',demonology:'Dämonologie',destruction:'Zerstörung',monk:'Mönch',brewmaster:'Braumeister',mistweaver:'Nebelwirker',windwalker:'Windläufer',druid:'Druide',balance:'Gleichgewicht',feral:'Wildheit',guardian:'Wächter','demon hunter':'Dämonenjäger',demonhunter:'Dämonenjäger',havoc:'Verwüstung',vengeance:'Rachsucht',devourer:'Verschlinger',evoker:'Rufer',devastation:'Verheerung',preservation:'Bewahrung',augmentation:'Verstärkung'};return m[normal(v)]||v||'Unbekannt'}
  function wowheadUrl(item){return item?.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item?.itemId||''}`}

  function profileMatchesLoadedCharacterBase(p){
    if(!liveCharacter)return false;
    const classOk=(p.supportedClasses||[]).some(x=>normal(x)===normal(liveCharacter.class));
    const specOk=(p.supportedSpecs||[]).some(x=>normal(x)===normal(liveCharacter.active_spec_name));
    return classOk&&specOk;
  }
  function profilesForLoadedCharacter(){
    return liveCharacter?root.profiles.filter(profileMatchesLoadedCharacterBase):[];
  }
  const HERO_TALENT_ALIASES={
    'bergthan':['bergthan','mountain thane'],
    'diaboliker':['diaboliker','diabolist'],
    'hollenrufer':['höllenrufer','hollenrufer','hellcaller'],
    'koloss':['koloss','colossus'],
    'schlachter':['schlächter','schlachter','slayer']
  };
  function heroTalentAliases(name){
    const key=normal(name).replace(/\s+/g,' ');
    const compact=key.replace(/[^a-z0-9äöüß]+/g,'');
    for(const [aliasKey,values] of Object.entries(HERO_TALENT_ALIASES)){
      if(normal(aliasKey).replace(/[^a-z0-9äöüß]+/g,'')===compact)return values.map(normal);
      if(values.some(v=>normal(v)===key))return values.map(normal);
    }
    return[key];
  }
  function currentRioHeroTalent(){
    if(!liveCharacter)return'';
    const candidates=profilesForLoadedCharacter().filter(p=>p.heroTalent);
    if(!candidates.length)return'';
    const parts=[];
    const visit=(v,key='')=>{
      if(v==null)return;
      if(typeof v==='string'||typeof v==='number'||typeof v==='boolean'){parts.push(String(key),String(v));return}
      if(Array.isArray(v)){for(const x of v)visit(x,key);return}
      if(typeof v==='object'){for(const [k,x] of Object.entries(v))visit(x,k)}
    };
    for(const source of [liveCharacter.talentLoadout,liveCharacter.talents,liveCharacter.talents_categorized,liveCharacter.talent_loadout,liveCharacter.loadout])visit(source);
    const hay=normal(parts.join(' '));
    const explicit=[
      liveCharacter.talentLoadout?.active_hero_tree?.name,
      liveCharacter.talentLoadout?.active_hero_tree?.slug,
      liveCharacter.hero_talent,liveCharacter.heroTalent,liveCharacter.hero_talent_name,
      liveCharacter.talents?.hero_talent,liveCharacter.talents?.heroTalent,
      liveCharacter.talents_categorized?.hero_talent,liveCharacter.talents_categorized?.heroTalent
    ].map(normal).filter(Boolean);
    const exact=candidates.find(p=>heroTalentAliases(p.heroTalent).some(a=>explicit.includes(a)));
    if(exact)return exact.heroTalent;
    const matched=candidates.find(p=>heroTalentAliases(p.heroTalent).some(a=>hay.includes(a)));
    return matched?.heroTalent||'';
  }
  function refreshProfileSelectOptions(){
    const select=$('profileSelect');if(!select)return;
    const profiles=profilesForLoadedCharacter();
    if(!liveCharacter){
      select.innerHTML='<option value="">Bitte zuerst Charakter laden</option>';
      select.disabled=true;
      return;
    }
    if(!profiles.length){
      select.innerHTML='<option value="">Kein BiS-Profil für diese Spezialisierung verfügbar</option>';
      select.disabled=true;
      return;
    }
    select.disabled=false;
    select.innerHTML=profiles.map(p=>`<option value="${p.key}">${escapeHtml(p.heroTalent||p.label)}</option>`).join('');
    if(profiles.some(p=>p.key===activeProfile.key))select.value=activeProfile.key;
  }
  function initProfileSelect(){refreshProfileSelectOptions()}
  function heroTalentSiblingProfiles(){
    const seen=new Set();
    return profilesForLoadedCharacter().filter(p=>p.heroTalent).filter(p=>{const k=normal(p.heroTalent);if(!k||seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true});
  }
  function renderHeroTalentSwitcher(){
    const panel=$('heroBuildSwitcherPanel'),buttons=$('heroBuildButtons'),current=$('heroBuildCurrent'),hint=$('heroBuildHint');
    if(!panel||!buttons)return;
    const siblings=heroTalentSiblingProfiles();
    if(siblings.length<2){panel.classList.add('hidden');buttons.innerHTML='';return}
    panel.classList.remove('hidden');
    if(current)current.textContent=activeProfile.heroTalent||activeProfile.label;
    if(hint)hint.textContent=`${activeProfile.class} ${activeProfile.spec}: ${siblings.length} Hero-Talent-BiS-Profile hinterlegt. Ein Klick wechselt sofort die Zielausrüstung und den persönlichen BiS-Abgleich – ideal, um einen neuen Drop auch für das andere Talent zu prüfen.`;
    buttons.innerHTML=siblings.map(p=>{
      const t=p.statModel?.targets||{};
      const targets=[['crit','Krit'],['haste','Tempo'],['mastery','Meisterschaft'],['versatility','Versa']].filter(([k])=>Number.isFinite(Number(t[k]))).map(([k,l])=>`${l} ${t[k]}%`).join(' · ');
      return `<button type="button" class="hero-build-btn ${p.key===activeProfile.key?'active':''}" data-hero-profile="${escapeHtml(p.key)}"><strong>${escapeHtml(p.heroTalent||p.label)}</strong><small>${escapeHtml(targets||p.subtitle||'BiS-Profil')}</small></button>`;
    }).join('');
    buttons.querySelectorAll('[data-hero-profile]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>{
      const key=btn.dataset.heroProfile;if(!key||key===activeProfile.key)return;
      setProfile(key,false);
    }));
  }

Bitte BiS-Update ausführen, sobald eine aktuelle raidfreie Liste verfügbar ist.':'');

  function setProfile(key,automatic=false){const p=root.profiles.find(x=>x.key===key);if(!p)return;activeProfile=p;if(typeof bisListView!=='undefined')bisListView='mythicplus';loadStoredBackgroundRaidBis(activeProfile);comparisonCache=new Map();if(!automatic)profilePlanningLocked=true;if(liveCharacter)rebuildPersonalCraftOverrides();updateProfile();if(liveCharacter){renderCharacter();ensureBackgroundRaidBis(activeProfile,false).then(()=>{comparisonCache=new Map();renderCharacter();renderComparison();renderMissing()})}if(!automatic){const s=getSettings();s.profileKey=key;s.planningProfileLocked=true;saveSettingsObject(s)}}
  function profileClassMatches(p=activeProfile){if(!liveCharacter)return false;return (p.supportedClasses||[]).some(x=>normal(x)===normal(liveCharacter.class))}
  function profileMatches(p=activeProfile){return profileMatchesLoadedCharacterBase(p)}
  function planningProfileActive(p=activeProfile){return Boolean(liveCharacter&&profileClassMatches(p)&&!profileMatches(p))}
  function findProfile(){
    const candidates=profilesForLoadedCharacter();
    if(!candidates.length)return null;
    const hero=currentRioHeroTalent();
    if(hero){
      const matched=candidates.find(p=>p.heroTalent&&normal(p.heroTalent)===normal(hero));
      if(matched)return matched;
    }
    return candidates[0]||null;
  }

  function getItems(){return liveCharacter?.gear?.items||null}
  function getEquipped(slot){const items=getItems();if(!items)return null;const aliases=(slotAliases[slot]||[slot]).map(normal);if(Array.isArray(items))return items.find(x=>aliases.includes(normal(x?.slot||x?.slot_type||x?.type)))||null;for(const [k,v] of Object.entries(items))if(aliases.includes(normal(k)))return v;for(const v of Object.values(items))if(v&&aliases.includes(normal(v.slot||v.slot_type)))return v;return null}
  function eqBonuses(eq){
    const pools=[eq?.bonuses,eq?.bonus_ids,eq?.bonusIds,eq?.item?.bonuses,eq?.item?.bonus_ids,eq?.item?.bonusIds,eq?.item?.context?.bonus_ids,eq?.context?.bonus_ids];
    const out=[];
    for(const a of pools){if(Array.isArray(a))for(const v of a){const n=Number(v);if(Number.isFinite(n)&&!out.includes(n))out.push(n)}}
    return out;
  }
  function craftingMeta(eq){
    if(!eq)return null;
    const id=eqId(eq),known=CRAFTED_ITEM_CATALOG.get(id);
    const fields=[
      eq?.crafted_stats,eq?.crafting_stats,eq?.crafting_quality,eq?.craft_quality,eq?.quality_tier,
      eq?.crafted_by,eq?.crafter,eq?.is_crafted,eq?.recraft,eq?.recrafted,
      eq?.item?.crafted_stats,eq?.item?.crafting_stats,eq?.item?.crafting_quality,eq?.item?.crafted_by,eq?.item?.is_crafted,
      eq?.crafting,eq?.item?.crafting
    ];
    const hasField=fields.some(v=>v===true||(typeof v==='number'&&Number.isFinite(v))||(typeof v==='string'&&v.trim())||(v&&typeof v==='object'&&Object.keys(v).length));
    // 8960 ist der von aktuellen hergestellten Ausrüstungsteilen verwendete Crafting-Bonus.
    const hasCraftBonus=eqBonuses(eq).includes(8960);
    if(!(known||hasField||hasCraftBonus))return null;
    return {known:Boolean(known),profession:known?.profession||'Herstellung',reason:known?'item-id':hasCraftBonus?'bonus-8960':'crafting-meta'};
  }
  function isCraftedEquipped(eq){return Boolean(craftingMeta(eq))}
  function canReplaceCatalystSlot(item){if(!(item?.catalyst||item?.setSlot))return true;const setSlots=activeProfile.gear.filter(x=>x.catalyst||x.setSlot);if(setSlots.length<5)return false;const setNames=new Set(setSlots.map(x=>x.slot));const already=[...personalCraftOverrides.keys()].filter(s=>setNames.has(s)&&s!==item.slot).length;return setSlots.length-(already+1)>=4}
  function rebuildPersonalCraftOverrides(){
    personalCraftOverrides=new Map();
    if(!liveCharacter||!profileClassMatches())return;
    for(const item of activeProfile.gear){
      const eq=getEquipped(item.slot),meta=craftingMeta(eq);
      if(!eq||!meta||isBase(item,eq)||isTier(item,eq))continue;
      if(!canReplaceCatalystSlot(item))continue;
      const profession=meta.profession&&meta.profession!=='Herstellung'?` · ${meta.profession}`:'';
      personalCraftOverrides.set(item.slot,{itemId:eqId(eq),name:eqName(eq),itemLevel:eqIlvl(eq),wowheadUrl:`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${eqId(eq)}`,sourceType:'Hergestellt',source:`Persönlich hergestellt${profession}`,slot:item.slot,craftReason:meta.reason});
    }
  }
  function personalCraft(item,eq=null){const p=personalCraftOverrides.get(item.slot);if(!p)return null;if(eq&&eqId(eq)!==Number(p.itemId))return null;return p}
  function isBase(item,eq){if(!eq)return false;const id=eqId(eq);if(item.itemId&&id===Number(item.itemId))return true;const n=normal(eqName(eq));return [item.name,item.nameEn].some(x=>normal(x)===n)}
  function isTier(item,eq){if(!eq||!item.catalyst)return false;const id=eqId(eq);if((item.acceptItemIds||[]).map(Number).includes(id))return true;const n=normal(eqName(eq));return (item.acceptNames||[]).some(x=>normal(x)===n)}
  const BACKGROUND_RAID_STORE='wow-bis-background-raid:';
  const BACKGROUND_RAID_MAX_AGE=6*24*60*60*1000;
  function backgroundRaidStoreKey(profile=activeProfile){return BACKGROUND_RAID_STORE+(profile?.key||'unknown')}
  function normalizeBackgroundRaidEntry(x){if(!x||!Number(x.itemId)||!x.slot)return null;return{slot:x.slot,itemId:Number(x.itemId),name:x.name||`Gegenstand #${x.itemId}`,sourceType:'Raid',source:x.source||'KeystoneLoot Raid-BiS · Hintergrund',wowheadUrl:x.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${Number(x.itemId)}`}}
  function setBackgroundRaidTargets(profile,list,meta={}){
    if(!profile)return[];const clean=(Array.isArray(list)?list:[]).map(normalizeBackgroundRaidEntry).filter(Boolean);backgroundRaidCache.set(profile.key,clean);profile.backgroundRaidBis=clean;backgroundRaidMeta.set(profile.key,{fetchedAt:meta.fetchedAt||profile.backgroundRaidBisUpdatedAt||root.meta.dataDate||'',source:meta.source||'Hintergrund-Snapshot'});return clean
  }
  function loadStoredBackgroundRaidBis(profile=activeProfile){
    if(!profile)return[];if(backgroundRaidCache.has(profile.key))return backgroundRaidCache.get(profile.key)||[];
    try{
      const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(backgroundRaidStoreKey(profile))||'null');
      const versionOk=!profile.backgroundRaidBisVersion||saved?.snapshotVersion===profile.backgroundRaidBisVersion;
      if(saved?.patch===root.meta.patch&&versionOk&&Array.isArray(saved.items)&&saved.items.length)return setBackgroundRaidTargets(profile,saved.items,{fetchedAt:saved.fetchedAt,source:saved.source});
      if(saved&&!versionOk)try{localStorage.removeItem(backgroundRaidStoreKey(profile))}catch{}
    }catch{}
    return setBackgroundRaidTargets(profile,profile.backgroundRaidBis||[],{fetchedAt:profile.backgroundRaidBisUpdatedAt||root.meta.dataDate,source:'Integrierter Raid-BiS-Snapshot'});
  }
  function saveBackgroundRaidBis(profile,list,payload={}){
    const clean=setBackgroundRaidTargets(profile,list,{fetchedAt:payload.fetchedAt||new Date().toISOString(),source:payload.source||'KeystoneLoot Raid-BiS'});
    try{localStorage.setItem(backgroundRaidStoreKey(profile),JSON.stringify({patch:root.meta.patch,snapshotVersion:profile?.backgroundRaidBisVersion||'',items:clean,fetchedAt:payload.fetchedAt||new Date().toISOString(),source:payload.source||'KeystoneLoot Raid-BiS',sourceUrl:payload.sourceUrl||''}))}catch{}return clean
  }
  function restoreBackgroundRaidBis(){for(const profile of root.profiles)loadStoredBackgroundRaidBis(profile)}
  function backgroundRaidTargets(profile=activeProfile){return loadStoredBackgroundRaidBis(profile)}
  function sameSlotGroup(a,b){if(a===b)return true;const rings=new Set(['finger1','finger2']),trinkets=new Set(['trinket1','trinket2']);return(rings.has(a)&&rings.has(b))||(trinkets.has(a)&&trinkets.has(b))}
  function backgroundRaidBisMatch(item,eq){if(!eq)return null;const id=eqId(eq),name=normal(eqName(eq));return backgroundRaidTargets(activeProfile).find(r=>sameSlotGroup(item?.slot,r?.slot)&&(Number(r?.itemId)===id||(r?.name&&normal(r.name)===name)))||null}
  function evaluate(item,eq){if(!liveCharacter||!profileClassMatches())return{key:'unknown',label:'Nicht geprüft'};if(item?.invalidForRaidfree)return{key:'missing',label:'Kein raidfreies M+-BiS hinterlegt'};if(!eq)return{key:'missing',label:'Nicht ermittelt'};if(personalCraft(item,eq))return{key:'personal',label:'Persönliches BiS (hergestellt)'};if(isTier(item,eq))return{key:'match',label:'BiS / Set angelegt'};if(item.catalyst&&isBase(item,eq))return{key:'catalyst',label:'Katalysator offen'};if(isBase(item,eq))return{key:'match',label:'BiS angelegt'};if(backgroundRaidBisMatch(item,eq))return{key:'raidbis',label:'BiS angelegt · Raid-BiS'};if(isCraftedEquipped(eq)&&(item.catalyst||item.setSlot)&&!canReplaceCatalystSlot(item))return{key:'alternative',label:'Hergestellt · Setplatz geschützt'};return{key:'alternative',label:'Anderer Gegenstand'}}
  function isFulfilledBisStatus(key){return key==='match'||key==='personal'||key==='raidbis'}
  function buildComparisons(){const result=new Map();const grouped=new Set(['finger1','finger2','trinket1','trinket2']);for(const item of activeProfile.gear.filter(x=>!grouped.has(x.slot))){const eq=getEquipped(item.slot);result.set(item.slot,{item,eq,status:evaluate(item,eq)})}for(const slots of [['finger1','finger2'],['trinket1','trinket2']]){const targets=slots.map(s=>activeProfile.gear.find(x=>x.slot===s)).filter(Boolean);const equipped=slots.map(s=>getEquipped(s)).filter(Boolean);const used=new Set();for(const item of targets){let idx=equipped.findIndex((eq,i)=>!used.has(i)&&(personalCraft(item,eq)||isBase(item,eq)||isTier(item,eq)||backgroundRaidBisMatch(item,eq)));if(idx<0)idx=equipped.findIndex((eq,i)=>!used.has(i));const eq=idx>=0?equipped[idx]:null;if(idx>=0)used.add(idx);result.set(item.slot,{item,eq,status:evaluate(item,eq)})}}return result}

  function targetHtml(item){
    const pc=personalCraft(item);
    if(pc)return `<a class="item-name" href="${pc.wowheadUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="medium">${escapeHtml(pc.name)}</a><span class="personal-target-note">★ Persönliches BiS · hergestellt</span><span class="general-target-note">Allgemeines Ziel: ${escapeHtml(item.name)}</span>`;
    if(item.catalyst&&item.tierItemId){
      const base=catalystBaseFromItem(item);
      const tierUrl=item.tierWowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${Number(item.tierItemId)||0}`;
      if(base){
        return `<span class="catalyst-farm-target"><strong>BiS-Basisitem zum Umwandeln:</strong></span><a class="item-name" href="${base.wowheadUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="medium">${escapeHtml(base.name)}</a><span class="base-source">Quelle: ${escapeHtml(base.source)}</span><span class="tier-note">♻ Matrixkatalysator → <a href="${tierUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="small">${escapeHtml(item.tierName||item.name)}</a></span><span class="set-target-note">◆ Das umgewandelte Setteil zählt für den 4er-Bonus.</span>`;
      }
      return `<span class="catalyst-farm-target"><strong>BiS-Basisitem zum Umwandeln:</strong> noch nicht ermittelt</span><span class="tier-note">♻ Matrixkatalysator → <a href="${tierUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="small">${escapeHtml(item.tierName||item.name)}</a></span>`;
    }
    return `<a class="item-name" href="${wowheadUrl(item)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="medium">${escapeHtml(item.name)}</a>`
  }
  function currentHtml(eq){if(!eq)return'<span class="muted">Nicht ermittelt</span>';const icon=rioIcon(eq);return `<div style="display:flex;gap:8px;align-items:center">${icon?`<img src="${icon}" alt="" style="width:38px;height:38px;border-radius:5px">`:''}<div><a class="current-name" href="https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${eqId(eq)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escapeHtml(eqName(eq))}</a><div class="small">Gegenstandsstufe ${escapeHtml(eqIlvl(eq))}</div></div></div>`}
  function catalystBaseFromItem(item){
    if(!item?.catalyst)return null;
    let id=Number(item.catalystBaseItemId)||0;
    let name=item.catalystBaseName||'';
    let sourceType=item.catalystBaseSourceType||'Mythic+';
    let source=item.catalystBaseSource||'';
    if(!id&&Number(item.itemId)&&Number(item.itemId)!==Number(item.tierItemId)){
      id=Number(item.itemId);name=item.name||`Gegenstand #${id}`;sourceType=item.sourceType||'Mythic+';source=item.source||'';
    }
    if(!id||id===Number(item.tierItemId))return null;
    if(!name)name=`Gegenstand #${id}`;
    if(!source||isGenericKeystoneSource(source)){
      const acq=knownAcquisition({itemId:id,name},item,false);
      if(!acq.invalidForRaidfree){sourceType=acq.sourceType||'Mythic+';source=acq.source||source;}
    }
    return{itemId:id,name,wowheadUrl:item.catalystBaseWowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`,sourceType:sourceType||'Mythic+',source:source||'Dungeon noch nicht im lokalen Quellenkatalog'};
  }
  function setCatalystBaseFromEntry(item,entry){
    if(!item?.catalyst||!entry)return false;
    const id=Number(entry.itemId)||0,tierId=Number(item.tierItemId)||0;
    if(!id||id===tierId)return false;
    const name=updateItemName(entry,item);
    const acq=knownAcquisition(entry,item,false);
    if(acq.invalidForRaidfree||acq.sourceType==='Hergestellt'||acq.sourceType==='Nicht zulässig')return false;
    item.catalystBaseItemId=id;item.catalystBaseName=name;item.catalystBaseNameEn=name;
    item.catalystBaseWowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`;
    item.catalystBaseSourceType=acq.sourceType||'Mythic+';item.catalystBaseSource=acq.source||'Dungeon noch nicht im lokalen Quellenkatalog';
    item.catalystBaseUpdatedAt=new Date().toISOString();
    return true;
  }
  function ensureCatalystBaseMetadata(profile){
    for(const item of profile?.gear||[]){
      if(!item.catalyst)continue;
      if(Number(item.catalystBaseItemId)&&Number(item.catalystBaseItemId)!==Number(item.tierItemId))continue;
      if(Number(item.itemId)&&Number(item.itemId)!==Number(item.tierItemId)&&item.sourceType!=='Hergestellt'){
        item.catalystBaseItemId=Number(item.itemId);item.catalystBaseName=item.name||`Gegenstand #${item.itemId}`;item.catalystBaseNameEn=item.nameEn||item.catalystBaseName;
        item.catalystBaseWowheadUrl=item.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.itemId}`;item.catalystBaseSourceType=item.sourceType||'Mythic+';item.catalystBaseSource=item.source||'';
      }
    }
  }
  function catalystBaseInlineHtml(item){
    const base=catalystBaseFromItem(item);
    if(!base)return `<span class="catalyst-base-item pending">♻ <strong>Bestes M+-Basisitem:</strong> noch nicht im lokalen Profil gespeichert. „BiS-Items aktualisieren / importieren“ übernimmt es automatisch aus der KeystoneLoot-Katalysatorliste.</span>`;
    return `<span class="catalyst-base-item">♻ <strong>Bestes M+-Basisitem:</strong> <a href="${base.wowheadUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="small">${escapeHtml(base.name)}</a><span class="base-source">Quelle: ${escapeHtml(base.source)}</span></span>`;
  }
  function displaySourceForItem(item){
    const pc=personalCraft(item);if(pc)return pc.source||'Persönlich hergestellt';
    const base=item?.catalyst?catalystBaseFromItem(item):null;if(base)return base.source;
    return isGenericKeystoneSource(item?.source)?knownAcquisition(item,item,item?.sourceType==='Hergestellt').source:item?.source;
  }
  function renderCatalystBases(){
    const body=$('catalystBasisBody'),info=$('catalystBasisInfo'),summary=$('catalystBasisSummary');if(!body)return;
    ensureCatalystBaseMetadata(activeProfile);
    const items=(activeProfile.gear||[]).filter(x=>x.catalyst&&x.tierItemId&&['head','shoulder','chest','hands','legs'].includes(x.slot));
    const resolved=items.filter(x=>catalystBaseFromItem(x)).length;
    if(summary)summary.textContent=`${resolved}/${items.length} beste Basisitems konkret hinterlegt`;
    if(info){info.className='api-message '+(resolved===items.length&&items.length?'ok':'warning');info.textContent=resolved===items.length&&items.length?`✓ Für ${activeProfile.label} sind alle ${items.length} Katalysator-Basisitems samt Dungeonquelle hinterlegt.`:`${resolved} von ${items.length} Basisitems sind konkret hinterlegt. Fehlende Plätze werden beim nächsten KeystoneLoot-BiS-Update bzw. Import automatisch aus den Katalysator-Einträgen ergänzt.`;}
    if(!items.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="5" class="muted">Für dieses Profil sind keine Set-Slots hinterlegt.</td></tr>';return}
    body.innerHTML=items.map(item=>{
      const base=catalystBaseFromItem(item),tierUrl=item.tierWowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.tierItemId}`;
      const tier=`<a class="item-name" href="${tierUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="small">${escapeHtml(item.tierName||item.name)}</a><span class="catalyst-priority ${item.tierRequired===false?'optional':''}">${item.tierRequired===false?'5. Setplatz / optional':'Für 4er-Setplan'}</span>`;
      const baseHtml=base?`<a class="item-name" href="${base.wowheadUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="small">${escapeHtml(base.name)}</a>`:'<span class="muted">Noch nicht aus KeystoneLoot übernommen</span>';
      const source=base?`<span class="source-badge mplus">Dungeon</span><div class="small">${escapeHtml(base.source)}</div>`:'<span class="small">BiS-Update / KeystoneLoot-Import ausführen</span>';
      return `<tr><td><span class="slot-badge">${escapeHtml(item.slotLabel)}</span></td><td>${baseHtml}</td><td>${source}</td><td>${tier}</td><td class="catalyst-arrow">♻ Umwandeln</td></tr>`;
    }).join('');
    setTimeout(refreshWowhead,30);
  }

  function visibleTargetGear(){
    const seen=new Set();
    return activeProfile.gear.filter(item=>{
      if(item.invalidForRaidfree||item.hiddenRaidTarget)return false;
      const key=String(item.slot||'').toLowerCase();
      if(key&&seen.has(key))return false;
      if(key)seen.add(key);
      return true;
    });
  }

  // Web-BiS-Ansichten: Gesamt, Mythic+ und Raid sind drei getrennte KeystoneLoot-Listen.
  // Jede Ansicht wird unabhängig geladen und lokal zwischengespeichert. Die kuratierte Profil-Liste
  // bleibt nur noch als Ausfallsicherung für Mythic+ erhalten.
  let bisListView='mythicplus';
  const bisListViewCache=new Map();
  const BIS_VIEW_STORE='wow-bis-view-cache-v7:';
  function bisViewCacheKey(view,profile=activeProfile){return `${profile?.key||'unknown'}:${view}`}
  function bisViewStoreKey(view,profile=activeProfile){return BIS_VIEW_STORE+bisViewCacheKey(view,profile)}
  function bisViewLabel(view=bisListView){return view==='overall'?'Gesamt':view==='raid'?'Raid':'M+'}
  function bisViewSource(view=bisListView){return view==='overall'?'KeystoneLoot Gesamt-BiS':view==='raid'?'KeystoneLoot Raid-BiS':'KeystoneLoot Mythic+-BiS'}
  function readStoredBisView(view,profile=activeProfile){
    const key=bisViewCacheKey(view,profile);
    if(bisListViewCache.has(key))return bisListViewCache.get(key);
    try{
      const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(bisViewStoreKey(view,profile))||'null');
      if(saved?.patch===root.meta.patch&&Array.isArray(saved.gear)&&saved.gear.length){
        bisListViewCache.set(key,saved.gear);
        return saved.gear;
      }
    }catch{}
    return null;
  }
  function storeBisView(view,gear,payload={},profile=activeProfile){
    const key=bisViewCacheKey(view,profile);
    bisListViewCache.set(key,gear);
    try{
      localStorage.setItem(bisViewStoreKey(view,profile),JSON.stringify({
        patch:root.meta.patch,
        gear,
        fetchedAt:payload?.fetchedAt||new Date().toISOString(),
        source:payload?.source||bisViewSource(view),
        sourceUrl:payload?.sourceUrl||''
      }));
    }catch{}
    return gear;
  }
  function equippedCandidatesForSlot(slot){
    if(['finger1','finger2'].includes(slot))return [getEquipped('finger1'),getEquipped('finger2')].filter(Boolean);
    if(['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(slot))return [getEquipped('trinket1'),getEquipped('trinket2')].filter(Boolean);
    return [getEquipped(slot)].filter(Boolean);
  }
  function allEquippedItems(){
    const items=getItems();if(!items)return[];
    if(Array.isArray(items))return items.filter(Boolean);
    const out=[];for(const v of Object.values(items||{}))if(v&&typeof v==='object')out.push(v);
    return out;
  }
  function viewItemMatchesEquipped(item,eq){
    if(!item||!eq)return false;
    const id=Number(item.itemId)||0,n=normal(item.name||'');
    return Boolean((id&&eqId(eq)===id)||(n&&normal(eqName(eq))===n));
  }
  function viewTargetStatus(item){
    if(!liveCharacter||!profileClassMatches())return{key:'unknown',label:'Nicht geprüft'};
    // KeystoneLoot und Raider.IO können austauschbare Plätze unterschiedlich nummerieren
    // (Ring 1/2, Schmuck 1/2, Main-/Offhand). Zuerst deshalb die echte Item-ID in der
    // gesamten angelegten Ausrüstung suchen; die Slotprüfung bleibt als zusätzlicher Fallback.
    const all=allEquippedItems();
    if(all.some(eq=>viewItemMatchesEquipped(item,eq)))return{key:'match',label:'BiS angelegt'};
    const eqs=equippedCandidatesForSlot(item.slot);if(!eqs.length)return{key:'missing',label:'Nicht ermittelt'};
    if(eqs.some(eq=>viewItemMatchesEquipped(item,eq)))return{key:'match',label:'BiS angelegt'};
    return{key:'alternative',label:'Anderer Gegenstand'};
  }
  function viewGearFromPayload(profile,payload,view){
    const entries=(payload?.items||[]).filter(x=>x&&Number(x.itemId));
    const rawTemplate=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(profile?.gear||[]));
    const template=[];const seenTemplateSlots=new Set();
    for(const item of rawTemplate){
      const key=String(item?.slot||'').toLowerCase();
      if(key&&seenTemplateSlots.has(key))continue;
      if(key)seenTemplateSlots.add(key);
      template.push(item);
    }
    if(!template.length)return[];
    const explicit=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&x.slot&&BIS_IMPORT_ORDER.includes(x.slot));
    const explicitSlots=new Set(explicit.map(x=>x.slot));let regular=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&!x.slot);
    const slots=template.map(x=>x.slot).filter(slot=>!explicitSlots.has(slot));
    if(profile?.bisUpdate?.duplicateWeaponForOffhand&&template.some(x=>x.slot==='offhand')&&regular.length===slots.length-1&&regular.length)regular=[...regular,{...regular[regular.length-1]}];
    const bySlot=new Map(slots.map((slot,i)=>[slot,regular[i]]).filter(x=>x[1]));for(const e of explicit)bySlot.set(e.slot,e);
    return template.map(base=>{
      const e=bySlot.get(base.slot);if(!e)return null;const id=Number(e.itemId)||0;if(!id)return null;
      const crafted=isCraftEntry(e);
      const acq=knownAcquisition(e,null,crafted);
      let sourceType=acq.sourceType,source=acq.source;
      if(view==='raid'&&sourceType!=='Raid'){sourceType='Raid';source='Raid-Drop · konkreter Boss noch nicht im lokalen Quellenkatalog';}
      if(view==='overall'&&!sourceType)sourceType=crafted?'Hergestellt':'Gesamt';
      const note=view==='raid'?'BiS-Empfehlung: KeystoneLoot · tatsächliche Herkunft steht in der Spalte Quelle.':view==='mythicplus'?'BiS-Empfehlung: KeystoneLoot · tatsächlicher Dungeon/Beruf steht in der Spalte Quelle.':'BiS-Empfehlung: KeystoneLoot · tatsächliche Herkunft steht in der Spalte Quelle.';
      return {...base,itemId:id,name:updateItemName(e,base),nameEn:updateItemName(e,base),wowheadUrl:`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`,sourceType,source,catalyst:false,directTierTarget:false,setSlot:false,invalidForRaidfree:false,embellishment:crafted?(base.embellishment||'—'):'—',note};
    }).filter(Boolean)
  }
  async function fetchMurlokMeta(profile=activeProfile){
    try{
      const direct=String(profile?.murlokTargetSource||'').trim();
      const src=(profile?.sources||[]).find(x=>/murlok/i.test(String(x?.label||''))&&x?.url);
      const page=direct||String(src?.url||'').trim();
      if(!page)throw new Error('Keine Murlok-M+-Quelle für dieses Profil hinterlegt');
      const r=await fetch('https://r.jina.ai/'+page,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`Reader HTTP ${r.status}`);
      const text=await r.text(),low=text.toLowerCase(),a=low.indexOf('## best-in-slot gear');
      if(a<0)throw new Error('Murlok-BiS-Bereich nicht gefunden');
      const b=low.indexOf('\n## embellishments',a),section=text.slice(a,b>=0?b:text.length);
      const sm=text.match(/Based on\s+(\d+)\s+Characters/i),sampleSize=sm?Number(sm[1]):0;
      const slots={head:'head',neck:'neck',shoulders:'shoulder',shoulder:'shoulder',back:'back',chest:'chest',wrist:'wrist',wrists:'wrist',hands:'hands',waist:'waist',legs:'legs',feet:'feet',rings:'finger1',ring:'finger1',trinkets:'trinket1',trinket:'trinket1','main hand':'mainhand','off hand':'offhand',weapon:'mainhand'};
      const items=[];let slot='';
      for(const raw of section.split(/\r?\n/)){
        const line=raw.trim();
        if(line.startsWith('### ')){slot=slots[line.slice(4).trim().toLowerCase()]||'';continue}
        if(!slot||!line.toLowerCase().includes('wowhead.com'))continue;
        const clean=line.replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g,'');
        const lm=clean.match(/\[([^\]]+?)\]\((https?:\/\/[^)]*wowhead\.com\/[^)]*)\)/i);if(!lm)continue;
        const im=lm[2].match(/(?:item=|\/item\/)(\d+)/i);if(!im)continue;
        const parts=lm[1].replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim().split('*').map(x=>x.trim()).filter(Boolean);
        let usageCount=0;if(parts.length&&/^\d+$/.test(parts[parts.length-1]))usageCount=Number(parts.pop());
        const name=parts.filter(x=>!/^(set|craft)$/i.test(x)).join(' ').trim()||`Gegenstand #${im[1]}`;
        items.push({slot,itemId:Number(im[1]),name,usageCount,sampleSize,usagePct:usageCount&&sampleSize?Math.round(usageCount/sampleSize*1000)/10:null,wowheadUrl:lm[2]});
      }
      if(!items.length)throw new Error('Keine Murlok-BiS-Gegenstände erkannt');
      return{ok:true,items,sampleSize,source:'Murlok.io · Top-Spieler-Meta',sourceUrl:page,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
    }catch(e){
      console.warn('Murlok-Meta konnte nicht geladen werden:',e);
      return null;
    }
  }
  function murlokMetaLabel(item){
    const p=Number(item?.usagePct),n=Number(item?.usageCount),sample=Number(item?.sampleSize);
    if(Number.isFinite(p)&&p>=40)return 'Top-Spieler-Meta';
    if(Number.isFinite(p)&&p>=15)return 'Starke Meta-Alternative';
    if(n>=2)return 'Top-Spieler-Alternative';
    return 'Murlok-Meta';
  }
  function mergeMurlokMetaGear(gear,meta,view,profile=activeProfile){
    if(!Array.isArray(meta?.items)||!meta.items.length)return gear;
    const out=[...(gear||[])],seen=new Set(out.map(x=>Number(x.itemId)).filter(Boolean));
    const perSlot=new Map();
    for(const entry of meta.items){
      const id=Number(entry?.itemId)||0;if(!id)continue;
      const acq=knownAcquisition(entry,null,false);
      if(view==='raid'&&acq.sourceType!=='Raid')continue;
      const group=['finger1','finger2'].includes(entry.slot)?'finger':['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(entry.slot)?'trinket':entry.slot;
      const arr=perSlot.get(group)||[];
      arr.push({...entry,acq});
      perSlot.set(group,arr);
    }
    for(const arr of perSlot.values()){
      arr.sort((a,b)=>(Number(b.usageCount)||0)-(Number(a.usageCount)||0));
      for(const entry of arr.slice(0,3)){
        const id=Number(entry.itemId)||0,label=murlokMetaLabel(entry);
        const usage=Number(entry.usageCount)>0&&Number(entry.sampleSize)>0?`${entry.usageCount}/${entry.sampleSize} Top-Spieler · ${Number(entry.usagePct).toLocaleString('de-DE',{maximumFractionDigits:1})} %`:'von Murlok-Top-Spielern getragen';
        const existing=out.find(x=>Number(x.itemId)===id);
        if(existing){
          existing.metaLabel=label;existing.metaUsage=usage;
          existing.note=`${existing.note||''}${existing.note?' · ':''}${label}: ${usage}.`;
          continue;
        }
        if(seen.has(id))continue;
        const wantedSlot=entry.slot;
        let base=(profile.gear||[]).find(x=>sameSlotGroup(x.slot,wantedSlot))||(profile.gear||[]).find(x=>x.slot===wantedSlot)||{};
        const slot=['finger1','finger2'].includes(wantedSlot)?'finger1':['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(wantedSlot)?'trinket1':wantedSlot;
        const slotLabel=['finger1','finger2'].includes(wantedSlot)?'Ringe':['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(wantedSlot)?'Schmuck':(base.slotLabel||wantedSlot);
        out.push({...base,slot,slotLabel,itemId:id,name:entry.name||`Gegenstand #${id}`,nameEn:entry.name||`Item #${id}`,wowheadUrl:entry.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`,sourceType:entry.acq.sourceType||'Unbekannt',source:entry.acq.source||'Quelle nicht aufgelöst',catalyst:false,directTierTarget:false,setSlot:false,invalidForRaidfree:false,embellishment:'—',metaLabel:label,metaUsage:usage,note:`${label}: ${usage}. Quelle der Meta-Auswertung: Murlok.io.`});
        seen.add(id);
      }
    }
    return out;
  }

  function bisViewGear(){
    const cached=readStoredBisView(bisListView);
    if(cached?.length)return cached;
    return bisListView==='mythicplus'?visibleTargetGear():[];
  }
  async function fetchBisView(view,force=false){
    const key=bisViewCacheKey(view);
    const stored=readStoredBisView(view);
    if(!force&&stored?.length)return stored;
    const status=$('bisViewStatus');if(status){status.className='bis-view-status loading';status.textContent=`${bisViewLabel(view)}-BiS wird geladen …`;}
    try{
      const base=webApiBase();const u=new URL('/api/bis-refresh',base||location.origin);u.searchParams.set('profile',activeProfile.key);u.searchParams.set('view',view);
      const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});let d={};try{d=await r.json()}catch{}
      if(!r.ok||!d.ok||!Array.isArray(d.items))throw new Error(d?.detail||d?.error||`HTTP ${r.status}`);
      let gear=viewGearFromPayload(activeProfile,d,view);if(!gear.length)throw new Error('Die geladene BiS-Liste enthält keine auswertbaren Gegenstände.');
      if(view==='raid'||view==='overall'){
        const meta=await fetchMurlokMeta(activeProfile);
        if(meta)gear=mergeMurlokMetaGear(gear,meta,view,activeProfile);
      }
      return storeBisView(view,gear,d);
    }catch(e){
      // Bereits gespeicherte Ansicht weiter nutzbar halten, wenn KeystoneLoot vorübergehend nicht erreichbar ist.
      const fallbackStored=readStoredBisView(view);
      if(fallbackStored?.length)return fallbackStored;
      if(view==='mythicplus'){
        const fallback=visibleTargetGear();
        if(fallback.length)return fallback;
      }
      if(view==='raid'){
        const fallback=backgroundRaidTargets(activeProfile).map(r=>{const base=activeProfile.gear.find(x=>sameSlotGroup(x.slot,r.slot))||{};return{...base,...r,slotLabel:base.slotLabel||r.slot,sourceType:'Raid',source:r.source||'Raid-BiS',enchant:base.enchant||'—',embellishment:'—',note:'Raid-BiS aus dem integrierten Hintergrund-Snapshot.'}});
        if(fallback.length)return storeBisView(view,fallback,{source:'Integrierter Raid-BiS-Snapshot'});
      }
      throw e;
    }
  }
  function updateBisViewUi(){
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-bis-view]').forEach(b=>b.classList.toggle('active',b.dataset.bisView===bisListView));
    const h=$('gearHeading'),st=$('bisViewStatus');
    if(h)h.textContent=bisListView==='overall'?'Gesamt-BiS-Zielausrüstung':bisListView==='raid'?'Raid-BiS-Zielausrüstung':'Mythic+ BiS-Zielausrüstung';
    if(st){st.className='bis-view-status';st.textContent=bisListView==='overall'?'Ansicht: Gesamt · beste Ausrüstung über alle relevanten Quellen':bisListView==='raid'?'Ansicht: Raid · reine Raid-BiS-Liste':'Ansicht: Mythic+ · reine Mythic+- und Herstellungs-BiS-Liste';}
    const src=$('sourceFilter');if(src){src.innerHTML=bisListView==='mythicplus'?'<option value="all">Alle Quellen</option><option value="Mythic+">Nur Mythic+</option><option value="Hergestellt">Nur hergestellt</option>':'<option value="all">Alle Quellen</option>';src.value='all';}
  }
  async function setBisListView(view){
    if(!['overall','mythicplus','raid'].includes(view))view='mythicplus';
    bisListView=view;
    updateBisViewUi();
    try{
      await fetchBisView(view);
    }catch(e){
      const st=$('bisViewStatus');
      if(st){
        st.className='bis-view-status error';
        st.textContent=`${bisViewLabel(view)}-BiS konnte nicht geladen werden: ${e.message}`;
      }
      renderGear();
      return;
    }
    updateBisViewUi();
    renderGear();
  }

  // Die BiS-Umschaltung wird direkt hier gebunden und nicht vom restlichen Dashboard-Start abhängig gemacht.
  window.setBisListView=setBisListView;
  document.querySelectorAll('[data-bis-view]').forEach(btn=>{
    if(btn.dataset.bisViewBound==='1')return;
    btn.dataset.bisViewBound='1';
    btn.addEventListener('click',event=>{
      event.preventDefault();
      setBisListView(btn.dataset.bisView);
    });
  });

  function gearRows(){
    const q=normal($('search').value),source=$('sourceFilter').value,status=$('statusFilter').value,missing=$('missingOnly').checked;
    if(bisListView==='mythicplus'){
      comparisonCache=buildComparisons();return visibleTargetGear().filter(item=>{const st=comparisonCache.get(item.slot)?.status||{key:'unknown'};const hay=normal([item.slotLabel,item.name,item.nameEn,item.source,item.enchant,item.note,item.embellishment,item.tierName].join(' '));if(q&&!hay.includes(q))return false;if(source!=='all'&&item.sourceType!==source)return false;if(status!=='all'){if(status==='match'){if(!isFulfilledBisStatus(st.key))return false}else if(st.key!==status)return false}if(missing&&isFulfilledBisStatus(st.key))return false;return true})
    }
    return bisViewGear().filter(item=>{const st=viewTargetStatus(item),hay=normal([item.slotLabel,item.name,item.nameEn,item.source,item.enchant,item.note].join(' '));if(q&&!hay.includes(q))return false;if(status!=='all'){if(status==='match'){if(st.key!=='match')return false}else if(st.key!==status)return false}if(missing&&st.key==='match')return false;return true})
  }
  function renderGear(){
    if(!$('gearBody'))return;const rows=gearRows();
    if(bisListView==='mythicplus'){
      $('gearBody').innerHTML=rows.map(item=>{const c=comparisonCache.get(item.slot)||{},st=c.status||{key:'unknown',label:'Nicht geprüft'},pc=personalCraft(item),base=item.catalyst?catalystBaseFromItem(item):null,sourceType=pc?'Hergestellt':(base?.sourceType||item.sourceType),source=pc?'Persönlich hergestellt':displaySourceForItem(item);return `<tr class="${st.key==='match'||st.key==='raidbis'?'bis-equipped':st.key==='personal'?'personal-bis':''}"><td><span class="slot-badge">${escapeHtml(item.slotLabel)}</span></td><td>${targetHtml(item)}</td><td><span class="source-badge ${sourceType==='Hergestellt'?'crafted':''}">${escapeHtml(sourceType)}</span><div class="small">${escapeHtml(source)}</div></td><td class="small">${escapeHtml(item.enchant)}</td><td class="small">${item.embellishment!=='—'?`<div>${escapeHtml(item.embellishment)}</div>`:''}${item.catalyst?'<div class="tier-note">♻ Matrixkatalysator</div>':''}<div>${escapeHtml(item.note||'')}</div></td><td><span class="status ${st.key}">${escapeHtml(st.label)}</span></td></tr>`}).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="6">Keine Treffer.</td></tr>';
      renderMissing();renderCatalystBases();setTimeout(refreshWowhead,30);return;
    }
    $('gearBody').innerHTML=rows.map(item=>{const st=viewTargetStatus(item),url=item.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${Number(item.itemId)||0}`,meta=item.metaLabel?`<span class="set-target-note">${escapeHtml(item.metaLabel)}${item.metaUsage?` · ${escapeHtml(item.metaUsage)}`:''}</span>`:'';return `<tr class="${st.key==='match'?'bis-equipped':''}"><td><span class="slot-badge">${escapeHtml(item.slotLabel||item.slot)}</span></td><td><a class="item-name" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="medium">${escapeHtml(item.name||`Gegenstand #${item.itemId}`)}</a>${meta}</td><td><span class="source-badge ${item.sourceType==='Hergestellt'?'crafted':''}">${escapeHtml(item.sourceType||bisViewLabel())}</span><div class="small">${escapeHtml(item.source||bisViewSource())}</div></td><td class="small">${escapeHtml(item.enchant||'—')}</td><td class="small">${escapeHtml(item.note||'')}</td><td><span class="status ${st.key}">${escapeHtml(st.label)}</span></td></tr>`}).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="6">Keine Treffer.</td></tr>';
    setTimeout(refreshWowhead,30)
  }
  function comparisonTargetHtml(c){
    if(c?.status?.key==='raidbis'){
      const raid=backgroundRaidBisMatch(c.item,c.eq);const id=Number(raid?.itemId)||eqId(c.eq);const name=raid?.name||eqName(c.eq)||`Gegenstand #${id}`;const url=raid?.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`;
      return `<span class="raid-bis-target">◆ Raid-BiS (Hintergrund): <a href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wh-icon-size="medium">${escapeHtml(name)}</a></span><span class="general-target-note">Dungeon-Farmziel bleibt: ${escapeHtml(c.item.directTierTarget?(c.item.tierName||c.item.name):c.item.name)}</span>`;
    }
    return targetHtml(c.item)
  }
  function renderComparison(){if(!$('comparisonPanel'))return;if(!liveCharacter||!profileClassMatches()){$('comparisonPanel').classList.add('hidden');return}$('comparisonPanel').classList.remove('hidden');const planning=planningProfileActive();const planNote=$('comparisonPlanningNote');if(planNote){planNote.classList.toggle('hidden',!planning);planNote.innerHTML=planning?`<strong>Planungsmodus:</strong> ${escapeHtml(liveCharacter.name||'Charakter')} ist bei Raider.IO aktuell als <strong>${escapeHtml(deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||''))}</strong> gelistet. Der Vergleich wird trotzdem gegen <strong>${escapeHtml(activeProfile.label)}</strong> durchgeführt. Die aktuell angelegte Ausrüstung wird dafür slotweise mit diesem Zielprofil verglichen.`:''}comparisonCache=buildComparisons();const comps=visibleTargetGear().map(i=>comparisonCache.get(i.slot));const match=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='match').length,personal=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='personal').length,raidbis=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='raidbis').length,fulfilled=comps.filter(c=>isFulfilledBisStatus(c?.status?.key)).length,cat=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='catalyst').length,alt=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='alternative').length,miss=comps.filter(c=>c.status.key==='missing').length;$('comparisonCharacterName').textContent=liveCharacter.name;$('comparisonScore').textContent=`${fulfilled} / ${visibleTargetGear().length}`;$('comparisonProgress').style.width=`${Math.round(fulfilled/Math.max(1,visibleTargetGear().length)*100)}%`;const tier=tierSetStatus();$('comparisonSummary').innerHTML=`<span class="pill ${tier.active?'set-active':''}">◆ Set ${Math.min(tier.equipped,4)}/4${tier.active?' · aktiv':''}</span><span class="pill">✓ ${match} allgemeines BiS</span><span class="pill">★ ${personal} persönliches Craft-BiS</span><span class="pill raid-bis-pill">✓ ${raidbis} Raid-BiS als BiS gewertet</span><span class="pill">♻ ${cat} Katalysator</span><span class="pill">↗ ${alt} andere Items</span><span class="pill">? ${miss} unbekannt</span>`;$('comparisonBody').innerHTML=comps.map(c=>`<tr class="${c.status.key==='match'?'bis-equipped':c.status.key==='personal'?'personal-bis':c.status.key==='raidbis'?'bis-equipped':''}"><td><span class="slot-badge">${escapeHtml(c.item.slotLabel)}</span></td><td>${currentHtml(c.eq)}</td><td>${comparisonTargetHtml(c)}</td><td class="small">${escapeHtml(displaySourceForItem(c.item))}</td><td class="small">${escapeHtml(c.item.enchant)}</td><td><span class="status ${c.status.key}">${escapeHtml(c.status.label)}</span></td></tr>`).join('');setTimeout(refreshWowhead,30)}
  function renderMissing(){if(!liveCharacter||!profileClassMatches()){$('missingList').innerHTML='<p class="muted">Nach dem Laden eines Charakters derselben Klasse erscheint hier die persönliche Upgrade-Liste.</p>';return}comparisonCache=buildComparisons();const tier=tierSetStatus();const setWarning=tier.candidates>=4&&tier.equipped<4?`<div class="api-message warning set-warning"><strong>◆ 4er-Set noch nicht vollständig:</strong> ${tier.equipped}/4 Setteile erkannt. Setteile haben Vorrang vor normalen Slot-Upgrades.</div>`:'';const arr=visibleTargetGear().map(i=>comparisonCache.get(i.slot)).filter(c=>!isFulfilledBisStatus(c?.status?.key));$('missingList').innerHTML=setWarning+(arr.length?arr.map(c=>`<div class="missing-item"><strong>${escapeHtml(c.item.directTierTarget?(c.item.tierName||c.item.name):c.item.name)}</strong><div class="small">${escapeHtml(c.item.slotLabel)} · ${escapeHtml(isGenericKeystoneSource(c.item.source)?knownAcquisition(c.item,c.item,c.item.sourceType==='Hergestellt').source:c.item.source)} · ${escapeHtml(c.status.label)}</div></div>`).join(''):'<div class="api-message ok">✓ Alle hinterlegten BiS-Ziele sind angelegt.</div>')}

  function renderConsumables(){const ench=activeProfile.gear.filter(x=>!x.enchant.startsWith('Keine reguläre')).map(x=>`<div class="consumable-row"><b>${escapeHtml(x.slotLabel)}</b><span class="small">${escapeHtml(x.enchant)}</span></div>`).join('');$('consumables').innerHTML=`<div class="consumable-section"><h3>Gängige Verzauberungen</h3>${ench}</div><div class="consumable-section"><h3>Edelsteine</h3><div class="gem-list">${activeProfile.gems.map(x=>`<span>${escapeHtml(x)}</span>`).join('')}</div></div><div class="consumable-section"><h3>Verbrauchsgüter</h3>${activeProfile.consumables.map(x=>`<div class="consumable-row"><b>${escapeHtml(x.label)}</b><span class="small">${escapeHtml(x.value)}</span></div>`).join('')}</div>`}
  const GLOBAL_BIS_SOURCES=[
    {label:'Archon · Mythic+',url:'https://www.archon.gg/wow/builds',purpose:'M+-Meta, Build-, Gear-, Talent- und Verzauberungsverteilungen zur Plausibilisierung der BiS-Empfehlungen.'},

  function sourcePurpose(x){const l=normal(x?.label||'');if(l.includes('keystoneloot'))return 'Primäre raidfreie Mythic+-/Crafting-Quelle und Importbasis für die BiS-Zielausrüstung.';if(l.includes('wowhead'))return 'Spezialisierungs-Guides, BiS-Einordnung, Set-/Crafting-Hinweise, Verzauberungen und Verbrauchsgüter.';if(l.includes('raider'))return 'Geladene Charakterausrüstung, Mythic+-Wertung und zuletzt bekannte Keys.';return x?.purpose||'Ergänzende Datenquelle für dieses Profil.'}
  function dashboardSources(){const merged=[...(activeProfile.sources||[]).map(x=>({...x,purpose:sourcePurpose(x)})),...GLOBAL_BIS_SOURCES];const seen=new Set();return merged.filter(x=>{const key=normal(x.url||x.label);if(!key||seen.has(key))return false;seen.add(key);return true})}
  function renderSources(){$('sourceGrid').innerHTML=dashboardSources().map(x=>`<a class="source-card" href="${x.url}" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><strong>${escapeHtml(x.label)}</strong><span>${escapeHtml(x.purpose||'Datenquelle')}</span></a>`).join('')}
  function acquisitionSourceHtml(item){
    const base=item.catalyst?catalystBaseFromItem(item):null;
    const type=(base?.sourceType||item.sourceType)==='Hergestellt'?'Beruf':'Dungeon';
    const cls=type==='Beruf'?'crafted':'mplus';
    const shownSource=base?.source||displaySourceForItem(item)||'Nicht angegeben';
    const main=`<span class="source-badge ${cls}">${type}</span><strong>${escapeHtml(shownSource)}</strong>`;
    const catalyst=item.catalyst?(base?`<div class="acquisition-extra">♻ Bestes Basisitem: <b><a class="item-name" href="${base.wowheadUrl}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escapeHtml(base.name)}</a></b> → im Matrixkatalysator zu <b>${escapeHtml(item.tierName||'dem Set-Teil')}</b> umwandeln.</div>`:`<div class="acquisition-extra">♻ Das konkrete beste Basisitem wird beim nächsten KeystoneLoot-BiS-Update/Import ergänzt und danach hier samt Dungeon angezeigt.</div>`):'';
    return `<div class="acquisition-source">${main}${catalyst}</div>`;
  }
  function renderAcquisitionSources(){

  }
  const BUFF_STORE='wow-bis-buff-profile:';
  const RATING_PER_PERCENT={crit:46,haste:44,versatility:54,mastery:46};
  const MASTERY_PER_POINT={'schamane-elementar':1.875,'schamane-verstaerkung':2.5,'jaeger-tierherrschaft':1.9,'paladin-schutz-defensiv':1.0,'druide-waechter-defensiv':0.75};
  const FLASKS={
    none:null,
    magisters:{label:'Fläschchen der Magister',stat:'mastery',rating:165},
    'shattered-sun':{label:'Fläschchen der Zerschmetterten Sonne',stat:'crit',rating:165},
    'blood-knights':{label:'Fläschchen der Blutritter',stat:'haste',rating:165},
    'thalassian-resistance':{label:'Fläschchen des thalassischen Widerstands',stat:'versatility',rating:165}
  };
  const FOODS={
    none:null,
    harandar:{label:'Harandarfeier',primary:true},
    'champions-bento':{label:"Champion's Bento",highest:true,rating:65},
    'flora-frenzy':{label:'Flora Frenzy',highest:true,rating:65},

  function defaultBuffSettings(){return{enabled:true,raid:['schamane-elementar','schamane-verstaerkung'].includes(activeProfile.key)?'skyfury':'none',flask:'none',food:'none',highestTarget:activeProfile.key==='paladin-schutz-defensiv'?'haste':activeProfile.key==='druide-waechter-defensiv'?'haste':'mastery',custom:{crit:0,haste:0,mastery:0,versatility:0}}}
  function buffStoreKey(){return BUFF_STORE+activeProfile.key}
  function getBuffSettings(){const d=defaultBuffSettings();try{const s=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(buffStoreKey())||'null');if(!s)return d;return{...d,...s,custom:{...d.custom,...(s.custom||{})}}}catch{return d}}
  function saveBuffSettings(s){try{localStorage.setItem(buffStoreKey(),JSON.stringify(s))}catch{}}
  function readBuffSettingsFromUI(){const num=id=>{const n=parseFloat($(id)?.value);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:0};return{enabled:Boolean($('buffsEnabled')?.checked),raid:$('buffRaid')?.value||'none',flask:$('buffFlask')?.value||'none',food:$('buffFood')?.value||'none',highestTarget:$('buffHighestTarget')?.value||'mastery',custom:{crit:num('buffCustomCrit'),haste:num('buffCustomHaste'),mastery:num('buffCustomMastery'),versatility:num('buffCustomVersatility')}}}
  function loadBuffSettingsToUI(){if(!$('buffsEnabled'))return;const s=getBuffSettings();$('buffsEnabled').checked=s.enabled!==false;$('buffRaid').value=s.raid||'none';$('buffFlask').value=s.flask||'none';$('buffFood').value=s.food||'none';$('buffHighestTarget').value=s.highestTarget||'mastery';for(const [id,k] of [['buffCustomCrit','crit'],['buffCustomHaste','haste'],['buffCustomMastery','mastery'],['buffCustomVersatility','versatility']])$(id).value=Number(s.custom?.[k]||0)}
  function ratingToPercent(stat,rating){const base=Number(rating)/RATING_PER_PERCENT[stat];if(stat!=='mastery')return base;return base*Number(MASTERY_PER_POINT[activeProfile.key]||1)}
  function calculateBuffBonuses(){const s=getBuffSettings(),bonus={crit:0,haste:0,mastery:0,versatility:0},details=[];let approx=false;if(!s.enabled)return{bonus,details:['Buffprofil deaktiviert'],approx:false,settings:s};
    if(s.raid==='skyfury'){bonus.mastery+=2;details.push('Skyfury: +2 % Meisterschaft')}
    const flask=FLASKS[s.flask];if(flask){const p=ratingToPercent(flask.stat,flask.rating);bonus[flask.stat]+=p;details.push(`${flask.label}: +${flask.rating} ${statLabels[flask.stat]} ≈ +${fmt(p)} %`);approx=true}
    const food=FOODS[s.food];if(food){if(food.primary)details.push(`${food.label}: Primärwert, kein Sekundärwert-Bonus`);else{const stat=food.highest?(s.highestTarget||'mastery'):food.stat,p=ratingToPercent(stat,food.rating);bonus[stat]+=p;details.push(`${food.label}: +${food.rating} ${statLabels[stat]} ≈ +${fmt(p)} %`);approx=true}}
    for(const k of ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility']){const v=Number(s.custom?.[k]||0);if(Number.isFinite(v)&&v!==0){bonus[k]+=v;details.push(`Eigene Korrektur ${statLabels[k]}: ${v>0?'+':''}${fmt(v)} %`)}}
    return{bonus,details,approx,settings:s};
  }
  function effectiveStats(){if(!characterStats)return null;const b=calculateBuffBonuses().bonus,out={};for(const k of ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'])out[k]=Number.isFinite(statNumber(characterStats[k]))?statNumber(characterStats[k])+Number(b[k]||0):null;return out}
  function renderBuffSummary(){if(!$('buffSummary'))return;const x=calculateBuffBonuses();const active=Object.entries(x.bonus).filter(([,v])=>Math.abs(v)>0.001).map(([k,v])=>`${statLabels[k]} ${v>=0?'+':''}${fmt(v)} %`);$('buffSummary').innerHTML=`<strong>Berechnung:</strong> ${active.length?active.join(' · '):'keine Sekundärwert-Boni'}${x.approx?' · <span class="muted">Rating→% ist eine Näherung; Diminishing Returns können den tatsächlichen Ingame-Wert reduzieren.</span>':''}`}
  function buffSettingsChanged(){const s=readBuffSettingsFromUI();saveBuffSettings(s);renderBuffSummary();renderStatComparison()}
  function resetBuffSettings(){try{localStorage.removeItem(buffStoreKey())}catch{}loadBuffSettingsToUI();renderBuffSummary();renderStatComparison()}

  function renderStatPriority(){const m=activeProfile.statModel;$('statHeadline').textContent=m.headline;$('statDescription').textContent=m.description;$('priorityPath').innerHTML=m.priority.map(s=>{const t=Number(m?.targets?.[s.key]);return `<div class="priority-chip"><span class="priority-rank">${escapeHtml(s.rank)}</span><strong>${escapeHtml(s.label)}</strong>${Number.isFinite(t)?`<span class="target-mini">Ziel ${fmt(t)} %</span>`:''}</div>`}).join('');renderStatComparison()}
  function elementalMasteryInfo(v){
    const m=activeProfile.statModel||{},target=Number(m.masteryTargetBuffed||72),x=calculateBuffBonuses(),buff=Number(x.bonus.mastery||0);
    const base=statNumber(v),buffed=Number.isFinite(base)?base+buff:null;
    const labels=[];if(x.settings?.raid==='skyfury')labels.push('Skyfury');if(FLASKS[x.settings?.flask]?.stat==='mastery')labels.push(FLASKS[x.settings.flask].label);const f=FOODS[x.settings?.food];if(f&&(f.stat==='mastery'||(f.highest&&x.settings.highestTarget==='mastery')))labels.push(f.label);if(Number(x.settings?.custom?.mastery||0))labels.push('eigene Korrektur');
    return{target,buff,buffed,label:labels.length?labels.join(' + '):'keine Meisterschafts-Buffs'};
  }
  function statState(k,v){
    if(!Number.isFinite(v))return{cls:'',text:'Kein Wert verfügbar'};
    if(activeProfile.key==='schamane-elementar'&&k==='mastery'){
      const x=elementalMasteryInfo(v),delta=x.target-x.buffed;
      if(delta>0.05)return{cls:'warn',text:`${fmt(x.buffed)} % mit Buffprofil · noch ${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte bis ${fmt(x.target)} %`};
      return{cls:'good',text:`${fmt(x.buffed)} % mit Buffprofil · 72-%-Breakpoint erreicht`};
    }
    if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(tertiaryLabels,k)){
      const target=Number(activeProfile.statModel?.tertiaryTargets?.[k]);
      if(Number.isFinite(target)){
        if(target<=0){
          if(v<=0.05)return{cls:'good',text:'Ziel 0 % erreicht'};
          return{cls:'warn',text:`Ziel 0 % · ${fmt(v)} %-Punkte darüber`};
        }
        const delta=target-v;
        if(delta>0.05)return{cls:'warn',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % · noch ${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte`};
        if(delta<-0.05)return{cls:'good',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % · ${fmt(Math.abs(delta))} %-Punkte darüber`};
        return{cls:'good',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % erreicht`};
      }
      return{cls:'',text:'Kein Zielwert hinterlegt'};
    }
    const target=Number(activeProfile.statModel?.targets?.[k]);
    if(Number.isFinite(target)){
      const delta=target-v,exact=activeProfile.statModel?.targetMode==='exact';
      if(delta>0.05)return{cls:'warn',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % · noch ${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte`};
      if(delta<-0.05)return{cls:exact?'warn exact-over':'good',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % · ${fmt(Math.abs(delta))} %-Punkte darüber${exact?' · für dieses Profil reduzieren':''}`};
      return{cls:'good',text:`Ziel ${fmt(target)} % erreicht`};
    }
    const order=activeProfile.statModel.priority.map(x=>x.key),idx=order.indexOf(k);if(idx<0)return{cls:'',text:'Erkannt'};
    return idx<=1?{cls:'good',text:'Hohe Priorität'}:{cls:'warn',text:'Nachrangiger Wert'};
  }
  function statTargetValue(k){
    if(activeProfile.key==='schamane-elementar'&&k==='mastery'){
      const t=Number(activeProfile.statModel?.masteryTargetBuffed||72);
      return Number.isFinite(t)&&t>0?t:null;
    }
    const primaryTarget=Number(activeProfile.statModel?.targets?.[k]);
    if(Number.isFinite(primaryTarget)&&primaryTarget>0)return primaryTarget;
    const tertiaryTarget=Number(activeProfile.statModel?.tertiaryTargets?.[k]);
    return Number.isFinite(tertiaryTarget)&&tertiaryTarget>=0?tertiaryTarget:null;
  }
  function renderTertiaryTargets(){
    // Tertiärwerte werden seit 2026-10-22 direkt zusammen mit den übrigen Basiswerten gerendert.
  }
  function statProgressHtml(k,value,{buffed=false}={}){
    const target=statTargetValue(k);
    if(!Number.isFinite(target)||!Number.isFinite(Number(value)))return '';
    const v=Number(value),ratio=target>0?v/target:(v<=0.05?1:0),pct=Math.max(0,Math.min(100,ratio*100));
    const delta=v-target;
    const zeroTarget=target<=0;
    const state=zeroTarget?(v<=0.05?'reached':'over'):(delta>0.05?'over':(Math.abs(delta)<=0.05?'reached':'missing'));
    const status=zeroTarget
      ?(v<=0.05?'Ziel erreicht':`+${fmt(v)} %-Punkte über Ziel`)
      :state==='over'
        ?`+${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte über Ziel`
        :state==='reached'
          ?'Ziel erreicht'
          :`${fmt(Math.abs(delta))} %-Punkte fehlen`;
    const label=buffed?'Gebufft':'Basis';
    return `<div class="stat-target-progress ${state}">
      <div class="stat-target-progress-head"><span>${label}: ${fmt(v)} %</span><span>Ziel ${fmt(target)} % · ${status}</span></div>
      <div class="stat-target-track" role="progressbar" aria-label="${escapeHtml(statLabels[k])} ${escapeHtml(label)}" aria-valuemin="0" aria-valuemax="${target}" aria-valuenow="${v}">
        <div class="stat-target-fill" style="width:${pct.toFixed(2)}%"></div>
        <i class="stat-target-marker" title="Ziel ${fmt(target)} %"></i>
      </div>
    </div>`;
  }

  function renderStatComparison(){
    if(!liveCharacter){$('statsLive').classList.add('hidden');$('statSource').textContent='Noch kein Charakter geladen';renderTertiaryTargets();return}
    $('statsLive').classList.remove('hidden');renderBuffSummary();renderTertiaryTargets();
    const buff=calculateBuffBonuses(),eff=effectiveStats();
    $('statSource').textContent=(characterStats?.sourceLabel||'Charakterwerte nicht automatisch verfügbar')+(buff.settings.enabled?' · Buffprofil aktiv':' · Buffprofil aus')+(planningProfileActive()?` · Achtung: Armory-Werte stammen aus ${deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||'der aktuellen Spezialisierung')}`:'');
    const keys=['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'];
    const baseKeys=[...keys,'leech','avoidance','speed'];

    // Obere Zeile: alle tatsächlich ermittelten Basiswerte in einer einheitlichen Kartenansicht.
    $('statGrid').innerHTML=baseKeys.map(k=>{
      const base=statNumber(characterStats?.[k]);
      const st=statState(k,base);
      const shown=Number.isFinite(base)?`${fmtStat(base)} %`:'—';
      const progress=statProgressHtml(k,base,{buffed:false});
      const typeText=Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(tertiaryLabels,k)?'Tertiärwert':'Basiswert';
      const src=statSourceInfo(k);
      return `<article class="stat-card ${st.cls}"><span>${statLabels[k]}</span>${progress}<strong>${shown}</strong><div class="small" style="clear:both;padding-top:6px">${typeText} · ${st.text}</div><span class="stat-source-badge ${src.cls}">${escapeHtml(src.label)}</span></article>`;
    }).join('');

    // Zweite Zeile: die vier regulären Sekundärwerte plus das gespeicherte Buff-/Food-/Fläschchenprofil.
    const anyBuff=keys.some(k=>Math.abs(Number(buff.bonus[k]||0))>0.001);
    if($('buffedStatsHint'))$('buffedStatsHint').textContent=buff.settings.enabled
      ?(anyBuff?'Gespeichertes Buffprofil ist eingerechnet. Rating→% ist bei Fläschchen/Food eine Näherung.':'Buffprofil ist aktiv, enthält aber aktuell keinen Sekundärwert-Bonus.')
      :'Buffprofil ist deaktiviert – daher entsprechen diese Werte den Basiswerten.';
    if($('buffedStatGrid'))$('buffedStatGrid').innerHTML=keys.map(k=>{
      const base=statNumber(characterStats?.[k]),value=statNumber(eff?.[k]),delta=Number(buff.bonus[k]||0);
      const state=statState(k,activeProfile.statModel?.targets&&Number.isFinite(value)?value:(Number.isFinite(base)?base:value));
      const shown=Number.isFinite(value)?`${fmtStat(value)} %`:'—';
      const deltaText=Number.isFinite(base)&&Number.isFinite(value)
        ?(Math.abs(delta)>0.001?`Basis ${fmt(base)} % · Buff ${delta>=0?'+':''}${fmt(delta)} %`:`Basis ${fmt(base)} % · kein Sekundärwert-Bonus`)
        :'Kein Basiswert verfügbar';
      const progress=statProgressHtml(k,value,{buffed:true});
      return `<article class="stat-card buffed-stat-card ${state.cls}"><span>${statLabels[k]}</span>${progress}<strong>${shown}</strong><div class="buff-delta">${deltaText}</div></article>`;
    }).join('');

    const advice=[];
    if(planningProfileActive())advice.push(`Planungsprofil aktiv: Die persönliche BiS-Ausrüstung wird gegen ${activeProfile.label} berechnet. Die automatisch ausgelesenen Prozentwerte stammen jedoch noch aus der bei Raider.IO/Armory aktiven Spezialisierung ${deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||'')}; insbesondere Meisterschaft kann sich nach dem Spezialisierungswechsel ändern.`);
    if(characterStats){
      const order=activeProfile.statModel.priority.map(x=>x.key);
      if(activeProfile.key==='schamane-elementar'){
        const x=elementalMasteryInfo(characterStats.mastery),delta=x.target-x.buffed;
        if(Number.isFinite(x.buffed)&&delta>0.05)advice.push(`Meisterschaft weiter priorisieren: ${fmt(characterStats.mastery)} % Basis ergeben mit deinem gespeicherten Buffprofil etwa ${fmt(x.buffed)} %. Es fehlen noch ${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte bis zum 72-%-Ziel im gebufften Zustand.`);
        else if(Number.isFinite(x.buffed))advice.push(`72-%-Meisterschafts-Breakpoint mit deinem gespeicherten Buffprofil erreicht. Weitere Sekundärwerte nun vor allem zwischen Krit und Tempo verteilen; Vielseitigkeit möglichst vermeiden.`);
      }else if(activeProfile.statModel?.targets){
        const targets=activeProfile.statModel.targets||{};
        const targetOrder=['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'];
        const missing=[],over=[],reached=[];
        for(const k of targetOrder){
          const target=Number(targets[k]),value=statNumber(eff?.[k]);
          if(!Number.isFinite(target)||!Number.isFinite(value))continue;
          const delta=target-value;
          if(delta>0.05)missing.push(`${statLabels[k]}: +${fmt(delta)} %-Punkte bis ${fmt(target)} %`);
          else if(delta<-0.05)over.push(`${statLabels[k]}: ${fmt(Math.abs(delta))} %-Punkte über ${fmt(target)} %`);
          else reached.push(`${statLabels[k]} ${fmt(target)} %`);
        }
        const prefix=activeProfile.key.startsWith('todesritter-blut')?'Blut-DK-Zielwerte':'Hinterlegte Zielwerte';
        if(missing.length)advice.push(`${prefix}: ${missing.join(' · ')}.`);
        if(activeProfile.statModel?.targetMode==='exact'&&over.length)advice.push(`Über Ziel: ${over.join(' · ')}. Bei neuen Items/Gems bevorzugt in noch fehlende Werte umverteilen.`);
        if(!missing.length&&!over.length&&reached.length)advice.push(`Alle hinterlegten Zielwerte sind mit deinem aktuellen Buffprofil getroffen.`);
        if(activeProfile.statModel?.tertiaryTargets){const tt=activeProfile.statModel.tertiaryTargets||{},parts=[];for(const k of ['leech','avoidance','speed']){const t=Number(tt[k]),v=statNumber(characterStats?.[k]);if(!Number.isFinite(t))continue;if(Number.isFinite(v)){const d=t-v;parts.push(`${tertiaryLabels[k]} ${fmt(v)} / ${fmt(t)} %${d>0.05?` · noch ${fmt(d)} %-Punkte`:d<-0.05?` · ${fmt(Math.abs(d))} über Ziel`:' · Ziel erreicht'}`)}else parts.push(`${tertiaryLabels[k]} — / ${fmt(t)} %`)}advice.push(`Tertiärwerte: ${parts.join(' · ')}.`);}
        advice.push('Für Zielwertprofile verwendet die Bewertung die zweite Wertezeile inklusive des gespeicherten Buffprofils.');
      }else advice.push(`Priorität dieses Profils: ${activeProfile.statModel.headline}. Für die Empfehlung wird die zweite Wertezeile inklusive deines gespeicherten Buffprofils verwendet.`)
    }else advice.push(statsReadStatus==='loading'?'Automatische Charakterwerte werden abgerufen …':statsReadStatus==='failed'?'Keine vollständigen Charakterwerte empfangen. Die Abrufdetails zeigen, welche Verbindung oder Auswertung gescheitert ist. Du kannst den Armory-Werteblock einlesen oder Werte manuell eintragen.':'Automatische Charakterwerte wurden noch nicht geladen.');
    $('statDiagnostics').hidden=!window.__lastStatReadError;
    $('statDiagnosticText').textContent=window.__lastStatReadError||'';
    if(characterStats?.equipmentEnhancements){
      const e=characterStats.equipmentEnhancements,applied=Object.entries(e.appliedRatings||{}).filter(([,v])=>Number(v)>0);
      const correction=applied.length?` · ergänzt: ${applied.map(([k,v])=>`${statLabels[k]||k} +${Math.round(Number(v))} Wertung`).join(', ')}`:' · keine doppelte Anrechnung nötig';
      advice.push(`Ausrüstungsboni geprüft: ${Number(e.enchantCount||0)} Verzauberung(en), ${Number(e.gemCount||0)} Edelstein(e) erkannt${correction}.`);
    }
    $('statAdvice').innerHTML=`<strong>Was fehlt noch?</strong><ul>${advice.map(x=>`<li>${escapeHtml(x)}</li>`).join('')}</ul>`;
    for(const [id,k] of [['manualCrit','crit'],['manualHaste','haste'],['manualMastery','mastery'],['manualVersatility','versatility'],['manualLeech','leech'],['manualAvoidance','avoidance'],['manualSpeed','speed']])$(id).value=Number.isFinite(characterStats?.[k])?characterStats[k]:''
  }

  function requestedCharacterIdentity(s={}){
    const region=normal(s.region||$('region')?.value)||'eu';
    const realm=cleanRealmSlug(s.realm||$('realm')?.value||'');
    const name=normal(s.character||s.name||$('character')?.value||'');
    return [region,realm,name].join('|');
  }
  function resolvedCharacterIdentity(character=liveCharacter,fallback={}){
    if(!character)return requestedCharacterIdentity(fallback);
    return requestedCharacterIdentity({region:character.region||fallback.region,realm:character.realm||fallback.realm,name:character.name||fallback.character||fallback.name});
  }

    window.__browserStatErrors=[];
    const statsLive=$('statsLive');if(statsLive)statsLive.classList.add('hidden');
    const statSource=$('statSource');if(statSource)statSource.textContent=reason;
    for(const id of ['manualCrit','manualHaste','manualMastery','manualVersatility','manualLeech','manualAvoidance','manualSpeed']){const el=$(id);if(el)el.value=''}
  }

  }

  function manualKey(){return liveCharacter?`wow-bis-manual:${normal($('region').value)}:${normal($('realm').value)}:${normal($('character').value)}`:null}
  function applyManual(){statsLoadSerial++;statsReadStatus='idle';const r=id=>{const n=parseFloat($(id).value);return Number.isFinite(n)&&n>=0?n:null};const vals={crit:r('manualCrit'),haste:r('manualHaste'),mastery:r('manualMastery'),versatility:r('manualVersatility'),leech:r('manualLeech'),avoidance:r('manualAvoidance'),speed:r('manualSpeed')};const statSources={};for(const k of ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility','leech','avoidance','speed'])if(Number.isFinite(statNumber(vals[k])))statSources[k]='Manuell';characterStats={...vals,statSources,sourceLabel:'Manuell eingetragene Werte'};if(manualKey())localStorage.setItem(manualKey(),JSON.stringify(characterStats));renderStatComparison()}
  function statNumber(v){return (typeof v==='number'||typeof v==='string'&&v.trim()!=='')&&Number.isFinite(Number(v))?Number(v):NaN}
  function completeStats(v){return Boolean(v&&['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'].every(k=>Number.isFinite(statNumber(v[k]))&&statNumber(v[k])>=0))}
  function completeTertiaryStats(v){return Boolean(v&&['leech','avoidance','speed'].every(k=>Number.isFinite(statNumber(v[k]))&&statNumber(v[k])>=0))}
  function completeAllStats(v){return completeStats(v)&&completeTertiaryStats(v)}
  function statSourceInfo(k){
    const explicit=characterStats?.statSources?.[k];
    const source=String(explicit||characterStats?.sourceLabel||'').toLowerCase();
    if(source.includes('verifiziert')||source.includes('ingame-wert'))return{label:'Verifiziert · Ingame',cls:'auto'};
    if(source.includes('manuell'))return{label:'Manuell',cls:'manual'};
    if(source.includes('blizzard api')||source.includes('character statistics'))return{label:'Automatisch · Blizzard API',cls:'auto'};
    if(source.includes('automatisch')||source.includes('armory')||source.includes('wowarmory'))return{label:'Automatisch · Armory-Fallback',cls:'fallback'};
    return{label:Number.isFinite(statNumber(characterStats?.[k]))?'Übernommen':'Nicht verfügbar',cls:'fallback'};
  }
  function cleanArmoryText(raw){return String(raw||'').replace(/<script\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/script>/gi,' ').replace(/<style\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/style>/gi,' ').replace(/<svg\b[^>]*>[\s\S]*?<\/svg>/gi,' ').replace(/<br\s*\/?>/gi,' ').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/&nbsp;|&#160;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim()}
  function embeddedArmoryText(raw){
    let text=String(raw||'');
    text=text.replace(/\\u([0-9a-fA-F]{4})/g,(_,h)=>{try{return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(h,16))}catch{return _}})
      .replace(/\\\//g,'/').replace(/\\"/g,'"').replace(/\\n|\\t/g,' ').replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ')
      .replace(/&nbsp;|&#160;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();
    return text;
  }
  function armoryStatWindow(text,lang){
    const cfg=lang==='de'?{exports:['Charakterbild speichern','Charakterbild exportieren'],resources:['Gesundheit'],ends:['Spezialisierung','Talentdetails anzeigen']}:{exports:['Export character image','Save character image'],resources:['Health'],ends:['Specialization','Talent Details']};
    let start=-1;
    for(const a of cfg.exports){const pos=text.indexOf(a);if(pos>=0)start=Math.max(start,pos+a.length)}
    let method='Armory-Statbereich';
    if(start<0){const ps=cfg.resources.map(a=>text.indexOf(a)).filter(x=>x>=0);if(ps.length){start=Math.max(0,Math.min(...ps)-80);method='Armory-Statbereich (Health-Anker)'}}
    if(start<0)return null;
    const ends=cfg.ends.map(a=>text.indexOf(a,start)).filter(x=>x>=0);const end=ends.length?Math.min(...ends):Math.min(text.length,start+1800);
    return {text:text.slice(start,Math.min(end,start+2200)),method};
  }
  function parseWindowStats(window,labels){
    // Determine a consistent orientation for the entire block. Mixing the
    // preceding and following numbers can assign Crit's percentage to Haste.
    const text=String(window||'').replace(/!\[[^\]]*\]\([^)]*\)/g,' ')
      .replace(/\[([^\]]+)\]\([^)]*\)/g,'$1').replace(/[*_`#|]/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ');
    const found=[];
    for(const direction of ['before','after']){
      const out={};let valid=true;
      for(const key of ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility']){
        const number=String.raw`(\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?)\s*%`;
        const pattern=direction==='before'?String.raw`${number}\s*[:\-–—]?\s*(?:${labels[key]})\b`:String.raw`\b(?:${labels[key]})\s*[:\-–—]?\s*${number}`;
        const matches=[...text.matchAll(new RegExp(pattern,'ig'))].map(m=>Number(m[1].replace(',','.')));
        if(matches.some(v=>v!==matches[0]))return null;
        if(!matches.length){valid=false;break}
        out[key]=matches[0];
      }
      if(valid&&completeStats(out))found.push(attachTertiaryStats(out,text,labels));
    }
    return found.length&&found.every(v=>statsClose(v,found[0],0))?found[0]:null;
  }

  function nearestPercentToLabel(text,start,end,radius=90){
    const lo=Math.max(0,start-radius),hi=Math.min(text.length,end+radius),seg=text.slice(lo,hi),re=/(\d{1,3}(?:[.,]\d{1,2})?)\s*%/g;let m,best=null;
    while((m=re.exec(seg))){const a=lo+m.index,b=a+m[0].length,dist=b<=start?start-b:a>=end?a-end:0,c={value:Number(m[1].replace(',','.')),distance:dist,pos:start};if(!best||c.distance<best.distance)best=c}
    return best;
  }
  function attachTertiaryStats(stats,text,labels){
    if(!stats||!text||!labels)return stats;
    const out={...stats};
    for(const key of ['leech','avoidance','speed']){
      const label=labels[key];if(!label)continue;
      const hits=[];const re=new RegExp(`(?:${label})`,'ig');let m;
      while((m=re.exec(text))){const x=nearestPercentToLabel(text,m.index,m.index+m[0].length,75);if(x&&Number.isFinite(x.value)&&x.value>=0&&x.value<=100)hits.push(x)}
      if(!hits.length)continue;
      hits.sort((a,b)=>a.distance-b.distance);const best=hits[0];
      const near=hits.filter(x=>x.distance<=best.distance+8);
      if(near.every(x=>Math.abs(x.value-best.value)<=0.05))out[key]=best.value;
    }
    return out;
  }
  function parseLabelProximityStats(text,labels){
    if(!text)return null;const hits={};
