    for(const key of ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility']){hits[key]=[];const re=new RegExp(`(?:${labels[key]})`,'ig');let m;while((m=re.exec(text))){const x=nearestPercentToLabel(text,m.index,m.index+m[0].length);if(x)hits[key].push(x)}if(!hits[key].length)return null}
    const candidates=[];
    for(const c of hits.crit)for(const h of hits.haste){if(h.pos<=c.pos||h.pos-c.pos>500)continue;for(const m of hits.mastery){if(m.pos<=h.pos||m.pos-h.pos>500)continue;for(const v of hits.versatility){if(v.pos<=m.pos||v.pos-m.pos>500)continue;const span=v.pos-c.pos;if(span>1100)continue;const stats={crit:c.value,haste:h.value,mastery:m.value,versatility:v.value};if(!completeStats(stats))continue;const context=text.slice(Math.max(0,c.pos-360),Math.min(text.length,v.pos+300));let score=span+4*(c.distance+h.distance+m.distance+v.distance);if(/(?:Gesundheit|Health).{0,420}(?:Fokus|Focus).{0,220}(?:Beweglichkeit|Agility)/is.test(context))score-=800;if(/(?:Ausdauer|Stamina)/i.test(context))score-=180;if(/(?:Spezialisierung|Specialization)/i.test(context))score-=250;if(/(?:Verzaubert|Enchanted:|Enchant(?:ed)?\s)/i.test(context))score+=250;candidates.push({score,stats})}}}
    if(!candidates.length)return null;candidates.sort((a,b)=>a.score-b.score);const best=candidates[0];for(const x of candidates.slice(1)){if(x.score>best.score+120)break;if(!statsClose(best.stats,x.stats))return null}return best.stats;
  }

  function parseAnywhereStats(text,labels){return parseWindowStats(text,labels)}
  function parseBlizzardStatsText(raw){
    // String.raw is intentional: the previous browser fallback lost \\s / \\d
    // escapes in normal JS strings and therefore could never match reliably.
    const sets={
      de:{crit:String.raw`(?:Kritische\s+Trefferchance|Kritische\s+Trefferwertung|Kritischer\s+Trefferwert|Kritischer\s+Treffer|Krit)`,haste:'Tempo',mastery:'Meisterschaft',versatility:'Vielseitigkeit',leech:'Lebensraub',avoidance:'Vermeidung',speed:'Geschwindigkeit'},
      en:{crit:String.raw`Critical\s+Strike(?:\s+Chance)?`,haste:'Haste',mastery:'Mastery',versatility:'Versatility',leech:'Leech',avoidance:'Avoidance',speed:String.raw`(?:Movement\s+Speed|Speed)`}
    };
    for(const [text,kind] of [[cleanArmoryText(raw),'sichtbar'],[embeddedArmoryText(raw),'eingebettete Daten']]){
      
      for(const lang of ['de','en']){
        const w=armoryStatWindow(text,lang);
        if(w){const stats=parseWindowStats(w.text,sets[lang]);if(stats)return{stats,method:`${w.method} · Labelparser · ${kind} (${lang.toUpperCase()})`}}
      }
      // Zusätzlicher sicherer Fallback: auch sichtbaren Text als vollständige 4er-Sequenz prüfen.
      // Das hilft insbesondere bei Jägerseiten, deren Werteblock über Fokus/Beweglichkeit gerendert wird.
      for(const lang of ['de','en']){const stats=parseAnywhereStats(text,sets[lang]);if(stats)return{stats,method:`Blizzard-Wertefolge · ${kind} (${lang.toUpperCase()})`}}
    }
    return null;
  }
  function statsClose(a,b,t=.15){return ['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'].every(k=>Math.abs(Number(a[k])-Number(b[k]))<=t)}
  function clusterBrowserStats(items){const clusters=[];for(const cand of items){let c=clusters.find(x=>statsClose(x[0].stats,cand.stats));if(c)c.push(cand);else clusters.push([cand])}clusters.sort((a,b)=>b.length-a.length);return clusters}
  function chooseBrowserConsensus(candidates){
    if(!candidates.length)return null;
    const direct=candidates.filter(x=>x.direct),fallback=candidates.filter(x=>!x.direct);
    if(direct.length){const dc=clusterBrowserStats(direct);if(dc.length>1)return null;const best=dc[0],extra=fallback.filter(x=>statsClose(x.stats,best[0].stats));return{...best[0],verifiedBy:best.length+extra.length,confidence:'hoch'}}
    const clusters=clusterBrowserStats(fallback);if(!clusters.length)return null;if(clusters.length>1&&clusters[0].length===clusters[1].length)return null;if(clusters.length>1&&clusters[0].length<2)return null;return{...clusters[0][0],verifiedBy:clusters[0].length,confidence:clusters[0].length>=2?'hoch':'mittel'};
  }

  function cleanRealmSlug(value){
    return String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase().normalize('NFC')
      .replace(/[’'\s]+/g,'-').replace(/-+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'');
  }
  function realmSlugVariants(value){
    const raw=String(value||'').trim().toLowerCase().normalize('NFD').replace(/[\u0300-\u036f]/g,'').replace(/’/g,"'");const out=[];const add=v=>{v=v.replace(/-+/g,'-').replace(/^-|-$/g,'');if(v&&!out.includes(v))out.push(v)};add(raw.replace(/[\s']+/g,'-'));add(raw.replace(/'/g,'').replace(/\s+/g,'-'));add(raw.replace(/\s+/g,'-').replace(/[^a-z0-9_-]/g,''));add(raw.replace(/[^a-z0-9]/g,''));return out;
  }
  function rioCanonicalIdentity(){
    const fallback={region:normal(liveCharacter?.region||$('region').value)||'eu',realm:cleanRealmSlug(liveCharacter?.realm||$('realm').value),name:String(liveCharacter?.name||$('character').value).trim()};
    const raw=String(liveCharacter?.profile_url||'');
    try{
      const u=new URL(raw);const parts=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
      const i=parts.findIndex(x=>x.toLowerCase()==='characters');
      if(i>=0&&parts.length>=i+4){
        return{region:decodeURIComponent(parts[i+1]).toLowerCase(),realm:cleanRealmSlug(decodeURIComponent(parts[i+2])),name:decodeURIComponent(parts[i+3])||fallback.name,source:'Raider.IO Profil-URL'};
      }
    }catch{}
    return{...fallback,source:'Charakterdaten'};
  }
  function statIdentityCandidates(){
    const canonical=rioCanonicalIdentity(),bases=[canonical,
      {region:normal($('region').value)||canonical.region,realm:$('realm').value,name:$('character').value.trim(),source:'Eingabe'},
      {region:normal(liveCharacter?.region)||canonical.region,realm:liveCharacter?.realm,name:String(liveCharacter?.name||'').trim(),source:'Raider.IO Daten'}
    ];
    const expanded=[];for(const b of bases){const variants=b===canonical?[cleanRealmSlug(b.realm),...realmSlugVariants(b.realm)]:realmSlugVariants(b.realm);for(const realm of variants)expanded.push({...b,realm})}
    const seen=new Set();return expanded.filter(x=>{if(!x.realm||!x.name)return false;const k=[x.region,x.realm,normal(x.name)].join('|');if(seen.has(k))return false;seen.add(k);return true});
  }
  function blizzardCharacterUrls(){
    const out=[];
    for(const id of statIdentityCandidates()){
      const locales=id.region==='eu'?['de-de','en-gb']:id.region==='kr'?['ko-kr','en-us']:id.region==='tw'?['zh-tw','en-us']:['en-us'];
      for(const loc of locales)out.push({url:`https://worldofwarcraft.blizzard.com/${loc}/character/${encodeURIComponent(id.region)}/${encodeURIComponent(id.realm)}/${encodeURIComponent(id.name.toLowerCase())}`,identity:id,locale:loc});
    }
    return out;
  }
  function wowArmoryCharacterUrls(){
    const out=[];
    for(const id of statIdentityCandidates()){
      const region=(id.region==='eu'||id.region==='us')?id.region:null;
      if(!region)continue;
      out.push({url:`https://wowarmory.gg/${region}/character/${encodeURIComponent(id.realm)}/${encodeURIComponent(id.name.toLowerCase())}`,identity:id});
    }
    return out;
  }
  function parseWowArmoryStatsText(raw){
    // Parser v10: WoWArmory liefert je nach Abruf entweder HTML oder bereits
    // extrahierten Markdown/Text. Beide Formen werden zuerst in sichtbaren Text
    // normalisiert; dadurch funktionieren auch <span>16%</span><span>Critical Strike</span>.
    const source=String(raw||'');
    const variants=[];
    const add=t=>{t=String(t||'').replace(/\r/g,' ').replace(/\n+/g,' ').replace(/\s+/g,' ').trim();if(t&&!variants.includes(t))variants.push(t)};
    add(cleanArmoryText(source));
    add(source.replace(/<[^>]+>/g,' ').replace(/&nbsp;|&#160;/gi,' ').replace(/&amp;/gi,'&'));
    add(source);
    const labels={crit:String.raw`Critical\s+Strike(?:\s+Chance)?`,haste:'Haste',mastery:'Mastery',versatility:'Versatility',leech:'Leech',avoidance:'Avoidance',speed:String.raw`(?:Movement\s+Speed|Speed)`};
    for(const text of variants){
      if(!text)return null;
      const lower=text.toLowerCase();
      let start=lower.indexOf('## stats');
      if(start<0)start=lower.search(/(?:^|\s)stats(?:\s|$)/i);
      let window=text;
      if(start>=0){
        const ends=['## specializations & talents','specializations & talents','## mythic keystone dungeons','mythic keystone dungeons','raid progression'];
        let end=Math.min(text.length,start+3200);
        for(const token of ends){const i=lower.indexOf(token,start+5);if(i>=0&&i<end)end=i}
        window=text.slice(start,end);
      }else{
        // Falls die Überschrift durch ein Rendering-/Proxy-Format fehlt, reicht eine
        // vollständige vierteilige Wertefolge als sicherer Fallback.
        const health=lower.indexOf('health');
        if(health>=0)window=text.slice(Math.max(0,health-180),Math.min(text.length,health+3000));
      }
      const stats=parseWindowStats(window,labels) || parseAnywhereStats(window,labels);
      if(completeStats(stats))return{stats,method:'WoWArmory.gg · Stats-Block · Parser v10'};
    }
    return null;
  }

  async function timedStatFetch(url,options={},ms=8000,read='text'){
    const controller=new AbortController(),timer=setTimeout(()=>controller.abort(),ms);
    try{
      const response=await fetch(url,{...options,signal:controller.signal});
      if(!response.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${response.status}`);
      return await response[read]();
    }catch(e){
      if(e?.name==='AbortError')throw new Error('Zeitüberschreitung beim Abruf');
      if(e instanceof TypeError)throw new Error('Verbindung vom Browser nicht lesbar (Netzwerk, CORS oder Dienst nicht erreichbar)');
      throw e;
    }finally{clearTimeout(timer)}
  }
  async function browserStatsFallback(wowPages=wowArmoryCharacterUrls(),blizzardPages=blizzardCharacterUrls(),isCurrent=()=>true){
    const errors=[];window.__browserStatErrors=errors;
    const pages=[...wowPages.map(p=>({...p,label:'WoWArmory',parse:parseWowArmoryStatsText})),
      ...blizzardPages.map(p=>({...p,label:'Blizzard',parse:parseBlizzardStatsText}))];
    const queue=[],seen=new Set();
    const add=(p,url,route)=>{if(!seen.has(url)){seen.add(url);queue.push({...p,url,source:`${p.label} · ${p.identity.realm} · ${route}`})}};
    // Try both providers before spending time on additional proxy variants.
    for(const p of pages)add(p,'https://r.jina.ai/'+p.url,'Reader');
    for(const p of pages)add(p,p.url,'Direktabruf');
    for(const p of pages)add(p,'https://corsproxy.io/?url='+encodeURIComponent(p.url),'CORS-Fallback');
    let next=0,result=null;const deadline=Date.now()+45000;
    async function worker(){
      while(!result&&isCurrent()&&next<queue.length&&Date.now()<deadline){
        const target=queue[next++];
        try{
          let raw=await timedStatFetch(target.url,{cache:'no-store',mode:'cors',credentials:'omit',referrerPolicy:'no-referrer'},Math.min(8000,Math.max(1,deadline-Date.now())));
          if(!isCurrent()||result)return;
          try{const j=JSON.parse(raw);raw=j.body??j.contents??j.data??raw}catch{}
          const parsed=target.parse(raw);
          if(!parsed||!completeStats(parsed.stats))throw new Error('Antwort empfangen, aber kein eindeutiger vollständiger Prozentwerteblock gefunden');
          result={stats:parsed.stats,sourceLabel:`Automatisch · ${target.source} · ${parsed.method}`,confidence:'mittel',verifiedBy:1};
        }catch(e){if(isCurrent()&&!result)errors.push(`${target.source}: ${e.message||e}`)}
      }
    }
    await Promise.all([worker(),worker(),worker()]);
    if(isCurrent()&&!result&&next<queue.length)errors.push('Gesamtzeit von 45 Sekunden erreicht; weitere Abrufwege wurden nicht versucht.');
    return isCurrent()?result:null;
  }

  function webApiBase(){return '';}
  let localHelperBaseCache;
  function localHelperCandidates(){ const base=webApiBase(); return base?[base,'']:['']; }
  async function resolveLocalHelperBase(force=false){
    if(!force&&localHelperBaseCache!==undefined){
      if(localHelperBaseCache!==null)return localHelperBaseCache;
      throw new Error('Lokaler Helfer nicht erreichbar');
    }
    const errors=[];
    for(const base of localHelperCandidates()){
      try{
        const r=await timedStatFetch(`${base}/api/health?ts=${Date.now()}`,{cache:'no-store',mode:base?'cors':'same-origin',credentials:'omit'},2200,'json');
        if(r?.ok&&r?.service==='wow-bis-dashboard-helper'){localHelperBaseCache=base;return base}
        errors.push(`${base||'gleicher Ursprung'}: unerwartete Antwort`);
      }catch(e){errors.push(`${base||'gleicher Ursprung'}: ${e.message||e}`)}
    }
    localHelperBaseCache=null;
    const err=new Error('Web-API nicht erreichbar. Bitte die Serverkonfiguration von waschi.fun prüfen.');
    err.details=errors;
    throw err;
  }
  async function refreshBlizzardApiStatus(){
    const el=$('blizzardApiStatus');if(!el)return;
    try{
      const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(true);
      const r=await timedStatFetch(`${base}/api/blizzard-config-status?ts=${Date.now()}`,{cache:'no-store',mode:base?'cors':'same-origin',credentials:'omit'},3000,'json');
      const direct=true;
      if(r.configured){el.className='api-message ok';el.textContent=`✓ Web-API verbunden · Blizzard-API serverseitig eingerichtet. Lebensraub, Vermeidung und Geschwindigkeit werden über Blizzard Character Statistics geladen.`;if($('blizzardApiSetup'))$('blizzardApiSetup').open=false}
      else{el.className='api-message warning';el.textContent='✓ Web-API verbunden · Blizzard-API ist auf dem Server noch nicht eingerichtet. Setze BLIZZARD_CLIENT_ID und BLIZZARD_CLIENT_SECRET in der Hosting-Umgebung.';if($('blizzardApiSetup'))$('blizzardApiSetup').open=false}
      return Boolean(r.configured);
    }catch(e){
      el.className='api-message error';
      el.textContent='Web-API von waschi.fun ist nicht erreichbar. Bitte Server und Umgebungsvariablen prüfen.';
      if($('blizzardApiSetup'))$('blizzardApiSetup').open=true;
      return false;
    }
  }
  async function saveBlizzardApiConfig(){
    const id=$('blizzardClientId').value.trim(),secret=$('blizzardClientSecret').value.trim(),el=$('blizzardApiStatus');
    if(!id||!secret){el.className='api-message warning';el.textContent='Bitte Battle.net API Client ID und Client Secret vollständig eintragen. Das ist kein normaler Battle.net-Account-Login.';return}
    el.className='api-message';el.textContent='Lokaler Helfer wird geprüft …';
    try{
      const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(true);
      el.textContent='Blizzard-API-Zugang wird lokal gespeichert und bei Battle.net geprüft …';
      const r=await timedStatFetch(`${base}/api/blizzard-config`,{method:'POST',cache:'no-store',mode:base?'cors':'same-origin',credentials:'omit',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({client_id:id,client_secret:secret})},20000,'json');
      if(!r.ok)throw new Error(r.detail||r.error||'Zugang konnte nicht gespeichert werden');
      $('blizzardClientSecret').value='';el.className='api-message ok';el.textContent='✓ Blizzard-API eingerichtet und Zugang erfolgreich geprüft.';if($('blizzardApiSetup'))$('blizzardApiSetup').open=false;
      if(liveCharacter)await loadStats(true);
    }catch(e){
      el.className='api-message error';
      const msg=String(e?.message||e||'Unbekannter Fehler');
      if(msg.includes('Lokaler Helfer nicht erreichbar'))el.textContent='Blizzard-API konnte nicht eingerichtet werden: Der lokale Helfer läuft nicht oder wurde vom Browser blockiert. Bitte ausschließlich die ZIP-Version verwenden, STARTEN.bat starten und danach http://127.0.0.1:8765/ öffnen.';
      else el.textContent=`Blizzard-API konnte nicht eingerichtet werden: ${msg}`;
    }
  }
  async function clearBlizzardApiConfig(){
    const el=$('blizzardApiStatus');
    try{
      const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(true);
      const r=await timedStatFetch(`${base}/api/blizzard-config`,{method:'POST',cache:'no-store',mode:base?'cors':'same-origin',credentials:'omit',headers:{'Content-Type':'application/json'},body:JSON.stringify({action:'clear'})},5000,'json');
      if(!r.ok)throw new Error(r.detail||r.error||'Löschen fehlgeschlagen');
      $('blizzardClientId').value='';$('blizzardClientSecret').value='';el.className='api-message warning';el.textContent='Blizzard-API-Zugang wurde vom lokalen Helfer entfernt.';if($('blizzardApiSetup'))$('blizzardApiSetup').open=true;
    }catch(e){el.className='api-message error';el.textContent=`Zugang konnte nicht entfernt werden: ${e.message||e}`}
  }

  function activeTalentRankByName(name){
    const wanted=normal(name);
    const roots=[liveCharacter?.talentLoadout,liveCharacter?.talents_categorized,liveCharacter?.talents].filter(Boolean);
    let best=0;
    const visit=v=>{
      if(!v)return;
      if(Array.isArray(v)){for(const x of v)visit(x);return}
      if(typeof v!=='object')return;
      const entries=Array.isArray(v?.node?.entries)?v.node.entries:[];
      const selected=entries[Number(v?.entryIndex||0)]||entries[0];
      const spellName=selected?.spell?.name||'';
      if(normal(spellName)===wanted)best=Math.max(best,Number(v?.rank||0)||0);
      for(const x of Object.values(v))visit(x);
    };
    for(const root of roots)visit(root);
    return best;
  }
  function applyActiveTalentStatBonuses(stats,statSources={}){
    const out={...(stats||{})},sources={...(statSources||{})};
    const cls=normal(liveCharacter?.class||''),spec=normal(liveCharacter?.active_spec_name||'');
    // Lycaras Lehren wirkt formabhaengig. Fuer die aktive Spezialisierung wird die
    // Form verwendet, in der diese Spezialisierung regulär gespielt wird:
    // Guardian=Bär/Vielseitigkeit, Feral=Katze/Krit, Balance=Moonkin/Meisterschaft,
    // Restoration/keine Form=Tempo. Pro Rang gewährt das Talent 3 Prozentpunkte.
    if(cls==='druid'||cls==='druide'){
      const rank=activeTalentRankByName("Lycara's Teachings")||activeTalentRankByName('Lycaras Lehren');
      if(rank>0){
        const key=spec==='guardian'||spec==='wächter'||spec==='wachter'?'versatility':
          spec==='feral'||spec==='wildheit'?'crit':
          spec==='balance'||spec==='gleichgewicht'?'mastery':
          spec==='restoration'||spec==='wiederherstellung'?'haste':'';
        if(key&&Number.isFinite(statNumber(out[key]))){
          const bonus=3*rank;
          out[key]=Number(out[key])+bonus;
          sources[key]=`${sources[key]||'Blizzard Character Statistics'} + Lycaras Lehren Rang ${rank} (+${bonus} %)`;
        }
      }
    }
    return{stats:out,statSources:sources};
  }

  async function loadStats(force=false){
    if(!liveCharacter)return false;
    const thisLoad=++statsLoadSerial;
    const expectedIdentity=loadedCharacterIdentity||resolvedCharacterIdentity(liveCharacter);
    // Alle Kandidaten werden zu Beginn eingefroren. So kann ein späterer Charakterwechsel
    // keine URLs oder Realm-Slugs mitten in einer laufenden Abfrage austauschen.
    const identities=statIdentityCandidates().map(x=>({...x}));
    const wowPages=wowArmoryCharacterUrls().map(x=>({...x,identity:{...x.identity}}));
    const blizzardPages=blizzardCharacterUrls().map(x=>({...x,identity:{...x.identity}}));
    const isCurrent=()=>thisLoad===statsLoadSerial&&expectedIdentity===loadedCharacterIdentity&&Boolean(liveCharacter);
    window.__lastStatReadError='';
    statsReadStatus='loading';renderStatComparison();
    let local='';
    const serverErrors=[];
    try{const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(false);local=`${base}/api/character-stats`}catch(e){serverErrors.push(`Lokaler Helfer: ${e?.message||e}`)}
    for(const id of identities){
      if(!local)break;
      if(!isCurrent())return false;
      try{
        const u=new URL(local,location.href);u.searchParams.set('region',id.region);u.searchParams.set('realm',id.realm);u.searchParams.set('name',id.name);u.searchParams.set('identitySource',id.source);u.searchParams.set('class',String(liveCharacter?.class||''));u.searchParams.set('spec',String(liveCharacter?.active_spec_name||activeProfile?.spec||''));u.searchParams.set('_ts',String(Date.now()));
        const d=await timedStatFetch(u,{cache:'no-store',mode:u.origin===location.origin?'same-origin':'cors',credentials:'omit'},12000,'json');if(!isCurrent())return false;
        if(d.ok&&completeStats(d.stats)){
          if(!isCurrent())return false;
          const verify=d.verifiedBy>1?`${d.verifiedBy} Quellen verifiziert`:(d.confidence==='hoch'?'direkter Armory-Block':'eine Quelle');
          const adjusted=applyActiveTalentStatBonuses(d.stats,d.statSources||{});
          characterStats={...adjusted.stats,statSources:adjusted.statSources,sourceLabel:`Automatisch · ${d.method||d.source||'Charakterwerte'} + aktive Talentboni · ${verify} · ${id.source}`,confidence:d.confidence||'mittel',verifiedBy:d.verifiedBy||1,equipmentEnhancements:d.equipmentEnhancements||null,equipmentDetails:d.equipmentDetails||null,characterIdentity:expectedIdentity};
          statsReadStatus='success';window.__lastStatReadError='';renderStatComparison();return true;
        }
        serverErrors.push(`${id.source} (${id.realm}): ${d?.detail||d?.error||'Antwort enthält keine vollständigen Werte'}`);
      }catch(e){if(!isCurrent())return false;serverErrors.push(`Werte-Server: ${e?.message||e}`);break}
    }
    if(!isCurrent())return false;
    window.__lastStatReadError=serverErrors.length?serverErrors.slice(-4).join(' | '):'Lokaler Werte-Server nicht erreichbar';
    const browser=await browserStatsFallback(wowPages,blizzardPages,isCurrent);
    if(!isCurrent())return false;
    if(browser&&completeStats(browser.stats)){
      const fallbackSources=Object.fromEntries(['crit','haste','mastery','versatility','leech','avoidance','speed'].filter(k=>Number.isFinite(statNumber(browser.stats?.[k]))).map(k=>[k,'Automatisch · Armory-Fallback']));
      const adjusted=applyActiveTalentStatBonuses(browser.stats,fallbackSources);
      characterStats={...adjusted.stats,statSources:adjusted.statSources,sourceLabel:`${browser.sourceLabel} + aktive Talentboni`,confidence:'niedrig',verifiedBy:1,characterIdentity:expectedIdentity};statsReadStatus='success';window.__lastStatReadError='';renderStatComparison();return true
    }
    if(window.__browserStatErrors?.length)window.__lastStatReadError=[window.__lastStatReadError,...window.__browserStatErrors].filter(Boolean).join('\n');
    statsReadStatus='failed';
    if(characterStats&&isCurrent()){renderStatComparison();return false}
    if(manualKey()){
      try{
        const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(manualKey())||'null');
        if(saved&&['crit','haste','mastery','versatility'].some(k=>Number.isFinite(statNumber(saved[k])))&&isCurrent()){characterStats={...saved,characterIdentity:expectedIdentity};renderStatComparison();return false}
      }catch{}
    }
    if(!isCurrent())return false;
    characterStats=null;renderStatComparison();return false;
  }


  function getSettings(){try{return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem('wow-bis-settings')||'{}')}catch{return{}}}function saveSettingsObject(s){localStorage.setItem('wow-bis-settings',JSON.stringify(s))}function cleanupLegacyMixedMplusCaches(){const flag='wow-bis-mplus-character-scope-v2-cleaned';try{if(localStorage.getItem(flag)==='1')return;for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&(k.startsWith('wow-bis-season-run-archive:')||k.startsWith('wow-bis-recent-keys:')))localStorage.removeItem(k)}localStorage.setItem(flag,'1')}catch{}}function cleanupRemovedRaidBisState(){try{const s=getSettings();if(Object.prototype.hasOwnProperty.call(s,'bisListMode')){delete s.bisListMode;saveSettingsObject(s)}for(let i=localStorage.length-1;i>=0;i--){const k=localStorage.key(i);if(k&&k.startsWith('wow-bis-overall-list:'))localStorage.removeItem(k)}}catch{}}function saveSettings(){const s={region:$('region').value,realm:$('realm').value.trim(),character:$('character').value.trim(),refreshHours:Number($('refreshHours').value),profileKey:activeProfile.key,planningProfileLocked:profilePlanningLocked};saveSettingsObject(s);return s}
  function loadSettings(){const s=getSettings();if(s.profileKey&&root.profiles.some(p=>p.key===s.profileKey))activeProfile=root.profiles.find(p=>p.key===s.profileKey);profilePlanningLocked=Boolean(s.planningProfileLocked);$('region').value=s.region||'eu';$('realm').value=s.realm||'';$('character').value=s.character||'';const saved=s.refreshHours??0.25;$('refreshHours').value=String(saved);if(!$('refreshHours').value)$('refreshHours').value='0.25';updateRefreshSummary();scheduleRefresh()}
  function refreshIntervalLabel(h){if(h<1)return `alle ${Math.max(1,Math.round(h*60))} Minuten`;return h===1?'jede Stunde':`alle ${h} Stunden`}
  function updateRefreshSummary(){const h=Number($('refreshHours').value||0.25);$('refreshSummary').textContent=refreshIntervalLabel(h)}
  function scheduleRefresh(){if(refreshTimer)clearInterval(refreshTimer);const ms=Math.max(300000,Number($('refreshHours').value||0.25)*3600000);refreshTimer=setInterval(()=>refreshCharacter(false),ms)}
  const RIO_CHARACTER_FIELDS='gear,talents,talents:categorized,mythic_plus_scores_by_season:current,mythic_plus_recent_runs,mythic_plus_best_runs:all,mythic_plus_alternate_runs,mythic_plus_highest_level_runs,mythic_plus_weekly_highest_level_runs,mythic_plus_previous_weekly_highest_level_runs';
  async function fetchRioDirect(s){const u=new URL('https://raider.io/api/v1/characters/profile');u.searchParams.set('region',s.region);u.searchParams.set('realm',s.realm);u.searchParams.set('name',s.character);u.searchParams.set('fields',RIO_CHARACTER_FIELDS);const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);return await r.json()}
  function hasRioDungeonHistory(d){return Boolean(d&&(['mythic_plus_best_runs','mythic_plus_alternate_runs','mythic_plus_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_previous_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_dungeon_run_counts'].some(k=>Array.isArray(d[k]))))}
  function mergeRioCharacter(base,extra){if(!base)return extra;if(!extra)return base;const merged={...extra,...base};for(const k of ['mythic_plus_recent_runs','mythic_plus_best_runs','mythic_plus_alternate_runs','mythic_plus_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_previous_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_scores_by_season','mythic_plus_dungeon_run_counts'])if(Array.isArray(extra[k]))merged[k]=extra[k];if(base.gear)merged.gear=base.gear;return merged}
  async function fetchRio(s){
    const local=location.protocol==='file:'?'http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/raiderio':'/api/raiderio';let localData=null;
    try{const u=new URL(local,location.href);u.searchParams.set('region',s.region);u.searchParams.set('realm',s.realm);u.searchParams.set('name',s.character);const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});if(r.ok)localData=await r.json()}catch{}
    if(localData&&hasRioDungeonHistory(localData))return localData;
    try{return mergeRioCharacter(localData,await fetchRioDirect(s))}catch(e){if(localData)return localData;throw e}
  }
  async function fetchWarcraftLogs(s){
    const local=location.protocol==='file:'?'http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/warcraftlogs':'https://waschi-bis-tool.vercel.app/api/warcraftlogs';
    try{
      const u=new URL(local,location.href);
      u.searchParams.set('region',s.region);
      u.searchParams.set('realm',s.realm);
      u.searchParams.set('name',s.character);
      const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});
      if(!r.ok)return null;
      const d=await r.json();
      return d&&d.ok?d:null;
    }catch{return null}
  }
  async function refreshCharacter(showErrors=true){
    const s=saveSettings();
    if(!s.realm||!s.character){setMessage('Bitte Realm und Charaktername eintragen.','error');return}
    const requestSerial=++characterLoadSerial;
    const requestedIdentity=requestedCharacterIdentity(s);
    const switching=requestedIdentity!==loadedCharacterIdentity;

    // Alte Sekundärwert-Abfragen sofort entwerten. Das verhindert, dass ein später
    // eintreffendes Ergebnis des vorherigen Charakters den neuen Charakter überschreibt.
    resetCharacterDerivedState(switching?'Sekundärwerte werden für den neuen Charakter geladen …':'Sekundärwerte werden neu geladen …');
    if(switching){
      liveCharacter=null;
      loadedCharacterIdentity='';
      profilePlanningLocked=false;
  let statsReadStatus='idle';
      clearVisibleCharacterForSwitch();
      renderStatComparison();
    }
    setMessage('Raider.IO wird abgefragt …','');

    let fetchedCharacter;
    try{fetchedCharacter=await fetchRio(s)}catch(e){
      if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;
      if(showErrors)setMessage(`Raider.IO konnte nicht geladen werden (${e.message}).`,'error');
      return
    }
    if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;
    const rioIdentity=resolvedCharacterIdentity(fetchedCharacter,s);
    if(rioIdentity!==requestedIdentity){
      if(showErrors)setMessage('Die geladene Raider.IO-Antwort gehört nicht eindeutig zum aktuell ausgewählten Charakter. M+-Daten wurden aus Sicherheitsgründen verworfen.','error');
      return;
    }

    const warcraftLogs=await fetchWarcraftLogs(s);
    if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;
    const warcraftLogsIdentity=warcraftLogs?.identity?requestedCharacterIdentity(warcraftLogs.identity):'';
    if(warcraftLogs&&warcraftLogs.participationVerified===true&&warcraftLogsIdentity===requestedIdentity&&Array.isArray(warcraftLogs.runs)){
      fetchedCharacter.warcraft_logs_runs=warcraftLogs.runs;
      fetchedCharacter.warcraft_logs_meta={
        reportCount:warcraftLogs.reportCount||0,
        seasonReportCount:warcraftLogs.seasonReportCount||0,
        pagesRead:warcraftLogs.pagesRead||0,
        seasonZoneId:warcraftLogs.seasonZoneId||0,
        seasonZoneName:warcraftLogs.seasonZoneName||'',
        runCount:warcraftLogs.runCount??warcraftLogs.runs.length,
        timedCount:warcraftLogs.timedCount??0,
        outtimeCount:warcraftLogs.outtimeCount??0,
        source:warcraftLogs.source||'Warcraft Logs API v2',
        runDataTrusted:true,
        zoneRankings:warcraftLogs.zoneRankings||null
      };
    }else{
      fetchedCharacter.warcraft_logs_runs=[];
      fetchedCharacter.warcraft_logs_meta=null;
    }

    liveCharacter=fetchedCharacter;
    loadedCharacterIdentity=resolvedCharacterIdentity(fetchedCharacter,s)||requestedIdentity;
    characterStats=null;

    try{
      const p=findProfile();
      const keepPlanning=Boolean(profilePlanningLocked&&profileMatches(activeProfile));
      if(!keepPlanning&&p){profilePlanningLocked=false;setProfile(p.key,true)}
      else if(!profileMatches(activeProfile)&&p){profilePlanningLocked=false;setProfile(p.key,true)}
      if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;

      await ensureBackgroundRaidBis(activeProfile,false);
      if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;
      rebuildPersonalCraftOverrides();renderCharacter();renderRecentKeys();renderGear();renderComparison();
      const statsOk=await loadStats(false);
      if(requestSerial!==characterLoadSerial)return;

      const planning=planningProfileActive();
      const statSuffix=statsOk?' · Sekundärwerte automatisch geladen':(characterStats?.sourceLabel?.startsWith('Manuell')?' · gespeicherte manuelle Werte verwendet':' · automatische Sekundärwerte aktuell nicht verfügbar');
      if(planning)setMessage(`✓ ${liveCharacter.name||'Charakter'} geladen. Planungsprofil ${activeProfile.label} aktiv; Raider.IO meldet aktuell ${deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||liveCharacter.class)}. Die angelegte Ausrüstung wird trotzdem gegen das gewählte BiS-Profil verglichen${statSuffix}.`,'ok');
      else if(profileMatches())setMessage(`✓ ${activeProfile.label} erkannt und mit dem raidfreien BiS-Profil verglichen${statSuffix}.`,'ok');
      else setMessage(`Charakter geladen, aber das gewählte Profil gehört zu einer anderen Klasse. Das passende Profil wurde, soweit möglich, automatisch gewählt${statSuffix}.`,'warning');
      $('lastRefresh').textContent='Zuletzt aktualisiert: '+new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE',{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'}).format(new Date());
      saveSettings();
    }catch(e){
      console.error('Dashboard-Verarbeitung nach Raider.IO:',e);
      if(requestSerial===characterLoadSerial&&showErrors)setMessage(`Charakter wurde von Raider.IO geladen, aber die Dashboard-Verarbeitung ist fehlgeschlagen (${e.message}).`,'error')
    }
  }
  function currentScore(){const a=liveCharacter?.mythic_plus_scores_by_season;if(Array.isArray(a)&&a.length){const s=a[0].scores||{};return activeProfile.role==='tank'?(s.tank??s.all??'—'):(s.dps??s.all??'—')}return'—'}
  function renderCharacter(){if(!liveCharacter)return;$('characterCard').classList.remove('hidden');$('characterName').textContent=liveCharacter.name||$('character').value;$('characterMeta').textContent=[liveCharacter.realm,liveCharacter.region?.toUpperCase(),deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||liveCharacter.class)].filter(Boolean).join(' · ');$('characterIlvl').textContent=liveCharacter.gear?.item_level_equipped??liveCharacter.gear?.item_level??'—';$('characterScore').textContent=currentScore();comparisonCache=buildComparisons();const compatible=profileClassMatches(),planning=planningProfileActive();const visibleGear=visibleTargetGear(),m=compatible?visibleGear.filter(x=>isFulfilledBisStatus(comparisonCache.get(x.slot)?.status?.key)).length:0;$('characterMatches').textContent=compatible?`${m}/${visibleGear.length}`:'—';$('profileBadge').textContent=profileMatches()?`✓ BiS-Profil: ${activeProfile.label}`:(planning?`↻ Planungsprofil: ${activeProfile.label} · aktuell ${deutsch(liveCharacter.active_spec_name||'')}`:'Kein passendes Klassenprofil');$('rioLink').href=liveCharacter.profile_url||`https://raider.io/characters/${$('region').value}/${$('realm').value}/${$('character').value}`;const p=liveCharacter.thumbnail_url||'';if(p){$('characterPortrait').src=p;$('characterPortrait').style.visibility='visible'}else $('characterPortrait').style.visibility='hidden';updatePublicCharacterShare()}
  const RECENT_KEYS_STORE='wow-bis-recent-keys-v2:';
  const SEASON_RUN_ARCHIVE_STORE='wow-bis-season-run-archive-v2:';
  function currentSeasonSlug(){const a=liveCharacter?.mythic_plus_scores_by_season;return Array.isArray(a)&&a[0]?.season?String(a[0].season):'current'}
  function activeRunOwnerIdentity(){return loadedCharacterIdentity||resolvedCharacterIdentity(liveCharacter,{region:$('region')?.value,realm:$('realm')?.value,character:$('character')?.value})}
  function scopedRunStorageKey(prefix){return prefix+[normal(activeRunOwnerIdentity()),normal(currentSeasonSlug())].join(':')}
  function recentKeysStoreKey(){return scopedRunStorageKey(RECENT_KEYS_STORE)}
  function seasonRunArchiveKey(){return scopedRunStorageKey(SEASON_RUN_ARCHIVE_STORE)}
  function runIdentity(r){return String(r?.url||r?.run_url||r?.id||r?.run_id||[r?.completed_at,r?.dungeon,r?.short_name,r?.mythic_level,r?.clear_time_ms].join('|'))}
  function cleanRecentRun(r){const d=(r?.dungeon&&typeof r.dungeon==='object')?r.dungeon:null;return{source:r?.source||'Raider.IO',dungeon:d?.name||r?.dungeon||r?.short_name||'Unbekannter Dungeon',short_name:d?.short_name||r?.short_name||'',mythic_level:Number(r?.mythic_level??r?.level??0)||0,completed_at:r?.completed_at||'',clear_time_ms:Number(r?.clear_time_ms??r?.clearTimeMs??0)||0,par_time_ms:Number(r?.par_time_ms??r?.keystone_time_ms??r?.parTimeMs??0)||0,num_keystone_upgrades:Number(r?.num_keystone_upgrades??r?.num_chests??r?.keystone_upgrades??0)||0,score:Number(r?.score??r?.run_score??0)||0,url:r?.url||r?.run_url||'',id:r?.id??r?.run_id??r?.keystone_run_id??null,map_challenge_mode_id:Number(r?.map_challenge_mode_id??d?.map_challenge_mode_id??0)||0,zone_id:Number(r?.zone_id??d?.zone_id??0)||0,icon_url:r?.icon_url||d?.icon_url||''}}
  function readScopedRunStore(key){
    try{
      const raw=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(key)||'null');
      const identity=activeRunOwnerIdentity(),season=currentSeasonSlug();
      if(!raw||Array.isArray(raw)||raw.identity!==identity||String(raw.season)!==String(season)||!Array.isArray(raw.runs))return[];
      return raw.runs;
    }catch{return[]}
  }
  function writeScopedRunStore(key,runs,limit){
    try{localStorage.setItem(key,JSON.stringify({version:2,identity:activeRunOwnerIdentity(),season:currentSeasonSlug(),runs:runs.slice(0,limit),savedAt:new Date().toISOString()}))}catch{}
  }
  function getRecentKeysCache(){return readScopedRunStore(recentKeysStoreKey())}
  function saveRecentKeysCache(a){writeScopedRunStore(recentKeysStoreKey(),a,5)}
  function getSeasonRunArchive(){return readScopedRunStore(seasonRunArchiveKey())}
  function saveSeasonRunArchive(a){writeScopedRunStore(seasonRunArchiveKey(),a,2500)}
  function liveSeasonRunArrays(){return['mythic_plus_best_runs','mythic_plus_alternate_runs','mythic_plus_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_previous_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_recent_runs','warcraft_logs_runs']}
  function updateSeasonRunArchive(){
    if(!liveCharacter||!activeRunOwnerIdentity())return[];
    const seen=new Set(),merged=[];
    const add=raw=>{const r=cleanRecentRun(raw),id=runIdentity(r);if(!id||seen.has(id)||!r.dungeon||r.dungeon==='Unbekannter Dungeon'||r.mythic_level<=0)return;seen.add(id);merged.push(r)};
    for(const key of liveSeasonRunArrays())for(const raw of (Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.[key])?liveCharacter[key]:[]))add(raw);
    for(const raw of getSeasonRunArchive())add(raw);
    for(const raw of getRecentKeysCache())add(raw);
    merged.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.completed_at||0)-new Date(a.completed_at||0));
    saveSeasonRunArchive(merged);return merged;
  }
  function ingestRecentKeys(){
    const fresh=Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.mythic_plus_recent_runs)?liveCharacter.mythic_plus_recent_runs.map(cleanRecentRun):[];
    const cached=getRecentKeysCache().map(cleanRecentRun),seen=new Set(),merged=[];
    for(const r of [...fresh,...cached]){const id=runIdentity(r);if(!id||seen.has(id))continue;seen.add(id);merged.push(r)}
    merged.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.completed_at||0)-new Date(a.completed_at||0));
    const kept=merged.slice(0,5);saveRecentKeysCache(kept);return{runs:kept,freshCount:fresh.length};
  }
  function samePhysicalRun(a,b){
    if(!a||!b)return false;
    if(Number(a.mythic_level||0)!==Number(b.mythic_level||0))return false;
    const da=normal(a.dungeon||a.short_name||''),db=normal(b.dungeon||b.short_name||'');
    if(!da||!db||da!==db)return false;
    const ta=new Date(a.completed_at||0).getTime(),tb=new Date(b.completed_at||0).getTime();
    return Number.isFinite(ta)&&Number.isFinite(tb)&&Math.abs(ta-tb)<=180000;
  }
  function allKnownDungeonRuns(){
    const rows=[];
    const add=raw=>{
      const r=cleanRecentRun(raw);
      if(!r.dungeon||r.dungeon==='Unbekannter Dungeon'||r.mythic_level<=0)return;
      const duplicate=rows.find(x=>samePhysicalRun(x,r));
      if(duplicate){
        // Behalte die reichhaltigere Quelle; Warcraft Logs hat oft bessere Fight-Details,
        // Raider.IO teils Par-Zeit/Map-ID. Felder werden daher zusammengeführt.
        Object.assign(duplicate,{...r,...duplicate,
          par_time_ms:Number(duplicate.par_time_ms||r.par_time_ms||0),
          map_challenge_mode_id:Number(duplicate.map_challenge_mode_id||r.map_challenge_mode_id||0),
          zone_id:Number(duplicate.zone_id||r.zone_id||0),
          score:Number(duplicate.score||r.score||0)
        });
        return;
      }
      rows.push(r);
    };
    for(const key of ['warcraft_logs_runs','mythic_plus_all_known_runs','mythic_plus_best_runs','mythic_plus_alternate_runs','mythic_plus_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_previous_weekly_highest_level_runs','mythic_plus_recent_runs']){
      for(const raw of (Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.[key])?liveCharacter[key]:[]))add(raw);
    }
    for(const raw of getSeasonRunArchive())add(raw);
    rows.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.completed_at||0)-new Date(a.completed_at||0));
    return rows;
  }
  function characterDungeonRunCounts(){
    const rankings=liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_meta?.zoneRankings?.rankings;
    if(Array.isArray(rankings)&&rankings.length){
      return rankings.map(x=>({
        zone_id:Number(x?.encounter?.id||0)||0,
        dungeon:String(x?.encounter?.name||'Unbekannter Dungeon'),
        short_name:'',
        total:Math.max(0,Number(x?.totalKills||0)||0),
        timed:Math.max(0,Number(x?.totalKills||0)||0),
        best_level:Math.max(0,Number(x?.bestRank?.ilvl||0)||0),
        best_score:Number(x?.bestAmount||0)||0
      })).filter(x=>x.dungeon!=='Unbekannter Dungeon'&&x.total>0);
    }
    const raw=Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.mythic_plus_dungeon_run_counts)?liveCharacter.mythic_plus_dungeon_run_counts:[];
    return raw.map(x=>({zone_id:Number(x?.zone_id||0)||0,dungeon:String(x?.dungeon||x?.name||x?.short_name||'Unbekannter Dungeon'),short_name:String(x?.short_name||''),total:Math.max(0,Number(x?.season_runs_total??x?.total??0)||0),timed:Math.max(0,Number(x?.season_runs_timed??x?.timed??0)||0)})).filter(x=>x.dungeon!=='Unbekannter Dungeon'&&x.total>0);
  }
  function authoritativeRunTotals(){
    const rankings=liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_meta?.zoneRankings?.rankings;
    if(Array.isArray(rankings)&&rankings.length){
      const counts=characterDungeonRunCounts();
      const total=counts.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.total,0);
      return{source:'Warcraft Logs · Charakter-Zonenrangliste',counts,total,timed:total,outtime:0};
    }
    const merged=allKnownDungeonRuns();
    if(merged.length){
      const timed=merged.filter(runIsTimed).length;
      const hasWcl=merged.some(r=>String(r.source||'').toLowerCase().includes('warcraft'));
      const hasRio=merged.some(r=>!String(r.source||'').toLowerCase().includes('warcraft'));
      return{source:hasWcl&&hasRio?'Warcraft Logs + Raider.IO':hasWcl?'Warcraft Logs':'Raider.IO',counts:[],total:merged.length,timed,outtime:Math.max(0,merged.length-timed)};
    }
    const counts=characterDungeonRunCounts();
    if(!counts.length)return null;
    const total=counts.reduce((n,x)=>n+x.total,0),timed=counts.reduce((n,x)=>n+Math.min(x.total,x.timed),0);
    return{source:'Raider.IO',counts,total,timed,outtime:Math.max(0,total-timed)};
  }
  function dungeonIdentity(r){return r?.map_challenge_mode_id?`map:${r.map_challenge_mode_id}`:`name:${normal(r?.dungeon||r?.short_name||'')}`}
  function runIsTimed(r){const clear=Number(r?.clear_time_ms||0),par=Number(r?.par_time_ms||0),up=Number(r?.num_keystone_upgrades||0);return Boolean(up>0||(clear>0&&par>0&&clear<=par))}
  function betterSuccessfulDungeonRun(a,b){
    if(!a)return b;if(!b)return a;
    // Pro Dungeon zählt zuerst die höchste erfolgreich intime Key-Stufe.
    if(Number(b.mythic_level)!==Number(a.mythic_level))return Number(b.mythic_level)>Number(a.mythic_level)?b:a;
    // Bei gleicher Stufe gewinnt die höhere Raider.IO-Punktzahl.
    if(Number(b.score)!==Number(a.score))return Number(b.score)>Number(a.score)?b:a;
    // Danach der schnellere Lauf; zuletzt der jüngere Datensatz.
    const bc=Number(b.clear_time_ms||Infinity),ac=Number(a.clear_time_ms||Infinity);
    if(bc!==ac)return bc<ac?b:a;
    return new Date(b.completed_at||0)>new Date(a.completed_at||0)?b:a;
  }
  function successfulDungeonBestRuns(){
    const byDungeon=new Map();
    for(const r of allKnownDungeonRuns()){
      if(!runIsTimed(r))continue;
      const key=dungeonIdentity(r);if(!key||key==='name:')continue;
      byDungeon.set(key,betterSuccessfulDungeonRun(byDungeon.get(key),r));
    }
    return [...byDungeon.values()].sort((a,b)=>{
      const bs=Number(b.score||0),as=Number(a.score||0);
      if(bs!==as)return bs-as;
      if(Number(b.mythic_level)!==Number(a.mythic_level))return Number(b.mythic_level)-Number(a.mythic_level);
      return String(a.dungeon).localeCompare(String(b.dungeon),'de',{sensitivity:'base'});
    });
  }
  function renderDungeonProgress(){
    renderAllSeasonDungeons();
    const body=$('dungeonProgressBody'),count=$('mplusSuccessfulCount'),highestScore=$('mplusHighestScore'),highestKey=$('mplusHighestKey'),source=$('mplusDungeonSource'),info=$('mplusDungeonInfo');if(!body)return[];
    if(!liveCharacter){
      body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="8" class="muted">Noch kein Charakter geladen.</td></tr>';
      if(count)count.textContent='0';if(highestScore)highestScore.textContent='—';if(highestKey)highestKey.textContent='—';if(source)source.textContent='Raider.IO · noch kein Charakter geladen';
      return[];
    }
    const rows=successfulDungeonBestRuns();
    const maxScore=rows.reduce((m,r)=>Math.max(m,Number(r.score||0)),0),maxKey=rows.reduce((m,r)=>Math.max(m,Number(r.mythic_level||0)),0);
    if(count)count.textContent=String(rows.length);
    if(highestScore)highestScore.textContent=maxScore>0?fmt(maxScore):'—';
    if(highestKey)highestKey.textContent=maxKey>0?`+${maxKey}`:'—';
    if(source){const wcl=Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_runs)?liveCharacter.warcraft_logs_runs.length:0;source.textContent=`Raider.IO${wcl?` + Warcraft Logs (${wcl} geloggte Runs)`:''} · ${rows.length} erfolgreiche Dungeon${rows.length===1?'':'s'}`;}
    if(info){const wcl=Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_runs)?liveCharacter.warcraft_logs_runs.length:0;info.className='api-message mplus-board-note'+(rows.length?' ok':' warning');info.textContent=rows.length?`✓ ${rows.length} erfolgreiche Dungeon-Bestleistung${rows.length===1?'':'en'} für ${liveCharacter.name} erkannt. Raider.IO wird durch ${wcl} geloggte Warcraft-Logs-M+-Run${wcl===1?'':'s'} ergänzt. Pro Dungeon wird der höchste intime Key berücksichtigt.`:'Für diesen Charakter wurden aktuell keine erfolgreich innerhalb des Zeitlimits abgeschlossenen M+-Runs aus Raider.IO oder Warcraft Logs erkannt.'}
    if(!rows.length){body.innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="8" class="muted">Keine erfolgreichen Mythic+-Dungeon-Abschlüsse im Zeitlimit gemeldet.</td></tr>';return rows}
    body.innerHTML=rows.map((r,i)=>{const url=runRioUrl(r),score=Number(r.score)>0?fmt(r.score):'—';return`<tr><td><span class="mplus-rank">${i+1}</span></td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.dungeon)}</strong>${r.short_name?`<div class="small">${escapeHtml(r.short_name)}</div>`:''}</td><td><span class="dungeon-max-key">+${escapeHtml(r.mythic_level)}</span></td><td><span class="mplus-score">${escapeHtml(score)}</span></td><td><span class="key-result timed">${escapeHtml(runResult(r))}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDate(r.completed_at))}</td><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDuration(r.clear_time_ms))}</td><td>${url?`<a class="btn key-link" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Run öffnen ↗</a>`:'—'}</td></tr>`}).join('');
    return rows;
  }
  function allSeasonRunGroups(){
    const groups=new Map();
    for(const r of allKnownDungeonRuns()){
      const key=dungeonIdentity(r);if(!key||key==='name:')continue;
      if(!groups.has(key))groups.set(key,{dungeon:r.dungeon,short_name:r.short_name||'',runs:[]});
      groups.get(key).runs.push(r);
    }
    const rows=[...groups.values()];
    for(const g of rows)g.runs.sort((a,b)=>new Date(b.completed_at||0)-new Date(a.completed_at||0)||Number(b.mythic_level||0)-Number(a.mythic_level||0));
    rows.sort((a,b)=>String(a.dungeon).localeCompare(String(b.dungeon),'de',{sensitivity:'base'}));
    return rows;
  }
  function setAllSeasonRunGroupsOpen(open){document.querySelectorAll('#allSeasonRunsGroups details.mplus-run-group').forEach(d=>d.open=Boolean(open))}
  function dungeonPreferenceStats(groups){
    const exact=characterDungeonRunCounts();
    const source=exact.length?exact.map(c=>{
      const g=groups.find(x=>(c.zone_id&&x.runs.some(r=>Number(r.zone_id||0)===c.zone_id))||normal(x.dungeon)===normal(c.dungeon)||normal(x.short_name)===normal(c.short_name));
      return{dungeon:c.dungeon,short_name:c.short_name,runs:g?.runs||[],exactTotal:c.total,exactTimed:Math.min(c.total,c.timed)};
    }):groups.map(g=>({...g,exactTotal:g.runs.length,exactTimed:g.runs.filter(runIsTimed).length}));
    return source.map(g=>{
      const timed=g.exactTimed,total=g.exactTotal;
      const ratios=(g.runs||[]).map(r=>{const clear=Number(r?.clear_time_ms||0),par=Number(r?.par_time_ms||0);return clear>0&&par>0?clear/par:NaN}).filter(Number.isFinite);
      const avgRatio=ratios.length?ratios.reduce((a,b)=>a+b,0)/ratios.length:NaN;
      const bestRatio=ratios.length?Math.min(...ratios):NaN,worstRatio=ratios.length?Math.max(...ratios):NaN;
      return{...g,total,timed,outtime:Math.max(0,total-timed),avgRatio,bestRatio,worstRatio};
    });
  }
  function prefRatioText(v){if(!Number.isFinite(v))return'Zeit n/v';const pct=Math.round(v*100);return `Ø ${pct}% der Timerzeit`}
  function worstRatioText(v){if(!Number.isFinite(v))return'schlechteste Zeit n/v';const pct=Math.round(v*100);return `schlechtester Lauf ${pct}% der Timerzeit`}
  function popularDungeonRanking(stats){return[...stats].sort((a,b)=>b.total-a.total||b.timed-a.timed||((Number.isFinite(a.avgRatio)?a.avgRatio:Infinity)-(Number.isFinite(b.avgRatio)?b.avgRatio:Infinity))||((Number.isFinite(a.bestRatio)?a.bestRatio:Infinity)-(Number.isFinite(b.bestRatio)?b.bestRatio:Infinity))||String(a.dungeon).localeCompare(String(b.dungeon),'de',{sensitivity:'base'}))}
  function hatedDungeonRanking(stats){return[...stats].sort((a,b)=>a.total-b.total||a.timed-b.timed||((Number.isFinite(b.worstRatio)?b.worstRatio:-Infinity)-(Number.isFinite(a.worstRatio)?a.worstRatio:-Infinity))||((Number.isFinite(b.avgRatio)?b.avgRatio:-Infinity)-(Number.isFinite(a.avgRatio)?a.avgRatio:-Infinity))||String(a.dungeon).localeCompare(String(b.dungeon),'de',{sensitivity:'base'}))}
  function renderPreferenceRanking(targetId,rows,type){const el=$(targetId);if(!el)return;el.innerHTML=rows.slice(0,3).map((g,i)=>{const time=type==='popular'?prefRatioText(g.avgRatio):worstRatioText(g.worstRatio);return`<div class="mplus-pref-rank-row"><span class="mplus-pref-rank-no">${i+1}</span><span class="mplus-pref-rank-name">${escapeHtml(g.dungeon)}</span><span class="mplus-pref-rank-meta">${g.total} Runs · ${g.timed} intime · ${escapeHtml(time)}</span></div>`}).join('')}
  function renderDungeonPreferences(groups){
    const fav=$('mplusFavoriteDungeon'),favMeta=$('mplusFavoriteDungeonMeta'),hate=$('mplusHatedDungeon'),hateMeta=$('mplusHatedDungeonMeta');
    const stats=dungeonPreferenceStats(groups);
    if(!stats.length){if(fav)fav.textContent='—';if(favMeta)favMeta.textContent='Noch keine Saison-Runs ausgewertet.';if(hate)hate.textContent='—';if(hateMeta)hateMeta.textContent='Noch keine Saison-Runs ausgewertet.';renderPreferenceRanking('mplusFavoriteRanking',[],'popular');renderPreferenceRanking('mplusHatedRanking',[],'hated');return}
    const popular=popularDungeonRanking(stats),hated=hatedDungeonRanking(stats),p=popular[0],h=hated[0];
    if(fav)fav.textContent=p.dungeon;if(favMeta)favMeta.textContent=`${p.total} Runs · ${p.timed} intime · ${p.outtime} outtime · ${prefRatioText(p.avgRatio)}`;
    if(hate)hate.textContent=h.dungeon;if(hateMeta)hateMeta.textContent=`${h.total} Runs · ${h.timed} intime · ${h.outtime} outtime · ${worstRatioText(h.worstRatio)}`;
    renderPreferenceRanking('mplusFavoriteRanking',popular,'popular');renderPreferenceRanking('mplusHatedRanking',hated,'hated');
  }
  function renderAllSeasonDungeons(){
    const host=$('allSeasonRunsGroups'),count=$('mplusAllDungeonCount'),info=$('mplusAllDungeonInfo'),totalEl=$('mplusSeasonTotalRuns'),timedEl=$('mplusSeasonTimedRuns'),outtimeEl=$('mplusSeasonOuttimeRuns');if(!host)return[];
    if(!liveCharacter){
      host.innerHTML='<div class="muted">Noch kein Charakter geladen.</div>';
      if(count)count.textContent='0 Runs';
      if(totalEl)totalEl.textContent='0';if(timedEl)timedEl.textContent='0';if(outtimeEl)outtimeEl.textContent='0';
      renderDungeonPreferences([]);
      if(info){info.className='api-message mplus-board-note';info.textContent='Hier wird jeder einzelne für diesen Charakter erfasste Mythic+-Run der aktuellen Saison angezeigt – Intime und Outtime.'}
      return[];
    }
    const groups=allSeasonRunGroups(),exactTotals=authoritativeRunTotals(),detailRuns=groups.reduce((n,g)=>n+g.runs.length,0),totalRuns=exactTotals?.total??detailRuns,timedRuns=exactTotals?.timed??groups.reduce((n,g)=>n+g.runs.filter(runIsTimed).length,0),outRuns=exactTotals?.outtime??Math.max(0,totalRuns-timedRuns);
    if(count)count.textContent=`${totalRuns} Run${totalRuns===1?'':'s'}`;
    if(totalEl)totalEl.textContent=String(totalRuns);if(timedEl)timedEl.textContent=String(timedRuns);if(outtimeEl)outtimeEl.textContent=String(outRuns);
    renderDungeonPreferences(groups);
    if(info){const exactNote=exactTotals?.source==='Warcraft Logs'?` Die Saisonanzahl stammt direkt aus Warcraft Logs (${escapeHtml(liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_meta?.seasonZoneName||'aktuelle M+-Saison')}).`:exactTotals?.source==='Raider.IO'?` Warcraft Logs ist nicht verbunden; die Zahl ist daher nur eine Raider.IO-Näherung.`:` Warcraft Logs ist nicht verbunden; eine vollständige Saisonanzahl kann nicht bestätigt werden.`;info.className='api-message mplus-board-note'+(totalRuns?' ok':' warning');info.textContent=totalRuns?`✓ ${totalRuns} Mythic+-Runs für den aktuell geladenen Charakter ${liveCharacter.name}: ${timedRuns} intime · ${outRuns} outtime/abgeschlossen.${exactNote}`:'Für diesen Charakter wurden aktuell keine M+-Runs der laufenden Saison erkannt.'}
    if(!totalRuns){host.innerHTML='<div class="muted">Keine Mythic+-Runs der aktuellen Saison erfasst.</div>';return groups}
    host.innerHTML=groups.map(g=>{
      const highest=Math.max(...g.runs.map(r=>Number(r.mythic_level||0))),wclActive=Array.isArray(liveCharacter?.warcraft_logs_runs)&&liveCharacter.warcraft_logs_runs.length>0,exactCount=wclActive?null:characterDungeonRunCounts().find(c=>(c.zone_id&&g.runs.some(r=>Number(r.zone_id||0)===c.zone_id))||normal(c.dungeon)===normal(g.dungeon)||normal(c.short_name)===normal(g.short_name)),groupTotal=exactCount?.total??g.runs.length,timed=exactCount?.timed??g.runs.filter(runIsTimed).length,outtime=Math.max(0,groupTotal-timed);
      const rows=g.runs.map((r,i)=>{const url=runRioUrl(r),score=Number(r.score)>0?fmt(r.score):'—',cls=runResultClass(r);return`<tr><td class="mplus-run-index">${i+1}</td><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDate(r.completed_at))}</td><td><span class="key-level">+${escapeHtml(r.mythic_level||'?')}</span></td><td><span class="key-result ${cls}">${escapeHtml(runResult(r))}</span></td><td><span class="mplus-score">${escapeHtml(score)}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDuration(r.clear_time_ms))}</td><td>${url?`<a class="btn key-link" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Run öffnen ↗</a>`:'—'}</td></tr>`}).join('');
      return`<details class="mplus-run-group"><summary><span class="mplus-run-group-title"><strong>${escapeHtml(g.dungeon)}</strong>${g.short_name?`<span class="small">${escapeHtml(g.short_name)}</span>`:''}</span><span class="mplus-run-count">${groupTotal} Run${groupTotal===1?'':'s'}</span><span class="mplus-run-meta">Höchster +${highest}</span><span class="mplus-run-meta">${timed} intime · ${outtime} outtime</span></summary><div class="table-wrap"><table class="dungeon-progress-table mplus-run-table"><thead><tr><th>#</th><th>Datum</th><th>Key</th><th>Ergebnis</th><th>Score</th><th>Dauer</th><th>Quelle</th></tr></thead><tbody>${rows}</tbody></table></div></details>`;
    }).join('');
    return groups;
  }

  function formatRunDate(v){if(!v)return'—';const d=new Date(v);if(Number.isNaN(d.getTime()))return'—';return new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE',{day:'2-digit',month:'2-digit',year:'2-digit',hour:'2-digit',minute:'2-digit'}).format(d)}
  function formatRunDuration(ms){const n=Number(ms);if(!Number.isFinite(n)||n<=0)return'—';let s=Math.round(n/1000),h=Math.floor(s/3600);s%=3600;const m=Math.floor(s/60),sec=s%60;return h?`${h}:${String(m).padStart(2,'0')}:${String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}`:`${m}:${String(sec).padStart(2,'0')}`}
  function runResult(r){const clear=Number(r?.clear_time_ms||0),par=Number(r?.par_time_ms||0),up=Number(r?.num_keystone_upgrades||0);if(clear&&par)return clear<=par?`Im Zeitlimit${up>0?` · +${up}`:''}`:'Über Zeit';if(up>0)return`Im Zeitlimit · +${up}`;return'Abgeschlossen'}
  function runResultClass(r){const clear=Number(r?.clear_time_ms||0),par=Number(r?.par_time_ms||0),up=Number(r?.num_keystone_upgrades||0);return(clear&&par&&clear>par)?'depleted':(up>0||(clear&&par&&clear<=par))?'timed':'complete'}
  function runRioUrl(r){const raw=String(r?.url||'').trim();if(raw.startsWith('http://')||raw.startsWith('https://'))return raw;if(raw.startsWith('/'))return'https://raider.io'+raw;if(r?.id)return`https://raider.io/mythic-plus-runs/${encodeURIComponent(r.id)}`;return''}
  function renderRecentKeys(){
    if(!$('recentKeysBody'))return;
    renderDungeonProgress();
    if(!liveCharacter){$('recentKeysBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" class="muted">Noch kein Charakter geladen.</td></tr>';$('recentKeysSource').textContent='Raider.IO · noch kein Charakter geladen';return}
    const {runs,freshCount}=ingestRecentKeys();
    $('recentKeysSource').textContent=`Raider.IO · ${runs.length}/5 letzte Keys`;
    $('recentKeysInfo').className='api-message'+(runs.length?' ok':' warning');
    if(runs.length)$('recentKeysInfo').textContent=`✓ ${runs.length} zuletzt bekannte${runs.length===1?'r':''} Mythic+-Run${runs.length===1?'':'s'} für ${liveCharacter.name}. Die erfolgreiche Bestliste pro Dungeon findest du in Tafel VII.`;
    else if(freshCount)$('recentKeysInfo').textContent=`Raider.IO hat ${freshCount} aktuelle Run${freshCount===1?'':'s'} geliefert.`;
    else $('recentKeysInfo').textContent='Raider.IO meldet für diesen Charakter aktuell keine jüngsten Mythic+-Runs.';
    if(!runs.length){$('recentKeysBody').innerHTML='<tr><td colspan="7" class="muted">Keine aktuellen Mythic+-Runs von Raider.IO gemeldet.</td></tr>';return}
    $('recentKeysBody').innerHTML=runs.map(r=>{const url=runRioUrl(r),score=Number(r.score)>0?fmt(r.score):'—';return`<tr><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDate(r.completed_at))}</td><td><strong>${escapeHtml(r.dungeon)}</strong>${r.short_name?`<div class="small">${escapeHtml(r.short_name)}</div>`:''}</td><td><span class="key-level">+${escapeHtml(r.mythic_level||'?')}</span></td><td><span class="key-result ${runResultClass(r)}">${escapeHtml(runResult(r))}</span></td><td>${escapeHtml(formatRunDuration(r.clear_time_ms))}</td><td>${escapeHtml(score)}</td><td>${url?`<a class="btn key-link" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">Run öffnen ↗</a>`:'—'}</td></tr>`}).join('');
  }
  function setMessage(t,c){$('apiMessage').textContent=t;$('apiMessage').className='api-message'+(c?' '+c:'')}

  const CHAR_FAV='wow-bis-character-favorites';
  function getCharFavs(){try{const a=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(CHAR_FAV)||'[]');return Array.isArray(a)?a:[]}catch{return[]}}
  function saveCharFavs(a){localStorage.setItem(CHAR_FAV,JSON.stringify(a.slice(0,50)))}
  function renderCharFavs(){const a=getCharFavs();$('characterFavorites').innerHTML='<option value="">Favorit auswählen …</option>'+a.map((x,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${escapeHtml(x.character)} · ${escapeHtml(x.realm)} · ${x.region.toUpperCase()}</option>`).join('')}
  function saveCurrentChar(){const x={region:$('region').value,realm:$('realm').value.trim(),character:$('character').value.trim()};if(!x.realm||!x.character){setMessage('Bitte zuerst Realm und Charaktername eintragen.','warning');return}const a=getCharFavs().filter(v=>!(normal(v.region)===normal(x.region)&&normal(v.realm)===normal(x.realm)&&normal(v.character)===normal(x.character)));a.unshift(x);saveCharFavs(a);renderCharFavs();$('characterFavorites').value='0';setMessage(`★ ${x.character} wurde als Favorit gespeichert.`,'ok')}
  function loadCharFav(){const i=Number($('characterFavorites').value),a=getCharFavs();if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||!a[i])return;const x=a[i];$('region').value=x.region;$('realm').value=x.realm;$('character').value=x.character;refreshCharacter(true)}
  function deleteCharFav(){const i=Number($('characterFavorites').value),a=getCharFavs();if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||!a[i])return;const x=a.splice(i,1)[0];saveCharFavs(a);renderCharFavs();setMessage(`Favorit ${x.character} gelöscht.`,'ok')}

  const BIS_IMPORT_ORDER=['head','neck','shoulder','back','chest','wrist','hands','waist','legs','feet','finger1','finger2','trinket1','trinket2','mainhand','offhand'];
  const BIS_CATALYST_ORDER=['head','shoulder','chest','hands','legs'];
  function bundledBisPayload(profile,diagnostics=[]){
    const gear=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(BUNDLED_BIS_GEAR.get(profile.key)||[]));
    const items=[];
    for(const slot of BIS_IMPORT_ORDER){
      const item=gear.find(x=>x.slot===slot);if(!item)continue;
      if(item.catalyst){
        const tierId=Number(item.tierItemId||(item.acceptItemIds||[])[0]||item.itemId)||0;
        if(tierId)items.push({tier:3,itemId:tierId,name:item.tierName||item.name||`Gegenstand #${tierId}`});
      }else{
        const id=Number(item.itemId)||0;if(!id)continue;
        const entry={tier:3,itemId:id,name:item.name||`Gegenstand #${id}`};
        if(item.sourceType==='Hergestellt')entry.bonusIds=[8960];
        items.push(entry);
      }
    }
    for(const slot of BIS_CATALYST_ORDER){
      const item=gear.find(x=>x.slot===slot&&x.catalyst);const base=item?catalystBaseFromItem(item):null;const id=Number(base?.itemId||item?.itemId)||0;
      if(id)items.push({tier:5,itemId:id,name:base?.name||item?.name||`Gegenstand #${id}`});
    }
    if(!items.length)throw new Error('Auch der integrierte BiS-Snapshot ist leer');
    return{ok:true,items,source:'Integrierter BiS-Snapshot',sourceUrl:profile?.bisUpdate?.url||'',fetchedAt:BUNDLED_BIS_SNAPSHOT_DATE+'T12:00:00+02:00',snapshot:true,diagnostics};
  }
  const BIS_STORE='wow-bis-online-update:';
  function updateItemName(entry,currentItem=null){if(entry?.name)return entry.name;const id=Number(entry?.itemId)||0;if(currentItem){if(id&&id===Number(currentItem.itemId)&&currentItem.name)return currentItem.name;if(id&&id===Number(currentItem.tierItemId)&&currentItem.tierName)return currentItem.tierName}return `Gegenstand #${entry?.itemId||'?'}`}
  function isCraftEntry(entry){return Array.isArray(entry?.bonusIds)&&entry.bonusIds.map(Number).includes(8960)}
  function raidBackgroundFromPayload(profile,payload){
    const entries=(payload?.items||[]).filter(x=>x&&Number(x.itemId));const template=JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(profile.gear||[]));if(!template.length)return[];
    const explicit=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&x.slot&&BIS_IMPORT_ORDER.includes(x.slot));const explicitSlots=new Set(explicit.map(x=>x.slot));let regular=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&!x.slot);
    const slots=template.map(x=>x.slot).filter(slot=>!explicitSlots.has(slot));if(profile?.bisUpdate?.duplicateWeaponForOffhand&&template.some(x=>x.slot==='offhand')&&regular.length===slots.length-1&&regular.length)regular=[...regular,{...regular[regular.length-1]}];
    if(regular.length<slots.length)throw new Error(`Raid-BiS-Liste unvollständig (${regular.length}/${slots.length} reguläre Plätze)`);const bySlot=new Map(slots.map((slot,i)=>[slot,regular[i]]));for(const e of explicit)bySlot.set(e.slot,e);
    return template.map(base=>{const e=bySlot.get(base.slot);if(!e||isCraftEntry(e))return null;const id=Number(e.itemId)||0;if(!id)return null;return{slot:base.slot,itemId:id,name:updateItemName(e,base),sourceType:'Raid',source:'KeystoneLoot Raid-BiS · Hintergrund',wowheadUrl:`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`}}).filter(Boolean)
  }
  function keystoneViewPageUrl(profile,view='mythicplus'){
    const cfg=profile?.bisUpdate;if(!cfg)return'';let url=String(cfg.url||'');if(!url&&cfg.classSlug&&cfg.specSlug)url=`https://keystoneloot.io/de/classes/${cfg.classSlug}/${cfg.specSlug}/mythic-plus`;if(view==='raid')return url.replace(/\/mythic-plus(?:\?.*)?$/,'/raid');if(view==='overall')return url.replace(/\/mythic-plus(?:\?.*)?$/,'');return url
  }
  function clearTierFields(item){item.catalyst=false;item.acceptItemIds=[];item.acceptNames=[];delete item.tierItemId;delete item.tierName;delete item.tierNameEn;delete item.tierWowheadUrl;delete item.directTierTarget;delete item.catalystBaseItemId;delete item.catalystBaseName;delete item.catalystBaseNameEn;delete item.catalystBaseWowheadUrl;delete item.catalystBaseSourceType;delete item.catalystBaseSource;delete item.catalystBaseUpdatedAt}
  const TIER_SLOT_ORDER=['head','shoulder','chest','hands','legs'];
  function genericTierName(profile,slot){const labels={head:'Sethelm',shoulder:'Setschultern',chest:'Setbrust',hands:'Sethandschuhe',legs:'Setbeine'};return `Midnight-S2-${labels[slot]||'Setteil'} · ${profile.class}`}
  function repairTierSetIntegrity(profile){
    if(!profile?.gear)return;
    ensureCatalystBaseMetadata(profile);
    // Bei Final-only-Profilen ist itemId das raidfreie Basisitem; tierItemId bleibt das eigentliche Klassensetteil.
    // Konkrete Dungeonquellen dürfen bei der Integritätsreparatur niemals wieder durch einen generischen Katalysator-Text ersetzt werden.
    if(profile?.bisUpdate?.finalOnly){
      for(const item of profile.gear.filter(x=>TIER_SLOT_ORDER.includes(x.slot))){
        if(!item.setSlot)continue;
        const tierId=Number(item.tierItemId)||Number(item.itemId);
        const tierName=item.tierName||genericTierName(profile,item.slot);
        item.catalyst=true;item.directTierTarget=true;item.tierItemId=tierId;item.tierName=tierName;item.tierNameEn=item.tierNameEn||tierName;item.tierWowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${tierId}`;item.acceptItemIds=Array.from(new Set([tierId,...(item.acceptItemIds||[]).map(Number)].filter(Boolean)));item.acceptNames=Array.from(new Set([tierName,...(item.acceptNames||[])].filter(Boolean)));item.invalidForRaidfree=false;item.sourceType='Mythic+';
        if(!item.source)item.source='Matrixkatalysator aus Mythic+-Basisitem / Große Schatzkammer (M+)';
      }
    }
    // Curated profiles carry explicit tierItemId metadata. Never let source repair/update clear it.
    for(const item of profile.gear){
      if(item.tierItemId){item.catalyst=true;item.setSlot=TIER_SLOT_ORDER.includes(item.slot);item.acceptItemIds=Array.from(new Set([Number(item.tierItemId),...(item.acceptItemIds||[]).map(Number)].filter(Boolean)));item.acceptNames=Array.from(new Set([item.tierName,...(item.acceptNames||[])].filter(Boolean)));}
    }
    ensureCatalystBaseMetadata(profile);
  }
  function tierSetStatus(){const candidates=activeProfile.gear.filter(x=>x.catalyst&&x.tierItemId);let equipped=0;for(const item of candidates){const eq=getEquipped(item.slot);if(eq&&(isTier(item,eq)||(item.directTierTarget&&isBase(item,eq))))equipped++}return{equipped,needed:4,candidates:candidates.length,active:equipped>=4}}

  function isDefensiveProtectedSlot(profile,item){
    if(profile?.bisUpdate?.mode!=='defensive-curated')return false;
    // Die Tankprofile wurden bewusst auf Überleben/Selbstheilung kuratiert. Allgemeine Online-Listen
    // dürfen deshalb weder defensive Trinkets, hergestellte Defensivteile noch mögliche Setplätze überschreiben.
    return ['trinket1','trinket2',...BIS_CATALYST_ORDER].includes(item.slot)||item.sourceType==='Hergestellt';
  }
  function applyOnlineBis(profile,payload,persist=true){
    if(profile?.bisUpdate?.lockTargetBuild){repairTierSetIntegrity(profile);payload.updateSummary={changed:0,protectedCount:profile.gear.length,lockedTargetBuild:true};if(persist)try{localStorage.removeItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key)}catch{}return;}
    const entries=(payload.items||[]).filter(x=>x&&Number(x.itemId));
    ensureCatalystBaseMetadata(profile);
    if(profile?.bisUpdate?.finalOnly){
      let regular=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3);
      const catalystEntries=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===5);
      const catalystSlots=BIS_CATALYST_ORDER.filter(slot=>profile.gear.some(x=>x.slot===slot));
      const catalystBySlot=new Map(catalystSlots.map((slot,i)=>[slot,catalystEntries[i]]).filter(x=>x[1]));
      const slots=profile.gear.map(x=>x.slot);
      if(profile.bisUpdate?.duplicateWeaponForOffhand&&slots.includes('offhand')&&regular.length===slots.length-1&&regular.length){regular=[...regular,{...regular[regular.length-1]}]}
      if(regular.length<slots.length)throw new Error(`Online-Liste unvollständig (${regular.length}/${slots.length} Zielplätze)`);
      let changed=0,protectedCount=0;
      for(let i=0;i<slots.length;i++){
        const item=profile.gear[i],online=regular[i];if(!online)continue;
        if(profile?.bisUpdate?.preserveCraftedSlots&&item.sourceType==='Hergestellt'){protectedCount++;continue;}
        const oldId=Number(item.itemId)||0,wasSet=Boolean(item.setSlot||item.catalyst),savedTierId=Number(item.tierItemId)||(wasSet&&item.directTierTarget?oldId:0),savedTierName=item.tierName;
        item.itemId=Number(online.itemId);item.name=updateItemName(online,item);item.nameEn=item.name;item.wowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.itemId}`;
        const crafted=isCraftEntry(online),acq=knownAcquisition(online,item,crafted);item.sourceType=acq.sourceType;item.source=acq.source;item.invalidForRaidfree=Boolean(acq.invalidForRaidfree);item.setSlot=TIER_SLOT_ORDER.includes(item.slot)&&wasSet;if(item.setSlot){item.catalyst=true;item.directTierTarget=true;item.tierItemId=savedTierId||Number(item.tierItemId)||Number(item.itemId);item.tierName=savedTierName||item.tierName||genericTierName(profile,item.slot);item.tierNameEn=item.tierName;item.tierWowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.tierItemId}`;item.acceptItemIds=[item.tierItemId];item.acceptNames=[item.tierName];item.invalidForRaidfree=false;item.sourceType='Mythic+';const catalystBase=catalystBySlot.get(item.slot);if(catalystBase)setCatalystBaseFromEntry(item,catalystBase)}else{clearTierFields(item)};
        if(oldId!==item.itemId){item.note='Online über die aktuelle raidfreie Mythic+-Liste aktualisiert. Persönliche Crafting-Alternativen bleiben aktiv.';if(!crafted)item.embellishment='—';changed++}
      }
      repairTierSetIntegrity(profile);const craftLimitChanges=enforceCraftedTargetLimit(profile);changed+=craftLimitChanges;root.meta.dataDate=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);payload.updateSummary={changed,protectedCount,defensiveCurated:false,curatedCrafts:Boolean(profile?.bisUpdate?.preserveCraftedSlots),finalOnly:true,craftLimit:craftedTargetCap(profile),craftLimitChanges};
      if(persist)try{localStorage.setItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key,JSON.stringify({patch:root.meta.patch,gear:profile.gear,fetchedAt:payload.fetchedAt||new Date().toISOString(),sourceUrl:payload.sourceUrl||profile.bisUpdate?.url||''}))}catch{}
      return;
    }
    const explicit=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&x.slot&&BIS_IMPORT_ORDER.includes(x.slot));
    const explicitSlots=new Set(explicit.map(x=>x.slot));
    const regular=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===3&&!x.slot), catalyst=entries.filter(x=>Number(x.tier)===5);
    const slots=BIS_IMPORT_ORDER.filter(slot=>profile.gear.some(x=>x.slot===slot)&&!explicitSlots.has(slot));
    if(regular.length<slots.length)throw new Error(`Online-Liste unvollständig (${regular.length}/${slots.length} reguläre Plätze)`);
    const regularBySlot=new Map(slots.map((slot,i)=>[slot,regular[i]]));
    for(const entry of explicit)regularBySlot.set(entry.slot,entry);
    const catalystSlots=BIS_CATALYST_ORDER.filter(slot=>profile.gear.some(x=>x.slot===slot));
    const catalystBySlot=new Map(catalystSlots.map((slot,i)=>[slot,catalyst[i]]).filter(x=>x[1]));
    let changed=0,protectedCount=0;
    for(const item of profile.gear){
      const online=regularBySlot.get(item.slot);if(!online)continue;
      if(isDefensiveProtectedSlot(profile,item)){protectedCount++;continue;}
      const base=catalystBySlot.get(item.slot);
      const oldId=Number(item.itemId)||0;
      if(base){
        item.itemId=Number(base.itemId);item.name=updateItemName(base,item);item.nameEn=item.name;item.wowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.itemId}`;
        const acq=knownAcquisition(base,item,false);item.sourceType=acq.sourceType;item.source=acq.source;item.invalidForRaidfree=Boolean(acq.invalidForRaidfree);item.catalyst=true;setCatalystBaseFromEntry(item,base);
        item.acceptItemIds=[Number(online.itemId)];item.acceptNames=[updateItemName(online,item)];item.tierItemId=Number(online.itemId);item.tierName=updateItemName(online,item);item.tierNameEn=item.tierName;item.tierWowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.tierItemId}`;
      }else{
        item.itemId=Number(online.itemId);item.name=updateItemName(online,item);item.nameEn=item.name;item.wowheadUrl=`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${item.itemId}`;clearTierFields(item);
        const crafted=isCraftEntry(online),acq=knownAcquisition(online,item,crafted);item.sourceType=acq.sourceType;item.source=acq.source;item.invalidForRaidfree=Boolean(acq.invalidForRaidfree);
        if(oldId!==item.itemId){item.note='Online über die aktuelle raidfreie KeystoneLoot-Liste aktualisiert.';if(!crafted)item.embellishment='—'}
      }
      if(oldId!==Number(item.itemId))changed++;
    }
    repairTierSetIntegrity(profile);const craftLimitChanges=enforceCraftedTargetLimit(profile);changed+=craftLimitChanges;root.meta.dataDate=new Date().toISOString().slice(0,10);
    payload.updateSummary={changed,protectedCount,defensiveCurated:profile?.bisUpdate?.mode==='defensive-curated',craftLimit:craftedTargetCap(profile),craftLimitChanges};
    if(persist)try{localStorage.setItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key,JSON.stringify({patch:root.meta.patch,gear:profile.gear,fetchedAt:payload.fetchedAt||new Date().toISOString(),sourceUrl:payload.sourceUrl||profile.bisUpdate?.url||''}))}catch{}
  }
  function restoreOnlineBis(){for(const profile of root.profiles){try{if(profile?.bisUpdate?.lockTargetBuild){localStorage.removeItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key)}else{const saved=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key)||'null');if(saved?.patch===root.meta.patch&&Array.isArray(saved.gear)&&saved.gear.length===profile.gear.length)profile.gear=saved.gear}}catch{}repairTierSetIntegrity(profile);repairAcquisitionSources(profile);repairTierSetIntegrity(profile);const limited=enforceCraftedTargetLimit(profile);if(limited)try{localStorage.setItem(BIS_STORE+profile.key,JSON.stringify({patch:root.meta.patch,gear:profile.gear,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString(),sourceUrl:profile?.bisUpdate?.url||'',craftLimitApplied:true}))}catch{}}}
  function namesFromKeystoneText(text){const names={};const patterns=[/\[([^\]]+)\]\((?:https?:\/\/)?(?:www\.)?wowhead\.com\/[^)]*?(?:item=|item\/)(\d+)[^)]*\)/gi,/<a[^>]+href=["'][^"']*(?:item=|\/item\/)(\d+)[^"']*["'][^>]*>([\s\S]*?)<\/a>/gi];for(const re of patterns){let m;while((m=re.exec(text))){const id=Number(re===patterns[0]?m[2]:m[1]);const raw=re===patterns[0]?m[1]:m[2];const name=String(raw).replace(/<[^>]+>/g,'').replace(/&amp;/g,'&').trim();if(id&&name)names[id]=name}}return names}
  async function decodeKeystoneText(text,specId){const m=String(text).match(/KeystoneLoot:v3,([A-Za-z0-9+/=_-]+)/);if(!m)throw new Error('KeystoneLoot-Importdaten nicht gefunden');if(typeof DecompressionStream==='undefined')throw new Error('Dieser Browser unterstützt die Dekomprimierung nicht');let b64=m[1].replace(/-/g,'+').replace(/_/g,'/');b64+='='.repeat((4-b64.length%4)%4);const bytes=Uint8Array.from(atob(b64),c=>c.charCodeAt(0));const stream=new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('deflate'));const decoded=await new Response(stream).text();const obj=JSON.parse(decoded);let items=obj[String(specId)]||Object.values(obj)[0];if(!Array.isArray(items))throw new Error('Keine BiS-Daten im Import gefunden');items=items.map(x=>({...x}));if(String(specId)==='66'&&Array.isArray(obj['70'])&&obj['70'][0])items.push({...obj['70'][0],slot:'trinket1'});const names=namesFromKeystoneText(text);return items.map(x=>({...x,name:names[Number(x.itemId)]||''}))}
  function keystoneApiUrl(profile,view='mythicplus'){
    const cfg=profile?.bisUpdate;if(!cfg)return'';const safe=['mythicplus','overall','raid'].includes(view)?view:'mythicplus';
    if(cfg.classSlug&&cfg.specSlug)return `https://keystoneloot.io/api/import/${encodeURIComponent(cfg.classSlug)}/${encodeURIComponent(cfg.specSlug)}?view=${safe}`;
    const m=String(cfg.url||'').match(/\/classes\/([^/]+)\/([^/]+)/i);if(!m)return'';
    return `https://keystoneloot.io/api/import/${encodeURIComponent(m[1])}/${encodeURIComponent(m[2])}?view=${safe}`;
  }
  async function fetchKeystoneNames(profile){
    try{
      const reader='https://r.jina.ai/'+profile.bisUpdate.url;
      const r=await fetch(reader,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)return{};
      return namesFromKeystoneText(await r.text());
    }catch{return{}}
  }
  function syncKeystoneLinks(profile=activeProfile){
    const url=profile?.bisUpdate?.url||profile?.sources?.find(x=>normal(x?.label||'').includes('keystoneloot'))?.url||'https://keystoneloot.io/de';
    for(const id of ['keystoneDirectLink','openBisSource']){const el=$(id);if(el){el.href=url;el.setAttribute('aria-label',`KeystoneLoot für ${profile?.label||'das aktive Profil'} öffnen`)}}
  }
  function showBisImportHelper(profile,diagnostics=[]){
    const box=$('bisImportHelper');if(!box)return;
    box.classList.remove('hidden');
    const hint=location.protocol==='file:'
      ?'Lokale HTML-Version: 1) KeystoneLoot-Quelle öffnen 2) dort „Import-String kopieren“ 3) hier „Aus Zwischenablage lesen“ 4) „Import anwenden“. Die lokale Datei versucht keine Fremd-API mehr und zeigt daher keine 404-Diagnose.'
      :'Der direkte Live-Abruf wurde blockiert. Du kannst den offiziellen KeystoneLoot-Importstring trotzdem sicher übernehmen.';
    $('bisImportHelpText').textContent=hint+(diagnostics.length?` Letzte Diagnose: ${diagnostics.slice(-2).join(' · ')}`:'');
    syncKeystoneLinks(profile);
  }
  function hideBisImportHelper(){ $('bisImportHelper')?.classList.add('hidden') }
  async function readBisClipboard(){
    try{
      if(!navigator.clipboard?.readText)throw new Error('Zwischenablage-Lesen wird von diesem Browser nicht freigegeben');
      const text=await navigator.clipboard.readText();
      if(!text.includes('KeystoneLoot:v3,'))throw new Error('In der Zwischenablage wurde kein KeystoneLoot-v3-Importstring gefunden');
      $('bisImportText').value=text.trim();
      $('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message ok';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent='✓ KeystoneLoot-Importstring aus der Zwischenablage übernommen. Klicke jetzt auf „Import anwenden“.';
    }catch(e){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message warning';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=`Zwischenablage konnte nicht automatisch gelesen werden: ${e.message}. Füge den Importstring bitte direkt in das Feld ein.`}
  }
  async function applyManualBisImport(){
    if(activeProfile?.bisUpdate?.lockTargetBuild){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message ok';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent='Die individuelle Hero-Talent-Zielwertliste ist geschützt und wird nicht durch einen allgemeinen Import ersetzt.';return;}
    const text=$('bisImportText')?.value.trim()||'';if(!text){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message warning';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent='Bitte zuerst einen KeystoneLoot-v3-Importstring einfügen.';return}
    try{
      const items=await decodeKeystoneText(text,activeProfile.bisUpdate.specId);
      const payload={ok:true,items,source:'KeystoneLoot Importstring',sourceUrl:activeProfile.bisUpdate.url,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString()};
