      applyOnlineBis(activeProfile,payload,true);rebuildPersonalCraftOverrides();comparisonCache=new Map();updateProfile();if(liveCharacter)renderCharacter();
      $('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message ok';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=`✓ ${activeProfile.label}: KeystoneLoot-Import erfolgreich angewendet. Die besten Katalysator-Basisitems wurden ebenfalls übernommen; persönliche hergestellte Alternativen wurden erneut berücksichtigt.`;
      $('lastRefresh').textContent='BiS-Liste aktualisiert: '+new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE',{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'}).format(new Date());hideBisImportHelper();
    }catch(e){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message error';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=`Import konnte nicht angewendet werden: ${e.message}`}
  }

  async function fetchOnlineBis(profile){
    const cfg=profile.bisUpdate;if(!cfg)throw new Error('Für dieses Profil ist keine Online-Quelle hinterlegt');
    const diagnostics=[];
    const local=location.protocol==='file:'?'http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/bis-refresh':`${webApiBase()}/api/bis-refresh`;
    try{
      const u=new URL(local,location.href);u.searchParams.set('profile',profile.key);
      const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});let d={};try{d=await r.json()}catch{}
      if(r.ok&&d.ok&&Array.isArray(d.items))return d;
      diagnostics.push(d?.detail||d?.error||`Lokaler Server HTTP ${r.status}`);
    }catch(e){diagnostics.push('Lokaler Server: '+e.message)}

    // Für die Einzel-HTML-Datei zuerst die gerenderte Mythic+-Seite verwenden.
    // Sie enthält den offiziellen KeystoneLoot:v3-Importstring direkt.
    const pageTargets=[
      'https://r.jina.ai/'+cfg.url,
      'https://r.jina.ai/'+cfg.url.replace('https://','http://'),
      cfg.url
    ];
    for(const target of pageTargets){
      try{
        const r=await fetch(target,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
        const raw=await r.text();const items=await decodeKeystoneText(raw,cfg.specId);const names=namesFromKeystoneText(raw);for(const item of items)if(!item.name&&names[Number(item.itemId)])item.name=names[Number(item.itemId)];
        return{ok:true,items,sourceUrl:cfg.url,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString(),source:target.startsWith('https://r.jina.ai/')?'KeystoneLoot Mythic+ · Reader':'KeystoneLoot Mythic+ · Seite'};
      }catch(e){diagnostics.push(`Mythic+-Seite: ${e.message}`)}
    }

    const api=keystoneApiUrl(profile);
    if(api){
      for(const candidate of [api,api+'&format=plain']){
        try{
          const r=await fetch(candidate,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);
          const raw=await r.text();let importString=raw,updated='';
          try{const d=JSON.parse(raw);if(d.error)throw new Error(d.error);if(d.string)importString=d.string;updated=d.updated||''}catch(e){if(raw.trim().startsWith('{'))throw e}
          const items=await decodeKeystoneText(importString,cfg.specId);const names=await fetchKeystoneNames(profile);for(const item of items)if(!item.name&&names[Number(item.itemId)])item.name=names[Number(item.itemId)];
          return{ok:true,items,sourceUrl:cfg.url,apiUrl:candidate,fetchedAt:updated||new Date().toISOString(),source:'KeystoneLoot API'};
        }catch(e){diagnostics.push(`KeystoneLoot API${candidate.includes('format=plain')?' (plain)':''}: ${e.message}`)}
      }
    }
    return{ok:false,manualRequired:true,diagnostics};
  }

  async function fetchBackgroundRaidPayload(profile){
    const cfg=profile?.bisUpdate;if(!cfg)throw new Error('Keine KeystoneLoot-Konfiguration');const diagnostics=[];
    const local=location.protocol==='file:'?'http://127.0.0.1:8765/api/bis-refresh':'/api/bis-refresh';
    try{const u=new URL(local,location.href);u.searchParams.set('profile',profile.key);u.searchParams.set('view','raid');const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store'});let d={};try{d=await r.json()}catch{}if(r.ok&&d.ok&&Array.isArray(d.items))return d;diagnostics.push(d?.detail||d?.error||`Lokaler Server HTTP ${r.status}`)}catch(e){diagnostics.push('Lokaler Server: '+e.message)}
    const page=keystoneViewPageUrl(profile,'raid');for(const target of ['https://r.jina.ai/'+page,page]){try{const r=await fetch(target,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);const raw=await r.text();const items=await decodeKeystoneText(raw,cfg.specId);const names=namesFromKeystoneText(raw);for(const item of items)if(!item.name&&names[Number(item.itemId)])item.name=names[Number(item.itemId)];return{ok:true,items,source:'KeystoneLoot Raid · Hintergrund',sourceUrl:page,fetchedAt:new Date().toISOString()}}catch(e){diagnostics.push(`Raid-Seite: ${e.message}`)}}
    const api=keystoneApiUrl(profile,'raid');for(const candidate of [api,api+'&format=plain']){try{const r=await fetch(candidate,{cache:'no-store'});if(!r.ok)throw new Error(`HTTP ${r.status}`);const raw=await r.text();let importString=raw,updated='';try{const d=JSON.parse(raw);if(d.error)throw new Error(d.error);if(d.string)importString=d.string;updated=d.updated||''}catch(e){if(raw.trim().startsWith('{'))throw e}const items=await decodeKeystoneText(importString,cfg.specId);return{ok:true,items,source:'KeystoneLoot Raid API · Hintergrund',sourceUrl:page,fetchedAt:updated||new Date().toISOString()}}catch(e){diagnostics.push(`Raid-API: ${e.message}`)}}
    throw new Error(diagnostics.slice(-3).join(' · ')||'Raid-BiS konnte nicht geladen werden')
  }
  async function ensureBackgroundRaidBis(profile=activeProfile,force=false){
    if(!profile?.bisUpdate)return backgroundRaidTargets(profile);const current=loadStoredBackgroundRaidBis(profile);const meta=backgroundRaidMeta.get(profile.key)||{};const t=Date.parse(meta.fetchedAt||'');if(!force&&current.length&&Number.isFinite(t)&&Date.now()-t<BACKGROUND_RAID_MAX_AGE)return current;
    if(backgroundRaidFetches.has(profile.key))return backgroundRaidFetches.get(profile.key);
    const task=(async()=>{try{const payload=await fetchBackgroundRaidPayload(profile);const items=raidBackgroundFromPayload(profile,payload);if(items.length)saveBackgroundRaidBis(profile,items,payload);return backgroundRaidTargets(profile)}catch(e){console.warn('Raid-BiS-Hintergrund konnte nicht aktualisiert werden:',profile.key,e);return current}finally{backgroundRaidFetches.delete(profile.key)}})();backgroundRaidFetches.set(profile.key,task);return task
  }
  async function updateBisItems(){
    const btn=$('updateBisBtn');if(!btn)return;const old=btn.textContent;if(activeProfile?.bisUpdate?.lockTargetBuild){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message ok';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent='Die individuelle Hero-Talent-Zielwertliste ist geschützt. KeystoneLoot bleibt als Referenzquelle hinterlegt, überschreibt diese kuratierte Liste aber nicht.';return;}
    // Auch aus einer lokal geöffneten HTML-Datei wird zuerst ein automatischer Abruf versucht.
    // Falls CORS oder Browser-Sicherheitsregeln das verhindern, fällt die Funktion anschließend
    // auf den sichtbaren Import-Assistenten mit echten KeystoneLoot-Links zurück.
    btn.disabled=true;btn.textContent='↻ BiS wird geprüft …';hideBisImportHelper();$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent='Aktuelle raidfreie Mythic+- und Herstellungs-Liste wird geprüft …';
    try{
      const payload=await fetchOnlineBis(activeProfile);
      if(payload?.manualRequired){
        showBisImportHelper(activeProfile,payload.diagnostics||[]);
        $('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message warning';
        $('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=location.protocol==='file:'
          ?'Der automatische Live-Abruf wurde vom Browser blockiert. Die vorhandene BiS-Liste bleibt unverändert. Öffne KeystoneLoot über den sichtbaren Direktlink und übernimm den offiziellen Import-String – oder starte die mitgelieferte Server-Version für den automatischen Abruf.'
          :'KeystoneLoot konnte gerade nicht automatisch live gelesen werden. Die vorhandene Liste bleibt unverändert; der Live-Import-Assistent ist eingeblendet.';
        return;
      }
      applyOnlineBis(activeProfile,payload,true);rebuildPersonalCraftOverrides();comparisonCache=new Map();updateProfile();if(liveCharacter)renderCharacter();
      const s=payload.updateSummary||{};const protection=s.defensiveCurated&&s.protectedCount?` ${s.protectedCount} bewusst defensive Tank-Slots (Set-/Trinket-/Craft-Plätze) wurden nicht durch die allgemeine Liste überschrieben.`:(s.curatedCrafts&&s.protectedCount?` ${s.protectedCount} kuratierte Deathbringer-Craft-Slots wurden bewusst nicht durch reine Dungeondrops überschrieben.`:'');
      const dateText=new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE',{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'}).format(new Date(payload.fetchedAt||Date.now()));
      $('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message ok';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=`✓ ${activeProfile.label}: BiS-Items und Katalysator-Basisitems live geprüft und aktualisiert. Quelle: ${payload.source||'KeystoneLoot'} · ${dateText}.${protection} Persönliche hergestellte Alternativen wurden anschließend erneut berücksichtigt.`;
      $('lastRefresh').textContent='BiS-Liste aktualisiert: '+new Intl.DateTimeFormat('de-DE',{dateStyle:'medium',timeStyle:'short'}).format(new Date());
    }catch(e){$('bisUpdateStatus').className='api-message warning';$('bisUpdateStatus').textContent=`BiS-Update nicht möglich: ${e.message}. Die zuletzt gespeicherte Liste bleibt unverändert.`}
    finally{btn.disabled=false;btn.textContent=old}
  }

  const MUSIC_FAV='wow-bis-music-favorites-v2';
  const OLD_AMAZON_FAV='wow-bis-amazon-favorites';

  function parseAmazon(v){
    try{const u=new URL(String(v||'').trim());return u.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./,'').startsWith('music.amazon.')?u:null}catch{return null}
  }
  function parseYoutubeMusic(v){
    const raw=String(v||'').trim(); if(!raw)return null;
    let u; try{u=new URL(raw.startsWith('http://')||raw.startsWith('https://')?raw:`https://${raw}`)}catch{return null}
    const host=u.hostname.toLowerCase().replace(/^www\./,'');
    if(!['youtube.com','m.youtube.com','youtu.be','music.youtube.com'].includes(host))return null;
    let video='', list=u.searchParams.get('list')||'';
    if(host==='youtu.be')video=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean)[0]||'';
    else{
      const parts=u.pathname.split('/').filter(Boolean);
      if(['shorts','live','embed'].includes(parts[0]))video=parts[1]||'';
      else video=u.searchParams.get('v')||'';
    }
    if(video&&!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{11}$/.test(video))video='';
    if(list&&!/^[A-Za-z0-9_-]+$/.test(list))list='';
    if(!video&&!list&&host!=='music.youtube.com')return null;
    let target;
    if(video){target=new URL('https://music.youtube.com/watch');target.searchParams.set('v',video);if(list)target.searchParams.set('list',list)}
    else if(list){target=new URL('https://music.youtube.com/playlist');target.searchParams.set('list',list)}
    else target=new URL(u.toString());
    return target;
  }
  function popupFeatures(width=480,height=640){
    const left=Math.max(0,Math.round((screen.availWidth-width)/2));
    const top=Math.max(0,Math.round((screen.availHeight-height)/2));
    return `popup=yes,width=${width},height=${height},left=${left},top=${top},resizable=yes,scrollbars=yes`;
  }
  function openSmallMusicWindow(url,name,width,height,statusId,label){
    const win=window.open(url,name,popupFeatures(width,height));
    const status=$(statusId);
    if(!win){
      status.className='api-message warning';
      status.textContent='Das kleine Playerfenster wurde vom Browser blockiert. Bitte Pop-ups für dieses Dashboard erlauben und erneut klicken.';
      return false;
    }
    try{win.focus()}catch{}
    status.className='api-message ok';
    status.textContent=`${label} wurde im kleinen Playerfenster geöffnet.`;
    return true;
  }
  function openAmazonMusicPopup(){
    const raw=$('amazonMusicInput').value.trim(),u=parseAmazon(raw);
    if(!u){$('amazonMusicStatus').className='api-message warning';$('amazonMusicStatus').textContent='Bitte einen gültigen Amazon-Music-Link eingeben.';return false}
    return openSmallMusicWindow(u.toString(),'wowAmazonMusicPlayer',520,680,'amazonMusicStatus','Amazon Music');
  }
  function openYoutubeMusicPopup(){
    const raw=$('youtubeMusicInput').value.trim(),target=parseYoutubeMusic(raw);
    if(!target){$('youtubeMusicStatus').className='api-message warning';$('youtubeMusicStatus').textContent='Bitte einen gültigen YouTube- oder YouTube-Music-Link eingeben.';return false}
    return openSmallMusicWindow(target.toString(),'wowYoutubeMusicPlayer',460,620,'youtubeMusicStatus','YouTube Music');
  }
  function getMusicFavs(){
    try{
      const current=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(MUSIC_FAV)||'null');
      if(Array.isArray(current))return current;
      const old=JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(OLD_AMAZON_FAV)||'[]');
      if(Array.isArray(old)&&old.length){const migrated=old.map(x=>({...x,provider:'amazon'}));localStorage.setItem(MUSIC_FAV,JSON.stringify(migrated));return migrated}
    }catch{}
    return [];
  }
  function saveMusicFavs(a){try{localStorage.setItem(MUSIC_FAV,JSON.stringify(a.slice(0,60)))}catch{}}
  function renderMusicFavs(){
    const a=getMusicFavs(),sel=$('musicFavorites'); if(!sel)return;
    sel.innerHTML='<option value="">Favorit auswählen …</option>'+a.map((x,i)=>`<option value="${i}">${x.provider==='youtube'?'YouTube Music':'Amazon Music'} · ${escapeHtml(x.label||x.url)}</option>`).join('');
  }
  function setMusicTab(provider){
    const amazon=provider!=='youtube';
    $('musicPaneAmazon').classList.toggle('hidden',!amazon);$('musicPaneYoutube').classList.toggle('hidden',amazon);
    $('musicTabAmazon').classList.toggle('active',amazon);$('musicTabYoutube').classList.toggle('active',!amazon);
    $('musicTabAmazon').setAttribute('aria-selected',amazon?'true':'false');$('musicTabYoutube').setAttribute('aria-selected',amazon?'false':'true');
  }

  function saveProviderMusic(provider){
    const isY=provider==='youtube', raw=$(isY?'youtubeMusicInput':'amazonMusicInput').value.trim();
    const valid=isY?parseYoutubeMusic(raw):parseAmazon(raw); if(!valid)return;
    const label=$(isY?'youtubeMusicLabel':'amazonMusicLabel').value.trim()||(isY?'YouTube Music':'Amazon Music');
    const a=getMusicFavs().filter(x=>!(x.url===raw&&x.provider===provider));a.unshift({url:raw,label,provider});saveMusicFavs(a);renderMusicFavs();
  }
  function playMusicFav(){
    const i=Number($('musicFavorites').value),a=getMusicFavs();if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||!a[i])return;const x=a[i];
    if(x.provider==='youtube'){setMusicTab('youtube');$('youtubeMusicInput').value=x.url;$('youtubeMusicLabel').value=x.label||'';openYoutubeMusicPopup()}
    else{setMusicTab('amazon');$('amazonMusicInput').value=x.url;$('amazonMusicLabel').value=x.label||'';openAmazonMusicPopup()}
  }
  function deleteMusicFav(){const i=Number($('musicFavorites').value),a=getMusicFavs();if(!Number.isInteger(i)||i<0||!a[i])return;a.splice(i,1);saveMusicFavs(a);renderMusicFavs()}

(function(){
  const q=id=>document.getElementById(id);
  const tabClick=key=>document.querySelector(`#questTabs .quest-tab[data-tab-target="${key}"]`)?.click();
  const shortLabels={crit:'Kritisch',haste:'Tempo',mastery:'Meisterschaft',versatility:'Vielseitigkeit',leech:'Lebensraub',avoidance:'Vermeidung',speed:'Geschwindigkeit'};
  const statGlyph={crit:'⚔',haste:'✹',mastery:'✦',versatility:'◆',leech:'♻',avoidance:'◈',speed:'➤'};
  const leftSlots=['head','neck','shoulder','back','chest','wrist','hands'];
  const rightSlots=['waist','legs','feet','finger1','finger2','trinket1','trinket2'];
  const weaponSlots=['mainhand','offhand'];
  const slotShort={head:'Kopf',neck:'Hals',shoulder:'Sch.',back:'Rücken',chest:'Brust',wrist:'Arm',hands:'Hand',waist:'Gürtel',legs:'Beine',feet:'Füße',finger1:'Ring',finger2:'Ring',trinket1:'Schm.',trinket2:'Schm.',mainhand:'Waffe',offhand:'Neben'};
  function num(v){const n=Number(v);return Number.isFinite(n)?n:null}
  function f(v,d=1){const n=num(v);return n==null?'—':n.toLocaleString('de-DE',{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d})}
  let wcCharacterObjectUrl='';
  let wcCharacterLoadToken=0;
  function renderCandidatesFromThumb(src){
    if(!src)return[];
    const clean=String(src).trim();
    const list=[
      clean.replace(/-avatar\.(jpg|jpeg|png)(\?.*)?$/i,'-main-raw.png$2'),
      clean.replace(/-inset\.(jpg|jpeg|png)(\?.*)?$/i,'-main-raw.png$2'),
      clean.replace(/-avatar\.(jpg|jpeg|png)(\?.*)?$/i,'-main.jpg$2'),
      clean.replace('/avatar/','/main-raw/'),
      clean
    ];
    return [...new Set(list.filter(Boolean))];
  }
  async function officialCharacterMediaCandidates(){
    if(!liveCharacter)return[];
    try{
      const d=await helperJson('/api/character-media',{region:q('region')?.value||liveCharacter?.region,realm:q('realm')?.value||liveCharacter?.realm,name:q('character')?.value||liveCharacter?.name});
      const priority={'main-raw':0,main:1,inset:2,avatar:3};
      return (Array.isArray(d.assets)?d.assets:[])
        .filter(x=>x&&x.value)
        .sort((a,b)=>(priority[String(a.key||'').toLowerCase()]??9)-(priority[String(b.key||'').toLowerCase()]??9))
        .map(x=>String(x.value));
    }catch(e){console.warn('Offizieller Blizzard-Charakterrender nicht verfügbar',e);return[]}
  }
  async function proxiedRenderBlob(url){
    try{
      const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(false);
      const u=new URL(base+'/api/render-image');u.searchParams.set('url',url);u.searchParams.set('_ts',String(Date.now()));
      const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store',mode:u.origin===location.origin?'same-origin':'cors',credentials:'omit'});
      if(!r.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status);
      return await r.blob();
    }catch(e){
      const r=await fetch(url,{cache:'no-store',mode:'cors',credentials:'omit'});
      if(!r.ok)throw new Error('HTTP '+r.status);
      return await r.blob();
    }
  }
  async function cropTransparentCharacter(blob){
    const bmp=await createImageBitmap(blob),w=bmp.width,h=bmp.height;
    const canvas=document.createElement('canvas');canvas.width=w;canvas.height=h;
    const ctx=canvas.getContext('2d',{willReadFrequently:true});ctx.drawImage(bmp,0,0);bmp.close?.();
    let data;try{data=ctx.getImageData(0,0,w,h).data}catch{return blob}
    const step=Math.max(1,Math.floor(Math.max(w,h)/1050));
    let threshold=24,visible=0;
    for(const t of [60,42,28,16]){let count=0;for(let y=0;y<h;y+=step)for(let x=0;x<w;x+=step)if(data[(y*w+x)*4+3]>t)count++;if(count>40){threshold=t;visible=count;break}}
    if(!visible)return blob;
    const cols=new Array(Math.ceil(w/step)).fill(0),rows=new Array(Math.ceil(h/step)).fill(0);
    for(let y=0,ry=0;y<h;y+=step,ry++)for(let x=0,rx=0;x<w;x+=step,rx++)if(data[(y*w+x)*4+3]>threshold){cols[rx]++;rows[ry]++}
    const colCut=Math.max(2,Math.floor((h/step)*.006)),rowCut=Math.max(2,Math.floor((w/step)*.006));
    let lx=cols.findIndex(v=>v>=colCut),rx=cols.length-1-[...cols].reverse().findIndex(v=>v>=colCut),ty=rows.findIndex(v=>v>=rowCut),by=rows.length-1-[...rows].reverse().findIndex(v=>v>=rowCut);
    if(lx<0||ty<0||rx<lx||by<ty)return blob;
    let minX=Math.max(0,lx*step-step*3),maxX=Math.min(w-1,(rx+1)*step+step*3),minY=Math.max(0,ty*step-step*3),maxY=Math.min(h-1,(by+1)*step+step*2);
    const bw=maxX-minX+1,bh=maxY-minY+1;if(bw>w*.985&&bh>h*.985)return blob;
    const padX=Math.round(bw*.025),padTop=Math.round(bh*.018),padBottom=Math.round(bh*.008);
    const sx=Math.max(0,minX-padX),sy=Math.max(0,minY-padTop),sw=Math.min(w-sx,bw+padX*2),sh=Math.min(h-sy,bh+padTop+padBottom);
    const out=document.createElement('canvas');out.width=sw;out.height=sh;out.getContext('2d').drawImage(canvas,sx,sy,sw,sh,0,0,sw,sh);
    return await new Promise(resolve=>out.toBlob(b=>resolve(b||blob),'image/png'));
  }
  async function loadCharacterRender(thumb){
    const token=++wcCharacterLoadToken,portrait=q('wcCharacterRender'),fallback=q('wcCharacterFallback');
    if(!portrait)return;
    portrait.style.visibility='hidden';fallback.style.display='grid';
    const official=await officialCharacterMediaCandidates();
    if(token!==wcCharacterLoadToken)return;
    const candidates=[...new Set([...official,...renderCandidatesFromThumb(thumb)])];
    for(const candidate of candidates){
      try{
        const raw=await proxiedRenderBlob(candidate);if(token!==wcCharacterLoadToken)return;
        const cropped=await cropTransparentCharacter(raw);if(token!==wcCharacterLoadToken)return;
        if(wcCharacterObjectUrl)URL.revokeObjectURL(wcCharacterObjectUrl);
        wcCharacterObjectUrl=URL.createObjectURL(cropped);
        await new Promise((resolve,reject)=>{portrait.onload=resolve;portrait.onerror=reject;portrait.src=wcCharacterObjectUrl});
        if(token!==wcCharacterLoadToken)return;
        portrait.style.visibility='visible';fallback.style.display='none';portrait.dataset.source=candidate;return;
      }catch(e){console.warn('Charakterrender nicht nutzbar',candidate,e)}
    }
    portrait.style.visibility='hidden';fallback.style.display='grid';
  }

  }
  function syncProfileSelect(){
    const src=q('profileSelect'),dst=q('wcProfileSelect');if(!src||!dst)return;
    const signature=JSON.stringify(Array.from(src.options).map(o=>[o.value,o.textContent,o.disabled]));
    if(dst.dataset.signature!==signature){
      dst.innerHTML=src.innerHTML;
      dst.disabled=src.disabled;
      dst.dataset.signature=signature;
    }else{
      dst.disabled=src.disabled;
    }
    dst.value=src.value;
  }

  function numberList(value){
    const out=[];
    const walk=v=>{
      if(v==null)return;
      if(Array.isArray(v)){v.forEach(walk);return}
      if(typeof v==='number'||/^\d+$/.test(String(v))){const n=Number(v);if(n>0)out.push(n);return}
      if(typeof v==='object'){
        for(const key of ['item_id','itemId','id','bonus_id','bonusId','enchantment_id','enchant_id'])if(v[key]!=null){walk(v[key]);return}
      }
    };
    walk(value);return [...new Set(out)];
  }
  function wowheadEquippedData(eq){
    const id=eqId(eq);if(!id)return null;
    const ilvl=Number(eqIlvl(eq))||0;
    const bonus=numberList(eq?.bonuses??eq?.bonus_list??eq?.bonus_ids??eq?.item?.bonuses??eq?.item?.bonus_list);
    const gems=numberList(eq?.gems??eq?.sockets??eq?.socketed_gems??eq?.gem_items);
    const enchants=numberList(eq?.enchantments??eq?.enchants??eq?.enchant??eq?.enchant_id??eq?.enchantment_id);
    const args=[];
    if(ilvl)args.push(`ilvl=${ilvl}`);
    if(bonus.length)args.push(`bonus=${bonus.join(':')}`);
    if(gems.length)args.push(`gems=${gems.join(':')}`);
    if(enchants.length)args.push(`ench=${enchants[0]}`);
    return{id,url:`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${id}`,data:args.join('&')};
  }
  function slotHtml(slot){
    let eq=null,st=null;try{eq=getEquipped(slot);st=comparisonCache?.get(slot)?.status?.key}catch{}
    const icon=eq?rioIcon(eq):'';const name=eq?eqName(eq):(slotShort[slot]||slot);const ilvl=eq?eqIlvl(eq):'';
    const cls=st==='raidbis'?'raidbis':(st==='match'||st==='personal'?'bis':'');
    if(!eq)return `<div class="wc-gear-slot ${cls}" aria-label="${escapeHtml(String(name))}"><span class="placeholder">${escapeHtml(slotShort[slot]||slot)}</span></div>`;
    const wh=wowheadEquippedData(eq);
    const statusText=st==='raidbis'?'Raid-BiS angelegt':(st==='match'||st==='personal'?'BiS angelegt':'Angelegter Gegenstand');
    return `<div class="wc-gear-slot ${cls}"><a href="${escapeHtml(wh?.url||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${eqId(eq)}`)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener" data-wc-slot="${escapeHtml(slot)}" aria-label="${escapeHtml(String(name))} – ${escapeHtml(statusText)}"><span class="wc-tooltip-hint">i</span>${icon?`<img src="${escapeHtml(icon)}" alt="${escapeHtml(String(name))}">`:`<span class="placeholder">${escapeHtml(slotShort[slot]||slot)}</span>`}${ilvl&&ilvl!=='—'?`<em>${escapeHtml(String(ilvl))}</em>`:''}</a></div>`;
  }
  const slotLong={head:'Kopf',neck:'Hals',shoulder:'Schultern',back:'Rücken',chest:'Brust',wrist:'Handgelenke',hands:'Hände',waist:'Taille',legs:'Beine',feet:'Füße',finger1:'Ring 1',finger2:'Ring 2',trinket1:'Schmuck 1',trinket2:'Schmuck 2',mainhand:'Waffe',offhand:'Nebenhand'};
  function firstReadableText(v){
    if(v==null)return'';
    if(typeof v==='string')return v.trim();
    if(typeof v==='number')return'';
    if(Array.isArray(v)){for(const x of v){const t=firstReadableText(x);if(t)return t}return''}
    if(typeof v==='object'){
      for(const k of ['display_string','displayString','name','description','spell_name','text']){const t=firstReadableText(v[k]);if(t)return t}
      for(const k of ['spell','item','enchantment','gem']){const t=firstReadableText(v[k]);if(t)return t}
    }
    return'';
  }
  function collectionSummary(v,fallbackLabel){
    if(v==null)return'';
    const arr=Array.isArray(v)?v:[v];
    const readable=[];
    for(const x of arr){const t=firstReadableText(x);if(t&&!readable.includes(t))readable.push(t)}
    if(readable.length)return readable.slice(0,3).join(' · ')+(readable.length>3?' …':'');
    const ids=numberList(v);return ids.length?`${ids.length} ${fallbackLabel} erkannt`:'';
  }
  function qualityClass(eq){
    const raw=eq?.qualityType??eq?.qualityName??eq?.item_quality??eq?.quality??eq?.item?.quality??'';
    const q=String(typeof raw==='object'?(raw.type||raw.name||raw.value||''):raw).toLowerCase();
    const n=Number(raw);
    if(n===5||q.includes('legend'))return'quality-legendary';
    if(n===4||q.includes('epic')||q.includes('episch'))return'quality-epic';
    if(n===3||q.includes('rare')||q.includes('selten'))return'quality-rare';
    if(n===2||q.includes('uncommon')||q.includes('ungewöhn'))return'quality-uncommon';
    return'quality-common';
  }
  function tooltipElement(){
    let el=document.getElementById('wcItemTooltip');
    if(!el){el=document.createElement('div');el.id='wcItemTooltip';el.setAttribute('role','tooltip');document.body.appendChild(el)}
    return el;
  }
  const blizzardSlotTypeMap={HEAD:'head',NECK:'neck',SHOULDER:'shoulder',BACK:'back',CHEST:'chest',WRIST:'wrist',HANDS:'hands',WAIST:'waist',LEGS:'legs',FEET:'feet',FINGER_1:'finger1',FINGER_2:'finger2',TRINKET_1:'trinket1',TRINKET_2:'trinket2',MAIN_HAND:'mainhand',OFF_HAND:'offhand'};
  let wcEquipmentDetails=[];
  let wcEquipmentIdentity='';
  let wcEquipmentPromise=null;
  const wcTooltipDetailCache=new Map();
  function currentEquipmentIdentity(){return [normal(q('region')?.value||liveCharacter?.region||''),normal(q('realm')?.value||liveCharacter?.realm||''),normal(q('character')?.value||liveCharacter?.name||'')].join(':')}
  function findEquipmentDetail(list,slot){
    const a=Array.isArray(list)?list:Object.values(list||{});
    return a.find(x=>{const type=String(x?.slotType||x?.slot?.type||'').toUpperCase();return(blizzardSlotTypeMap[type]||String(x?.dashboardSlot||''))===slot})||null;
  }
  function blizzardEquipmentForSlot(slot){return wcTooltipDetailCache.get(currentEquipmentIdentity()+':'+slot)||findEquipmentDetail(wcEquipmentDetails,slot)||findEquipmentDetail(characterStats?.equipmentDetails,slot)}
  async function helperJson(path,params={}){
    const base=await resolveLocalHelperBase(false),u=new URL(base+path);
    Object.entries(params).forEach(([k,v])=>{if(v!=null&&String(v)!=='')u.searchParams.set(k,String(v))});u.searchParams.set('_ts',String(Date.now()));
    const r=await fetch(u,{cache:'no-store',mode:u.origin===location.origin?'same-origin':'cors',credentials:'omit'});let d={};try{d=await r.json()}catch{}if(!r.ok||d.ok===false)throw new Error(d.detail||d.error||('HTTP '+r.status));return d;
  }
  async function ensureEquipmentDetails(force=false){
    if(!liveCharacter)return[];
    const identity=currentEquipmentIdentity();if(!force&&identity===wcEquipmentIdentity&&wcEquipmentDetails.length)return wcEquipmentDetails;if(wcEquipmentPromise&&!force)return wcEquipmentPromise;
    wcEquipmentPromise=(async()=>{try{const d=await helperJson('/api/equipment-details',{region:q('region')?.value||liveCharacter?.region,realm:q('realm')?.value||liveCharacter?.realm,name:q('character')?.value||liveCharacter?.name});wcEquipmentDetails=Array.isArray(d.equipmentDetails)?d.equipmentDetails:[];wcEquipmentIdentity=identity;return wcEquipmentDetails}catch(e){console.warn('Equipment-Details konnten nicht vorgeladen werden',e);return[]}finally{wcEquipmentPromise=null}})();return wcEquipmentPromise;
  }
  function tooltipDetailIsRich(d){return Boolean(d&&(Array.isArray(d.stats)&&d.stats.length||Array.isArray(d.enchantments)&&d.enchantments.length||Array.isArray(d.sockets)&&d.sockets.length||Array.isArray(d.effects)&&d.effects.length||d.armorDisplay||d.binding||d.subclassName))}
  function tooltipDetailIsComplete(d){return Boolean(d?.fullTooltip)}
  async function ensureRichTooltipDetail(slot){
    const key=currentEquipmentIdentity()+':'+slot,cached=wcTooltipDetailCache.get(key);if(cached&&tooltipDetailIsComplete(cached))return cached;
    const eq=getEquipped(slot),itemId=eqId(eq);const d=await helperJson('/api/item-tooltip',{region:q('region')?.value||liveCharacter?.region,realm:q('realm')?.value||liveCharacter?.realm,name:q('character')?.value||liveCharacter?.name,slot,itemId});
    if(d.detail){wcTooltipDetailCache.set(key,d.detail);return d.detail}return cached||null;
  }
  function activeItemSource(slot,id){
    const n=Number(id)||0;
    const raid=(activeProfile?.backgroundRaidBis||[]).find(x=>Number(x?.itemId)===n&&(x?.slot===slot||['finger1','finger2'].includes(slot)&&['finger1','finger2'].includes(x?.slot)||['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(slot)&&['trinket1','trinket2'].includes(x?.slot)));
    if(raid?.source)return{label:raid.source,type:'Raid'};
    const target=(activeProfile?.gear||[]).find(x=>Number(x?.itemId)===n||Number(x?.tierItemId)===n||Array.isArray(x?.acceptItemIds)&&x.acceptItemIds.map(Number).includes(n));
    if(target?.source)return{label:target.source,type:target.sourceType||''};
    const known=typeof ITEM_SOURCE_BY_ID!=='undefined'?ITEM_SOURCE_BY_ID.get(n):null;
    if(known?.source)return{label:known.source,type:known.sourceType||''};
    return null;
  }
  function tooltipStats(blz,eq){
    const stats=Array.isArray(blz?.stats)?blz.stats:[];
    if(stats.length)return stats.map(x=>({name:x.name||x.type||'Wert',value:x.value,display:x.display||''})).filter(x=>x.display||x.name);
    const raw=Array.isArray(eq?.stats)?eq.stats:[];
    return raw.map(x=>({name:firstReadableText(x?.type)||firstReadableText(x)||'Wert',value:x?.value,display:firstReadableText(x?.display)||''})).filter(x=>x.display||x.value!=null);
  }
  function detailLines(items,kind){
    if(!Array.isArray(items)||!items.length)return'';
    return items.map(x=>{
      const text=x?.display||x?.display_string||x?.text||x?.name||'';
      const id=x?.itemId||x?.id||'';
      return text?`<div class="${kind==='Verzauberung'?'wc-tip-enchant':'wc-tip-gem'}">${escapeHtml(String(text))}${id?` <span class="wc-tip-id">#${escapeHtml(String(id))}</span>`:''}</div>`:'';
    }).filter(Boolean).join('');
  }
  function buildLocalItemTooltip(slot,override=null,loading=false){
    const eq=getEquipped(slot);if(!eq)return'';
    let st=null;try{st=comparisonCache?.get(slot)?.status?.key}catch{}
    const blz=override||blizzardEquipmentForSlot(slot);
    const name=blz?.name||eqName(eq),ilvl=blz?.itemLevel??eqIlvl(eq),id=Number(blz?.itemId||eqId(eq))||0;
    const statusText=st==='raidbis'?'BiS angelegt · Raid-BiS':(st==='match'?'BiS angelegt':(st==='personal'?'BiS angelegt · persönlich':'Angelegter Gegenstand'));
    const statusClass=st==='raidbis'?'raidbis':(st==='match'||st==='personal'?'bis':'');
    const qName=blz?.qualityName||'',inv=blz?.inventoryType||blz?.slotName||slotLong[slot]||slotShort[slot]||slot;
    const ench=Array.isArray(blz?.enchantments)?blz.enchantments:[],gems=Array.isArray(blz?.sockets)?blz.sockets:[];
    const enchantFallback=collectionSummary(eq?.enchantments??eq?.enchants??eq?.enchant??eq?.enchant_id??eq?.enchantment_id,'Verzauberung(en)');
    const gemFallback=collectionSummary(eq?.gems??eq?.sockets??eq?.socketed_gems??eq?.gem_items,'Edelstein(e)');
    const bonus=[...new Set([...numberList(blz?.bonusIds),...numberList(eq?.bonuses??eq?.bonus_list??eq?.bonus_ids??eq?.item?.bonuses??eq?.item?.bonus_list)])];
    const stats=tooltipStats(blz,eq),source=activeItemSource(slot,id),armor=blz?.armorDisplay||'',durability=blz?.durabilityDisplay||'',req=blz?.requiredLevel?`Benötigt Stufe ${blz.requiredLevel}`:'';
    const effects=Array.isArray(blz?.effects)?blz.effects:[],setEffects=Array.isArray(blz?.setEffects)?blz.setEffects:[],weaponLines=Array.isArray(blz?.weaponLines)?blz.weaponLines:[];
    const statsHtml=stats.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Werte</div><div class="wc-tip-stats">${stats.map(x=>`<div class="wc-tip-stat"><span>${escapeHtml(String(x.display||x.name))}</span>${x.display?'':`<span>${escapeHtml(String(x.value??''))}</span>`}</div>`).join('')}</div></div>`:'';
    const weaponHtml=weaponLines.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Waffe</div>${weaponLines.map(x=>`<div class="wc-tip-weapon">${escapeHtml(String(x))}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
    const descriptionHtml=blz?.description?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-description">${escapeHtml(String(blz.description))}</div>`:'';
    const transmogHtml=blz?.transmog?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-transmog"><strong>Transmog:</strong> ${escapeHtml(String(blz.transmog))}</div>`:'';
    const contextHtml=blz?.context?`<div class="wc-tip-context">${escapeHtml(String(blz.context))}</div>`:'';
    const enchantHtml=ench.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Verzauberung</div>${detailLines(ench,'Verzauberung')}</div>`:(enchantFallback?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Verzauberung</div><div class="wc-tip-enchant">${escapeHtml(enchantFallback)}</div></div>`:'');
    const gemHtml=gems.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Sockel / Edelsteine</div>${detailLines(gems,'Edelstein')}</div>`:(gemFallback?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Sockel / Edelsteine</div><div class="wc-tip-gem">${escapeHtml(gemFallback)}</div></div>`:'');
    const effectsHtml=effects.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Effekte</div>${effects.map(x=>`<div class="wc-tip-effect">${escapeHtml(String(x.display||x.description||x.name||''))}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
    const setHtml=(blz?.setName||setEffects.length)?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><div class="wc-tip-section-title">Set${blz?.setName?': '+escapeHtml(String(blz.setName)):''}</div>${setEffects.map(x=>`<div class="wc-tip-set">${escapeHtml(String(x.display||x.description||x.name||''))}</div>`).join('')}</div>`:'';
    return `<div class="wc-tip-name ${qualityClass(blz||eq)}">${escapeHtml(String(name))}<span class="wc-tip-id">#${escapeHtml(String(id||'—'))}</span></div>`+
      (qName?`<div class="wc-tip-quality">${escapeHtml(String(qName))}</div>`:'')+
      `<div class="wc-tip-ilvl">Gegenstandsstufe ${escapeHtml(String(ilvl||'—'))}</div>`+
      `<div class="wc-tip-slot">${escapeHtml(String(inv))}${blz?.subclassName?` · ${escapeHtml(String(blz.subclassName))}`:''}</div>`+
      (blz?.binding?`<div class="wc-tip-binding">${escapeHtml(String(blz.binding))}</div>`:'')+
      (blz?.uniqueEquipped?`<div class="wc-tip-binding">${escapeHtml(String(blz.uniqueEquipped))}</div>`:'')+contextHtml+
      `<div class="wc-tip-status ${statusClass}">${escapeHtml(statusText)}</div>`+
      (source?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-source"><strong>Quelle:</strong><br>${escapeHtml(String(source.label))}</div>`:'')+
      (armor?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-detail"><strong>Rüstung:</strong> ${escapeHtml(String(armor))}</div>`:'')+
      weaponHtml+statsHtml+enchantHtml+gemHtml+effectsHtml+setHtml+descriptionHtml+transmogHtml+
      ((durability||req)?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-detail">${durability?escapeHtml(String(durability)):''}${durability&&req?'<br>':''}${req?escapeHtml(req):''}</div>`:'')+
      (bonus.length?`<div class="wc-tip-line"><strong>Bonus-IDs:</strong> ${escapeHtml(bonus.join(', '))}</div>`:'')+
      (loading?`<div class="wc-tip-loading">Vollständige Blizzard-Daten werden nachgeladen …</div>`:'')+
      (!loading&&!tooltipDetailIsRich(blz)?`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-empty">Die Quelle liefert für diesen Gegenstand keine zusätzlichen Werte.</div>`:'')+
      (blz?.enrichmentWarning?`<div class="wc-tip-warning">Item-Erweiterung teilweise fehlgeschlagen: ${escapeHtml(String(blz.enrichmentWarning))}</div>`:'')+
      `<div class="wc-tip-api">${blz?.fullTooltip?'Blizzard Character Equipment + Item API':(blz?'Blizzard Character Equipment':'Raider.IO-Fallback')}</div>`+
      `<div class="wc-tip-muted">Klick öffnet den Gegenstand auf Wowhead.</div>`;
  }
  function positionLocalItemTooltip(el,x,y,anchor){
    if(!el)return;const gap=16,rect=anchor?.getBoundingClientRect?.();let px=Number.isFinite(x)?x+gap:(rect?rect.right+gap:gap),py=Number.isFinite(y)?y+gap:(rect?rect.top:gap);
    el.style.left='0px';el.style.top='0px';el.classList.add('visible');const w=el.offsetWidth||380,h=el.offsetHeight||240;
    if(px+w>window.innerWidth-10)px=Math.max(10,(Number.isFinite(x)?x:rect?.left||window.innerWidth)-w-gap);if(py+h>window.innerHeight-10)py=Math.max(10,window.innerHeight-h-10);
    el.style.left=Math.max(10,px)+'px';el.style.top=Math.max(10,py)+'px';
  }
  let activeTooltipSlot='',activeTooltipAnchor=null,activeTooltipPointer={x:null,y:null},activeTooltipRequest=0;
  function showLocalItemTooltip(anchor,event){
    const slot=anchor?.dataset?.wcSlot;if(!slot)return;activeTooltipSlot=slot;activeTooltipAnchor=anchor;activeTooltipPointer={x:event?.clientX,y:event?.clientY};const existing=blizzardEquipmentForSlot(slot),needs=!tooltipDetailIsComplete(existing),el=tooltipElement();el.innerHTML=buildLocalItemTooltip(slot,existing,needs);positionLocalItemTooltip(el,event?.clientX,event?.clientY,anchor);
    if(needs){const serial=++activeTooltipRequest;ensureRichTooltipDetail(slot).then(detail=>{if(serial!==activeTooltipRequest||activeTooltipSlot!==slot||!detail)return;el.innerHTML=buildLocalItemTooltip(slot,detail,false);positionLocalItemTooltip(el,activeTooltipPointer.x,activeTooltipPointer.y,activeTooltipAnchor)}).catch(e=>{if(serial!==activeTooltipRequest||activeTooltipSlot!==slot)return;el.innerHTML=buildLocalItemTooltip(slot,existing,false)+`<div class="wc-tip-line wc-tip-empty">Detailabruf fehlgeschlagen: ${escapeHtml(String(e?.message||e))}</div>`;positionLocalItemTooltip(el,activeTooltipPointer.x,activeTooltipPointer.y,activeTooltipAnchor)})}
  }
  function hideLocalItemTooltip(){activeTooltipRequest++;activeTooltipSlot='';activeTooltipAnchor=null;const el=document.getElementById('wcItemTooltip');if(el)el.classList.remove('visible')}
  function initLocalItemTooltips(){
    document.addEventListener('pointerover',e=>{const a=e.target.closest?.('.wc-gear-slot>a[data-wc-slot]');if(a)showLocalItemTooltip(a,e)});
    document.addEventListener('pointermove',e=>{
      const a=e.target.closest?.('.wc-gear-slot>a[data-wc-slot]');
      const el=document.getElementById('wcItemTooltip');
      if(!a){if(el?.classList.contains('visible'))hideLocalItemTooltip();return}
      if(el?.classList.contains('visible')){activeTooltipPointer={x:e.clientX,y:e.clientY};activeTooltipAnchor=a;positionLocalItemTooltip(el,e.clientX,e.clientY,a)}
    },{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('pointerout',e=>{const a=e.target.closest?.('.wc-gear-slot>a[data-wc-slot]');if(a&&!a.contains(e.relatedTarget))hideLocalItemTooltip()});
    document.addEventListener('pointercancel',hideLocalItemTooltip,{passive:true});
    document.addEventListener('focusin',e=>{const a=e.target.closest?.('.wc-gear-slot>a[data-wc-slot]');if(a)showLocalItemTooltip(a)});
    document.addEventListener('focusout',e=>{if(e.target.closest?.('.wc-gear-slot>a[data-wc-slot]'))hideLocalItemTooltip()});
    document.documentElement.addEventListener('mouseleave',hideLocalItemTooltip);
    window.addEventListener('blur',hideLocalItemTooltip);
    document.addEventListener('visibilitychange',()=>{if(document.hidden)hideLocalItemTooltip()});
    window.addEventListener('scroll',hideLocalItemTooltip,{passive:true});
  }
  function updateGearSlots(){
    if(q('wcGearLeft'))q('wcGearLeft').innerHTML=leftSlots.map(slotHtml).join('');
    if(q('wcGearRight'))q('wcGearRight').innerHTML=rightSlots.map(slotHtml).join('');
    if(q('wcGearWeapons'))q('wcGearWeapons').innerHTML=weaponSlots.map(slotHtml).join('');
    setTimeout(refreshWowhead,30);
  }
  function targetFor(k){try{return statTargetValue(k)}catch{return null}}
  function updateStats(){
    const host=q('wcStatsRows');if(!host)return;
    const keys=['crit','haste','mastery','versatility','leech','avoidance','speed'];
    host.innerHTML=keys.map(k=>{
      const value=num(characterStats?.[k]),target=num(targetFor(k));
      const pct=value!=null&&target!=null&&target>0?Math.max(0,Math.min(100,value/target*100)):0;
      const diff=value!=null&&target!=null?value-target:null;
      const good=diff!=null&&diff>=-.05;
      const state=good?'good':'';
      const diffText=diff==null?'—':`${diff>=0?'+':''}${f(diff)} %`;
      return `<div class="wc-stat-row ${state}"><div class="wc-stat-name"><span class="wc-stat-icon">${statGlyph[k]}</span><span>${shortLabels[k]}</span></div><div class="wc-stat-current">${value==null?'—':f(value)+' %'}</div><div class="wc-stat-track"><i style="width:${pct.toFixed(1)}%"></i></div><div class="wc-stat-target">${target==null?'—':f(target)+' %'}</div><div class="wc-stat-diff ${good?'good':''}">${diffText}</div><div class="wc-stat-pct">${target==null||value==null?'—':Math.round(value/target*100)+' %'}</div></div>`;
    }).join('');
  }
  const wcBisIconCache=new Map();
  async function wcLoadBisIcon(el){
    const itemId=Number(el?.dataset?.itemId||0);if(!itemId||el.dataset.loaded==='1')return;el.dataset.loaded='1';
    try{
      let url=wcBisIconCache.get(itemId);if(url===undefined){const d=await helperJson('/api/item-media',{region:q('region')?.value||liveCharacter?.region||'eu',itemId});url=d.iconUrl||'';wcBisIconCache.set(itemId,url)}
      if(url){const img=document.createElement('img');img.alt='';img.src=url;img.addEventListener('error',()=>img.remove());el.replaceChildren(img)}
    }catch{el.dataset.loaded='0'}
  }
  function wcHydrateBisIcons(){document.querySelectorAll('.wc-bis-icon[data-item-id]').forEach(el=>wcLoadBisIcon(el))}
  function updateBis(){
    let gear=[];try{gear=visibleTargetGear()}catch{gear=activeProfile?.gear||[]}
    const total=gear.length||0;let fulfilled=0,raid=0;
    try{for(const item of gear){const key=comparisonCache?.get(item.slot)?.status?.key;if(isFulfilledBisStatus(key))fulfilled++;if(key==='raidbis')raid++;}}catch{}
    const open=Math.max(0,total-fulfilled),normalCount=Math.max(0,fulfilled-raid),pct=total?Math.round(fulfilled/total*100):0;
    q('wcBisPercent').textContent=pct+'%';q('wcBisFraction').textContent=`${fulfilled} / ${total}`;q('wcBisNormal').textContent=normalCount;q('wcBisRaid').textContent=raid;q('wcBisOpen').textContent=open;
    q('wcBisDonut').style.background=`conic-gradient(#48dd31 0deg ${pct*3.6}deg,#30302d ${pct*3.6}deg 360deg)`;
    const preferred=['head','shoulder','chest','legs','mainhand','offhand'];
    const rows=[...gear].sort((a,b)=>{const ai=preferred.indexOf(a.slot),bi=preferred.indexOf(b.slot);return(ai<0?99:ai)-(bi<0?99:bi)}).filter(x=>preferred.includes(x.slot)).slice(0,6);
    q('wcBisRows').innerHTML=rows.map(item=>{
      let c={};try{c=comparisonCache?.get(item.slot)||{}}catch{} const key=c.status?.key||'unknown';const good=isFulfilledBisStatus(key);
      const source=typeof displaySourceForItem==='function'?displaySourceForItem(item):(item.source||'—');
      const name=item.catalyst&&item.tierName?item.tierName:item.name;
      const itemId=Number((item.catalyst&&item.tierItemId)||item.itemId||0),url=(item.catalyst&&item.tierWowheadUrl)||item.wowheadUrl||`https://www.wowhead.com/de/item=${itemId}`;
      const crafted=normal(item.sourceType||'').includes('hergestellt')||normal(item.sourceType||'').includes('crafted');
      let label='Noch offen',statusClass='';
      if(key==='raidbis'){label='Angelegt · Raid-BiS';statusClass='good raid'}
      else if(good){label='Angelegt';statusClass='good'}
      else if(key==='catalyst'){label='Geplant';statusClass='planned'}
      else if(key==='unknown'){label='Nicht geprüft';statusClass='unknown'}
      const fallback=(item.slotLabel||item.slot||'?').trim().slice(0,1).toUpperCase();
      return `<tr class="${good?'bis':''}"><td><span class="wc-bis-icon" data-item-id="${itemId}"><span class="fallback">${escapeHtml(fallback)}</span></span></td><td>${escapeHtml(item.slotLabel||item.slot)}</td><td class="wc-bis-item ${crafted?'crafted':''}"><a class="wc-bis-item-link" href="${escapeHtml(url)}" target="_blank" rel="noopener">${escapeHtml(name||'—')}</a></td><td class="wc-bis-source">${escapeHtml(source||'—')}</td><td><span class="wc-bis-status-pill ${statusClass}"><i class="wc-status-dot"></i>${escapeHtml(label)}</span></td></tr>`;
    }).join('')||'<tr><td colspan="5">Noch keine BiS-Daten.</td></tr>';
    wcHydrateBisIcons();
  }
  function updateMplus(){
    let runs=[];try{runs=allKnownDungeonRuns()}catch{};
    const timed=runs.filter(r=>{try{return runIsTimed(r)}catch{return false}});
    const weekStart=new Date();weekStart.setHours(0,0,0,0);weekStart.setDate(weekStart.getDate()-((weekStart.getDay()+6)%7));
    const chooseBest=list=>[...list].sort((a,b)=>Number(b.mythic_level||0)-Number(a.mythic_level||0)||(Number(b.score||0)-Number(a.score||0))||((Number(a.clear_time_ms||Infinity))-(Number(b.clear_time_ms||Infinity))))[0]||null;
    const weekly=chooseBest(timed.filter(r=>{const d=new Date(r.completed_at||0);return!Number.isNaN(d.getTime())&&d>=weekStart})),season=chooseBest(timed);
    const runText=r=>{if(!r)return'—';const duration=typeof formatRunDuration==='function'?formatRunDuration(r.clear_time_ms):'';return`+${Number(r.mythic_level||0)}${duration&&duration!=='—'?` · ${duration}`:''}`};
    const high=Number(season?.mythic_level||0);q('wcTopKey').textContent=high?`+${high}`:'+—';
    q('wcWeekBest').textContent=runText(weekly);q('wcSeasonBest').textContent=runText(season);
    let score='—';try{const s=currentScore();score=(s??'—')}catch{} q('wcMplusScore').textContent=score;
    q('wcRunCount').textContent=`${timed.length} / ${runs.length}`;
  }
  function updateCockpit(){
    const tasks=[updateHeader,updateCharacter,updateGearSlots,updateStats,updateBis,updateMplus];
    for(const task of tasks){
      try{task()}catch(e){console.warn('Cockpit update · '+(task.name||'unbekannt'),e)}
    }
  }
  function init(){
    initLocalItemTooltips();
    document.querySelectorAll('[data-open-tab]').forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>tabClick(btn.dataset.openTab)));
    q('wcProfileSelect')?.addEventListener('change',e=>{const src=q('profileSelect');if(src){src.value=e.target.value;src.dispatchEvent(new Event('change',{bubbles:true}))}});
    const ids=['characterName','characterMeta','characterIlvl','characterScore','characterMatches','statGrid','gearBody','mplusSuccessfulCount','mplusHighestScore','mplusHighestKey','mplusSeasonTotalRuns','mplusSeasonTimedRuns','mplusSeasonOuttimeRuns','profileSelect'];
    const observer=new MutationObserver(()=>requestAnimationFrame(updateCockpit));
    ids.map(q).filter(Boolean).forEach(el=>observer.observe(el,{childList:true,subtree:true,characterData:true,attributes:true}));
    ['profileSelect','region','realm','character'].map(q).filter(Boolean).forEach(el=>el.addEventListener('change',updateCockpit));
    updateCockpit();setTimeout(updateCockpit,300);setTimeout(updateCockpit,1200);
  }
  /* Das Cockpit steht an dieser Stelle bereits vollständig im DOM. Direkt initialisieren,
     damit es unabhängig von DOMContentLoaded-Reihenfolge oder gespeicherten Tabs sofort befüllt wird. */
  init();
})();

})();

</script><script async="" src="https://wow.zamimg.com/widgets/power.js"></script>
<script>
(function(){
  function initMidnightTabs(){
    const buttons=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('#questTabs .quest-tab'));
    const content=Array.from(document.querySelectorAll('[data-quest-tab]'));
    function activate(key){
      buttons.forEach(btn=>{
        const active=btn.dataset.tabTarget===key;
        btn.classList.toggle('active',active);
        btn.setAttribute('aria-selected',active?'true':'false');
      });
      content.forEach(el=>el.classList.toggle('quest-hidden',el.dataset.questTab!==key));
      try{localStorage.setItem('wow-midnight-active-tab',key)}catch{}
    }
    buttons.forEach(btn=>btn.addEventListener('click',()=>activate(btn.dataset.tabTarget)));
    const start='overview';
    activate(start);
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading')document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',initMidnightTabs);
  else initMidnightTabs();
})();
</script>
<script>
(function(){
  /* Midnight-Art als großflächige Hintergrundquelle übernehmen */
  function applyMidnightBackdrop(){
    const logo=document.querySelector('.hero-logo');
    if(!logo||!logo.src)return;
    document.documentElement.style.setProperty('--midnight-art', 'url("'+logo.src.replace(/"/g,'%22')+'")');
  }
  if(document.readyState==='loading') document.addEventListener('DOMContentLoaded',applyMidnightBackdrop,{once:true});
  else applyMidnightBackdrop();
})();
</script>


</body></html>
